- **Models** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `VenteComptoir` + `LigneVenteComptoir` (XPOS1: a counter sale that, on `valider/`, becomes a real `ventes.Facture` + `Paiement`(s) + a stock movement via `ventes.services`/`stock.services` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â never a direct model import; `uuid_client` dedups an OFFLINE-created sale replayed on reconnect, NTRET1; `montant_arrhes`/`marchandise_remise` + `EN_ATTENTE_SOLDE` statut = deposit-on-order for backordered/custom items, NTRET5), `SessionCaisse` (XPOS4: caisse open/close, backed by the EXISTING `compta.Caisse` via `compta.services` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â no duplicate cash journal; `numero_rapport_z` race-safe one-shot legal Z-report number, NTRET2; `montant_tpe_compte`/`ecart_tpe` card-terminal reconciliation, XPOS18), `ShareLinkTicket` (XPOS3: tokenized public link to the ticket PDF, same pattern as `ventes.ShareLink`, kept local to `apps/pos`), `CommandeRetrait` + `LigneCommandeRetrait` (XPOS15: click-and-collect, ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â _preparerÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢pretÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢retire), `ConfigMaterielPOS` (XPOS18: ESC/POS printer config + TPE card-terminal reconciliation), `CodePinCaissier` (NTRET3: hashed quick-lock PIN per company+user ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â switch cashier on a shared till without a full re-login or losing the in-progress cart).
- Endpoints: `ventes/` (+ `{id}/lignes`, `{id}/valider`, `{id}/promotions`, `{id}/coupon`, `{id}/arrhes|solde-arrhes|remettre-marchandise` (NTRET5), `{id}/ticket-pdf|ticket-arrhes-pdf|ticket-escpos|ticket-share-link`, `encaisser-facture/`, `emettre-carte-cadeau/`, `{id}/payer-carte-cadeau`, `factures-recherche/`, `dashboard/`, `dashboard-export/`, `dashboard-retail/` + `dashboard-retail-export/` (NTRET16 ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â panier moyen/transformation/ventes-mÃƒâ€šÃ‚Â²/top produits, reads `parametres.BoutiquePos`)), `sessions/` (+ `{id}/cloturer`, `{id}/rapport-x` (NTRET2, in-progress read-only), `{id}/rapport-z|rapport-z-pdf` (official, one-shot numbered)), `retraits/` (+ `{id}/lignes`, `{id}/marquer-pret`, `{id}/remettre`), `config-materiel/`; `verifier-pin/`+`definir-pin/` (NTRET3, PIN lock); public `GET /api/django/public/pos/ticket/<token>/` (tokenized ticket PDF, no auth, same pattern as `ventes.ShareLink`). Promotions/coupons/gift cards are `apps.promotions` models consumed here (never duplicated) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â see the `promotions` section below.

### installations ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â chantiers / field execution
Installation projects spun up once a quote is signed, through to commissioning and
closure; work orders, checklists, regulatory (law 82-21) tracking. **ARC3** sweeps
all 61 installations ViewSets onto `CompanyScopedModelViewSet` (behaviour
identical). **ARC28** adds `apps/installations/platform.py`.
- **Installation** (chantier) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `company` FK; `reference`; `client` FK; `devis` FKÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ventes.Devis; `bon_commande` FKÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ventes.BonCommande; `lead` FKÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢crm.Lead; `statut` (SIGNE/MATERIEL_COMMANDE/PLANIFIE/EN_COURS/INSTALLE/RECEPTIONNE/CLOTURE + legacy values); `puissance_installee_kwc`; `type_installation`; `technicien_responsable` FK; `bom` JSON (frozen BoM from devis); `regime_8221` + `dossier_statut` (regulatory); `annule` bool + `motif_annulation`; milestone dates.
- **StockReservation** (N14) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `company` FK; `installation` FK; `produit` FKÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢stock.Produit; `quantite`; `consomme` bool (`unique_together (installation, produit)`). Seeded from the chantier's frozen `bom` at creation; consumed exactly once when the chantier reaches the canonical INSTALLE statut (one `MouvementStock` SORTIE per SKU, idempotent under `select_for_update()`/atomic ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â re-entering INSTALLE emits nothing); cancel/close releases the remaining (un-consumed) reservation. Drives the reservation-aware availability on the stock serializer (rÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©servÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© vs disponible) and low-stock alerts.
- **Intervention** (sortie chantier, F3) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `installation` FK; `ticket` FKÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢sav.Ticket (nullable); `type_intervention` (POSE/RACCORDEMENT/MISE_EN_SERVICE/CONTROLE/DEPANNAGE); `technicien` FK; `equipe` M2MÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢users (default = chantier installer, set server-side); `camionnette` FKÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢stock.EmplacementStock (nullable); `date_prevue/realisee`; **`statut`** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â its OWN ordered state machine (`a_preparer/prete/en_route/sur_site/terminee/validee` + `STATUT_ORDER`, default `a_preparer`) **completely separate from the chantier statut and the STAGES.py contract** (changing it never touches either). RÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©f/client/devis/ville/GPS are read-only, pulled from the chantier.
- **InterventionActivity** (F3) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â per-intervention chatter (same pattern as InstallationActivity), helper `intervention_activity.py` (creation + tracked-field changes incl. statut + manual notes; user/company server-side).
- **ChecklistTemplate** (N74) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `company` FK; `nom`; `type_installation` (nullable; auto-selects the template for a chantier of that market); `ordre`; `actif`; `protege` (the per-company "DÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©faut" fallback that carries today's 7 steps). **ChecklistEtapeModele / ChantierChecklistItem** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â template steps (now FKÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ChecklistTemplate, `unique_together (company, template, cle)`) and per-chantier checklist state; `capture_serie` flags serial-number capture steps (feeds the equipment registry); `fait` bool. Auto-selection (`template_for_installation`, services.py) matches by `type_installation`, falls back to DÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©faut ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â behaviour preserved.
- **TypeIntervention / InstallationActivity** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â configurable intervention types and chantier chatter.
- **JalonProjet / ModeleProjet (+ ModeleProjetJalon, ModeleProjetBomLigne) / ReunionChantier** (FG293/296/298, `models_projet.py`) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â project milestones/phases (ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©tude/appro/pose/MES/rÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©ception with `date_cible`/`date_reelle`/`atteint`), chantier-type templates (`services.instantiate_modele_projet` pre-creates standard jalons + appends BoM-type lines to the frozen `bom`, idempotent + additive), and timestamped site-meeting minutes (ordre du jour/prÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©sents/dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©cisions/actions, author + company server-side). Endpoints `jalons-projet/`, `modeles-projet/` (+ `{id}/instancier/`), `reunions-chantier/`.
- **FG291 ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â Projet (programme multi-chantiers, `models_program.py`)** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a `Projet` regrouping the chantiers + devis + tickets of one client/site (ferme ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â  4 forages, toiture par tranches), with link tables **ProjetChantier/ProjetDevis/ProjetTicket** referencing other apps by **string FK only** (`ventes.Devis`/`sav.Ticket`/`crm.Client` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â resolved via FK metadata + tenant-checked, never imported). Its OWN status machine (`brouillon/actif/en_pause/termine/annule`) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â **independent of STAGES.py and of devis/ticket document statuses** (attachment never touches them). Reference `PRG-` via the race-safe factory; company/created_by forced server-side. Idempotent `attacher_chantier|devis|ticket` actions (get_or_create); ViewSets `programmes/`, `programme-chantiers/`, `programme-devis/`, `programme-tickets/`. NEW architectural component.
- **FG292 ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â ProjetTache** (project tasks/sub-tasks with dependencies, on `models_program.py`): `projet` FKÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢Projet (same-app), `parent` self-FK (sub-tasks), `predecesseur` self-FK (dependency), `assigne` FKÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢user, `date_echeance`, own `Statut` (a_faire/en_cours/termine ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â NOT STAGES.py), `ordre`. `clean()` cycle-guards BOTH `parent` and `predecesseur` (rollback in an atomic block). ViewSet `programme-taches/` (`?projet`/`?statut`/`?parent`/`?assigne` filters, tenant checks on every FK).
- **FG294 ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â BudgetProjet + BudgetEngagement** (budget projet vs rÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©el): `BudgetProjet` (1-to-1 with Projet; HT envelopes matÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©riel/main-d'Ãƒâ€¦Ã¢â‚¬Å“uvre/sous-traitance/divers + `tarif_jour_mo` + `seuil_alerte_pct`) and `BudgetEngagement` (attaches a supplier cost ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â BCF/facture ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â by string-FK). `selectors.budget_projet_synthese` aggregates ACTUALS ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â devis via `apps.get_model('ventes','Devis')`, BCF/FactureFournisseur via function-local `apps.stock.selectors`, labour from same-app Installation ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â vs budget with an over-budget flag (no cross-app model import; import-linter 4/4). ViewSets `programme-budgets/` (+ `synthese`), `programme-engagements/`. INTERNE (responsable/admin ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â exposes purchase costs). **FG295** adds `selectors.projet_pnl` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â consolidated project P&L: REVENUE (client factures on the project's devis, cancelled excluded) ÃƒÂ¢Ã‹â€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ COSTS (matÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©riel/sous-traitance/imports via FG294 engagements + main-d'Ãƒâ€¦Ã¢â‚¬Å“uvre) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ `marge_brute` + `marge_pct`; action `programmes/{id}/pnl/` (responsable/admin, cross-app reads reuse FG294's `get_model`/selector helpers ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â import-linter safe). No model. **FG299** adds `selectors.plan_de_charge_equipes` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â the FIELD-TEAM workload view: per-technicien capacity (working days ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬â€ hours) vs allocated Interventions (principal `technicien` OR `equipe` member, de-duped, windowed) with a `sur_reservation` flag + `charge_pct`; endpoint `interventions/plan-de-charge/?debut=&fin=`. Distinct from gestion_projet's PROJ18 and STAGES.py. No model. **FG300** adds `selectors.conflits_affectation` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â double-booking detection: same technicien (principal/ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©quipe) or same camionnette on ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â°Ã‚Â¥2 interventions the same day (de-duped, material assets excluded), at `interventions/conflits-affectation/?debut=&fin=`. No model. **FG301** adds `selectors.nivellement_charge` (resource levelling) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â proposes moving interventions off overloaded technicians to under-loaded ones **without creating an FG300 same-day conflict** (read-only proposal, mutates nothing), at `interventions/nivellement-charge/`. No model. **FG302** adds **IndisponibiliteRessource** (`models_indispo.py`) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a field-resource unavailability calendar: a `technicien` XOR `camionnette` absent over `[date_debut, date_fin]`, `type_indispo` congÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©/formation/arrÃƒÆ’Ã‚Âªt/autre; ViewSet `indisponibilites-ressource/` + `selectors.ressource_indisponible` that FG299/FG300/FG301 can call to exclude unavailable resources. Migration installations/0019.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/installations/`): `chantiers/` ViewSet + `creer-depuis-devis`, `regime-suggestion`, `{id}/historique|noter|mise-en-service|annuler|reactiver`, `{id}/checklist|cocher-checklist`, `{id}/besoin-materiel|commander-besoin` (now reports a per-SKU `reserve`); `interventions/` (F3: `?statut=`/`?type_intervention=`/`?installation=` filters + `{id}/historique|noter`); `types-intervention/`; `checklist-etapes/` (filterable `?template=`); `checklist-templates/` (N74, named template CRUD, DÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©faut delete-protected). Frontend route `/interventions` (F4, CHANTIERS menu): list + statut kanban (drag-to-change-status, technicien reassign). **FG303** adds `selectors.planning_camionnettes(company, debut, fin)` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a per-van calendar grouping interventions (via `Intervention.camionnette`) with a daily load, zero-capacity on FG302 indisponibilitÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©s (over-reservation visible), at the `planning-camionnettes` read action (IsAnyRole). No model. **FG304/DC34** — **SousTraitant** is a FAÇADE, not a standalone model: a `stock.Fournisseur(type='service')` carrying a `stock.SousTraitantProfile` (métier/ICE/RIB/`actif`), orchestrated by `SousTraitantViewSet` (`sous-traitants/`, métier/actif filters + search) via stock's selectors/services only — never `stock.models` (see `views/soustraitant.py` header; the `sous-traitants/` API contract is unchanged). **FG305** adds **OrdreSousTraitance** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â subcontractor work orders (FKÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢SousTraitant FG304 + same-app chantier, race-safe `OST-` ref, prestation/montant/ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©chÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©ance, cycle brouillonÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©misÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢en_coursÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢rÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©ceptionnÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢clos with lifecycle actions). ViewSet `ordres-sous-traitance/`. Migration installations/0021. **SCA34** converts `OrdreSousTraitance` into the FIRST pilot of the `core.documents.DocumentMetier` kit ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a mixed conversion (redeclares `company` byte-identical, gains `created_at`/`updated_at` additively alongside the historical `date_creation`/`date_modification`, widens `statut` 20ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢32 chars for the abstract field, same 5 choices/default); reference format (`OST-YYYYMM-NNNN`) and the 3 existing lifecycle actions are unchanged (non-regression tested). **SCA36** converts `DemandeAchat` (models_demande_achat.py) into the kit's THIRD pilot (`LigneDocumentMetier` without a totals mixin ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a purchase requisition has no money total). **NTP2P40** adds `demandes-achat/<id>/importer-lignes-csv/` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â bulk-add requisition lines from an uploaded CSV. **NTP2P41** adds `rfq/<id>/comparatif-pdf/` (print-friendly PDF of `selectors.rfq_comparatif`) and `rfq/<id>/export-xlsx/` (one tab per consulted supplier + a comparatif tab, `apps.records.xlsx.build_workbook`) alongside the FG311 RFQ multi-supplier comparatif. **NTMOB21** adds `views_meteo.py`/`weather.py` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `GET installations/meteo/` (site weather for planning). **NTP2P2** adds **RegleApprobationAchat** + **EtapeApprobationAchat** (`models_approbation_achat.py`, migration installations/0101) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a same-app sequential approval plan for `DemandeAchat`, opt-in per company: a rule matches by montant bracket + optional chantier/programme scope (most-specific wins) and can `autorise_depassement_budget` (NTP2P4); no matching rule = the historical direct approve/refuse behaviour, byte-identical. ViewSet `regles-approbation-achat/`; `demandes-achat/{id}/` gains `etapes-approbation/` (GET), `approuver-etape/`, `rejeter-etape/` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â the final step flips the demande to `approuvee`, a rejection to `refusee`. `services.controler_budget_demande_achat` (called at `soumettre`) reaches `stock.selectors/services` (never `stock.models`) to check the NTP2P4 department budget before the workflow starts.
- **CHT2** adds `services.verifier_annulation_cloture` — blocks cancelling a CLÔTURÉ chantier (canonical `CLOTURE` statut OR `cloture_verrouillee` flag) unless the same role+motif conditions as the AUD326 lock are met (cancelling releases stock reservations/interventions exactly like the lock protects). **CHT5** — `consume_reservations` now traces a PARTIAL stock consumption (insufficient stock on hand) with one aggregated chatter note instead of closing the reservation silently. **CHT23** adds `StageModele.photos_min`/`checklist_pct_min` (`etapes-chantier/`) — ADDITIVE, opt-in count gates on stage-passage (real photos attached / % checklist done); defaults (`photos_min=0`, `checklist_pct_min=100`) keep the historical all-or-nothing gate byte-identical. **CHT11** adds `services.synchroniser_jalon_portail` — publishes an ATTEINT `JalonProjet` to the client-visible `portail.JalonChantierPortail` timeline via `portail.services.upsert_jalon_chantier` (never a `portail.models` import), ending jalon double-entry; best-effort, wired on both the manual PATCH and the auto-atteinte (réception) paths. **CHT17** adds `services.sync_indisponibilite_maintenance`, called from `flotte.services.changer_statut_vehicule` on ↔MAINTENANCE transitions, to open/close a marker-tagged `IndisponibiliteRessource` for the camionnette (never touches a manually-entered unavailability). **CHT12** adds `selectors.devis_id_du_chantier`/`devis_ids_des_chantiers` (batch) + an optional `company` scope on `installation_for_devis` — cross-app read entry points (e.g. `gestion_projet`) with zero N+1.

- **P2P scheduled-task gap closure (NTP2P33/45).** Celery task `relancer_rfq_en_attente_task` (NTP2P33) — an RFQ due in ≤2 days with a non-responding fournisseur gets ONE notification/day to the buyer (same anti-duplicate guard as `stock.tasks.relancer_bcf_en_retard_task`). **NTP2P45** closes a real notification gap in `services.approuver_etape_achat`: since `EtapeApprobationAchat` rows are `bulk_create`d (no `post_save` signal fires), only the FIRST approval step was ever notified (VX99); approving a step now immediately notifies the NEXT step's approvers instead of waiting for the next Celery beat cycle.

### outillage ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â durable field tools & kits (F1/F2)
Durable tooling (drills, ladders, metersÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚Â¦), tracked **strictly separate from the consumable stock catalogue** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â never sellable, never consumed, never on a client-facing document.
- **Outillage** (F1) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `company` FK; `nom`; `categorie` (free text); `asset_tag`; `numero_serie`; `emplacement` FKÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢stock.EmplacementStock (nullable; the tool's home location among the existing dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©pÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â´t/camionnette); `statut` (DISPONIBLE/EN_INTERVENTION/EN_REPARATION/PERDU); `date_achat`; `note`. Optional photo via the generic `records.Attachment` (`outillage.outillage` whitelisted in `records.ALLOWED_TARGETS`).
- **KitOutillage / KitOutillageItem** (F2) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â named, reusable tooling kit templates editable in ParamÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¨tres; each an ordered list of catalogue tools (`KitOutillageItem.outil` FKÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢Outillage, `ordre`, `unique_together (kit, outil)`); `type_intervention` (TypeIntervention key) pre-selects a kit; `actif` toggle. Three defaults (pose structure / raccordement / mise en service) seeded on first list (idempotent), fully editable.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/outillage/`): `outils/` (read any role, write responsable/admin; filter `?statut=`/`?emplacement=`, search nom/asset_tag/numero_serie/categorie), `kits/` (seed-on-list, write admin), `kit-items/` (write admin, item company follows its kit). Frontend route `/outillage` (CHANTIERS menu) + ParamÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¨tres ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â« Kits d'outillage Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â» tab.

### sav ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â after-sales: equipment registry, tickets, maintenance contracts
Tracks installed equipment + warranty clocks and the SAV ticket lifecycle.
- **Equipement** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `company` FK; `produit` FKÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢stock.Produit; `installation` FKÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢installations.Installation; `numero_serie`; `date_pose`; `date_fin_garantie(_production)` (computed from `date_pose` + product warranty); `statut` (EN_SERVICE/REMPLACE/HORS_SERVICE); `remplace_par_ticket` FKÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢Ticket.
- **Ticket** (SAV) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `company` FK; `reference`; `client` FK; `installation` FK (nullable); `equipement` FK (nullable); `type` (CORRECTIF/PREVENTIF); `statut` (NOUVEAU/PLANIFIE/EN_COURS/RESOLU/CLOTURE); `priorite`; `sous_garantie` (OUI/NON/A_DETERMINER, computed from equipment warranty if linked); `cout` (internal, never client-facing); `annule` bool + `motif_annulation`.
- **TicketActivity** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â ticket chatter. **ContratMaintenance** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â preventive contracts (`periodicite`, `date_debut`, `derniere_visite`, `actif`, `duree_mois`, `date_renouvellement`).
- **PieceConsommee** (N46) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â parts consumed on a SAV ticket: `company` FK; `ticket` FKÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢Ticket; `produit` FKÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢stock.Produit; `quantite`; `stock_decremente` (guards double stock moves). Shown on the intervention report by designation/marque/quantitÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© only ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â never buy price or margin; recording it can decrement stock via `MouvementStock`.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/sav/`): `equipements/`, `tickets/` (+ `{id}/historique|noter|annuler|reactiver|rapport-pdf`), `contrats-maintenance/`. **NTMOB23** adds `equipements/<id>/partage-qr/` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a QR-shareable equipment record for field techs. **FG280** adds **AlarmeOnduleur** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â inverter alarms/faults DISTINCT from the SAV ticket (code/gravitÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© info-warning-critique/ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©quipement, statut active/acquittee/resolue/escaladee), with `acquitter` (server-side user+date, idempotent) and `escalader` (links or opens a SAV ticket) actions; ViewSet `alarmes-onduleur/`. Migration sav/0011.
- **Groupe NTSRV (relation client SAV).** **TicketEmailThread** (NTSRV1, migration sav/0052) — mémoire de threading e-mail (`message_id` unique par société, `thread_root`) alimentée par le registre `core/email_intake.py` (aucune connexion IMAP dans `apps.sav`). **NTSRV2** ajoute le portail dépôt de ticket tokenisé `portail/tickets/` (AllowAny + throttle). **NTSRV3** ajoute le webhook `webhooks/whatsapp-inbound/` (canal SAV, gated 404 tant que `WHATSAPP_BUSINESS_API_KEY` n'est pas configurée). **NTSRV5** ajoute `tickets/{id}/log-appel/`. **NTSRV6** ajoute `CategorieTicket.competences_requises` (M2M string-FK `rh.Competence`) + `niveau_competence_min` pour filtrer l'affectation par compétence (vide = comportement actuel inchangé ; lecture des niveaux employés via `apps.rh.selectors`, jamais `apps.rh.models`). **NTSRV8** ajoute `EquipeMaintenance.capacite_max_tickets_ouverts` (vide = aucune limite) et `file-attente/` (lecture pure, proposition de débordement par équipe). **NTSRV11** ajoute `horaires_ouvres`/`sla_heures_ouvrees_actif` (fenêtre ouvrée du calcul SLA, OFF par défaut). **NTSRV12** ajoute **EscaladeSlaNiveau** (paliers d'escalade SLA ordonnés, remplace le binaire XSAV6 ; vide = comportement inchangé). **NTSRV20** ajoute les compteurs KCS `KbArticle.nb_vues_depuis_ticket`/`nb_resolutions_attribuees` (voir §kb).
- **ARC4** sweeps all 15 sav ViewSets onto `CompanyScopedModelViewSet`. **ARC37** makes `sav` an emitter on the `core.events` bus ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `services.emettre_changement_statut_ticket` fires `ticket_resolu`/`equipement_remplace` at the guarded transition site (never a second state machine). **ARC28/29/30/33** add `apps/sav/platform.py`.
- **Groupe NTSRV (suite — Problem Management + relation client, round 2).** **NTSRV16** adds **Probleme** (`TenantModel`, référence `PRB-YYYYMM-NNNN` via `apps.ventes.utils.references`/`create_with_reference` — never `count()+1`): `statut` (identifie/en_analyse/resolu, une machine STRICTEMENT INDÉPENDANTE des tickets liés) + `cause_racine`; M2M `tickets`→`Ticket` via la table de liaison explicite **ProblemeIncident** (`unique_together probleme+ticket`). Migration sav/0060. **NTSRV17** adds `selectors.tickets_candidats_probleme` — regroupement en lecture seule des tickets ouverts partageant équipement produit + cause (XSAV14), ≥ seuil d'occurrences sur une fenêtre (ne crée jamais un Probleme automatiquement). **NTSRV31** adds l'action `problemes/creer-depuis-regroupement` (POST, transactionnelle : crée le Probleme ET lie les tickets cochés en un seul appel, tout-ou-rien) — écran `/sav/problemes` (`ProblemesPage.jsx`). Endpoints : `problemes/` ViewSet (`?ordering=impact` = nb tickets × ancienneté) + `{id}/lier-ticket|delier-ticket|tickets` + `regroupements-suggeres` (lecture pure).
- **NTSRV23** adds des sous-notes CSAT optionnelles : `SavSlaSettings.csat_detaille_actif` (OFF par défaut) arme `TicketSatisfaction.sous_notes` JSON (clés fermées `rapidite`/`courtoisie`/`resolution`, 1-5) sur le formulaire public de satisfaction. Migration sav/0061.
- **NTSRV24** adds `selectors.taux_resolution_premier_contact` (FCR) — un ticket compte comme résolu au premier contact seulement s'il est clôturé, jamais réouvert (`reopen_count`) et ≤1 échange client ; endpoint `insights/sav-fcr/` (+`?export=xlsx`), responsable/admin uniquement. **NTSRV27** adds `selectors.performance_agent` (tickets traités/délai moyen/CSAT moyen/taux SLA par technicien, trié ALPHABÉTIQUEMENT — jamais un classement) — `insights/sav-performance-agent/` (+`?export=xlsx`).
- **NTSRV28** adds `pdf.fiche_synthese_ticket_pdf` — une fiche imprimable INTERNE (WeasyPrint, chatter complet + fiche d'intervention + signature client si présente) pour le classeur d'intervention/dossier assurance ; JAMAIS `Ticket.cout` ni le prix d'achat/marge. Endpoint `tickets/{id}/fiche-pdf/`.
- **NTSRV34** adds `ReponseType.canaux_autorises` (JSON, vide/NULL = tous canaux — comportement XSAV23 inchangé) restreignant une macro à un sous-ensemble de `CANAUX = (email, whatsapp, portail, interne)` ; le canal de réponse est dérivé de `Ticket.canal_ouverture` via `ReponseType.canal_de_ticket` et appliqué CÔTÉ SERVEUR (une macro hors canal est rejetée) sur `tickets/{id}/noter`. Migration sav/0062.
- **NTSRV38 — cadence SLA au quart d'heure.** Le beat `sav.scan_sla_pre_alerts_and_escalations_quotidien` (pré-alerte + paliers d'escalade NTSRV12) passe de quotidien à `crontab(minute='*/15')`, et `sav.scan_sla_breaches_quart_heure` (scan de dépassement SLA FG81, qui n'avait AUCUN beat avant) est planifié `crontab(minute='7,22,37,52')` ; les exécutions concurrentes sont exclues par un verrou cache best-effort (600s TTL) — jamais une double notification de la même escalade.
- **NTSRV39/NTSRV40 — permissions fines SAV** : `sav_probleme_gerer` (écritures Probleme, distinct de `sav_gerer` — câblé sur `ProblemeViewSet`), `sav_repondre_client_externe` (écrire VERS le client, distinct de traiter le ticket — câblé sur `tickets/{id}/repondre-email`, accordé par défaut à tout rôle ayant déjà `sav_gerer`).
- **NTSRV43** adds `imports.py` — import CSV/XLSX en masse des compétences requises par catégorie de ticket, déclaré comme cible **ARC32** `sav_categorie_competence` dans `platform.py` `import_specs` (un lecteur propre, hors `dataimport.FIELD_MAPS`, même patron que les lecteurs propres de `apps.ao`) — peuple le M2M `CategorieTicket.competences_requises` + `niveau_competence_min`, additif/idempotent, rejets ligne par ligne en français, jamais un rollback global silencieux ; compétences résolues via `apps.rh.selectors.competences_par_code`, jamais `apps.rh.models`. Endpoint `categories-ticket/importer-competences/` (POST multipart `file` + `?apercu=1` pour un aperçu sans écriture).

### portail ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â client self-service portal (Groupe FG228-233, new app)  *( `/api/django/portail/` )*
The Odoo Portal equivalent: a client logs in to see their own devis/factures/chantiers/tickets. Originally built inside `compta`; **ODX12** moved the models here with `db_table = 'compta_<model>'` (`SeparateDatabaseAndState`, state-only ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â no data moved) + a re-export shim left in `apps/compta/models.py`. Links to `crm.Client`/`crm.Lead`/devis/factures/chantiers/tickets are STRING-FK (never a `models` import of ventes/crm/sav/installations). **WIR95** upgraded these from opaque `PositiveIntegerField` ids to REAL `ForeignKey`s string-referencing the target model (`'ventes.Devis'`, `'facturation.Facture'`, `'crm.Client'`, `'crm.Lead'`, `'installations.Installation'`, `'sav.Ticket'`) with `on_delete=SET_NULL, db_constraint=False` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â same pattern as `pos.CommandeRetrait.devis`: no DB-level FK constraint (the 5 domains stay decoupled), but a deletion via the ORM now nulls the reference instead of leaving a silent orphan id; the `_id` attname stays usable everywhere the old field was read. Auth surface (portal tokens/accounts) preserved byte-identical ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â no access widened by the split.
- **Models** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `ComptePortailClient` (tokenized client access account, reuses the client's email ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â no 2nd identity copy per DC32), `AcceptationDevisPortail` (quote e-acceptance from the portal, `devis` FK), `PaiementFacturePortail` (self-service invoice payment record, `facture` FK), `DocumentClientPortail` (client-visible document refs, `client`/`lead` FK ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â **WIR94**: the upload is now ALSO deposited as a real `ged.Document` via `ged.services.deposit_document` (lazy cross-app call, never a `ged.models` import) and referenced by `document_ged`; the historical `fichier` FileField is kept for backward compat), `JalonChantierPortail` (site-progress milestones surfaced to the client), `DemandeTicketPortail` (SAV ticket requests raised from the portal).
- **CHT10** adds `JalonChantierPortail.cle_phase` (stable per-phase key) + `services.upsert_jalon_chantier` (idempotent upsert by company+chantier+phase — the ONLY write path for site-progress milestones) + `selectors.jalons_du_chantier`; `installations`' CHT11 is its sole caller today. **NTPRT9/NTPRT14** add client-only endpoints `client/tableau-de-bord/` and `mes-chantiers/` (+ a `photos/` action) — jalon timeline + before/during/after gallery, never financial data.
- Endpoints (new prefix; the SAME ViewSets are also still served under `/api/django/compta/ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚Â¦` for backward compatibility, incl. the public tokenized `portail/<token>/ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚Â¦` views which stay on compta): `comptes-portail/`, `acceptations-devis-portail/`, `paiements-facture-portail/`, `documents-client-portail/`, `jalons-chantier-portail/`, `demandes-ticket-portail/`.
- **NTPRT21/28/30/32/34/35 (self-service connecté, non tokenisé).** `mes-bons-commande/` (NTPRT21, fournisseur connecté — même effet que le lien tokenisé XPUR22) ; `mes-soumissions/` (NTPRT28, deal registration partenaire, anti-doublon 30 j, GATÉ par le statut d'agrément NTPRT32/FG237 — un partenaire non agréé/suspendu est refusé, son historique reste consultable) ; `mes-commissions/` (NTPRT30, relevé + export PDF, lecture seule) ; `ma-preference/` (NTPRT34, langue — seule surface commune aux 3 portails) ; `satisfaction/` (NTPRT35, le déclencheur d'écran qui manquait à l'enquête FG238/239). Écrans `/portail/fournisseur/commandes`, `/portail/partenaire/affaires`, `/portail/partenaire/commissions`.
- **NTSRV2** adds `selectors.client_par_token_acces(token)` — lecture seule, `(company_id, client_id)` du compte portail ACTIF pour ce jeton, ou `None` (jeton révoqué/inconnu). Consommé par `apps.sav` (formulaire portail public → ticket SAV) : la société n'est jamais lue du corps de requête.

### reporting ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â dashboards, KPIs, insights, audit log  *(no models)*
Read-only aggregation across crm/ventes/installations/sav/stock, role-filtered.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/reporting/`): `dashboard/`, `search/`, `notifications/`, `calendar/` and `calendar/reschedule/` (agenda events + drag-reschedule), `pipeline/` (funnel value by STAGES, weighted forecast), `reports/sales|stock|service/` (+`?export=xlsx`), `insights/recurring-revenue|audit-log|job-costing|analytics|commissions/`, `archive/client/{id}/` and `archive/chantier/{id}/`. `job-costing` (margin via internal `prix_achat`) and `commissions` (sales commission per `CompanyProfile.commission_mode`) are admin-only.
- **RapportDefinition** (NTEXT10, `rapport_builder.py`, previously undocumented here) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a rejouable cross-tab report definition (source query + optional `pivot_spec`); endpoint `rapport-definitions/` (+ an execute action building rows via `PivotSpec`/`build_pivot`). This batch's **PACT146** fixes the execute response shape: without a `pivot_spec` the payload is now a LITERAL `{'rows': rows}` (the `pivot` key stays absent, not a dynamically-omitted one ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `scripts/check_api_shapes.py` only sees literal dict keys) and gives it its first screen, `/reporting/rapport-builder`. **PACT144** gives the pre-existing `ai_governance` "rapport pÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©riodique" feature (see below) its first screen, `/reporting/rapport-periode`. **NTEXT11** adds `rapport-definitions/{id}/export/?format=csv|xlsx` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a file export of the replayed definition, reusing the existing subscription-export infra, buy-price/margin columns stripped. **NTDATA38-40 (31/08)** : `SavedReport.Schedule` gagne `monthly` + `heure_envoi`/`jour_du_mois` (borné 1-28, anti-doublon mensuel sur `last_sent_at`) ; canal `whatsapp` = LIEN signé expirant 7 j vers un rendu public throttlé (jamais la pièce jointe ; no-op sans BSP armé — `notifications.whatsapp_bsp_actif`) ; **EnvoiRapport** (`TenantModel`, migrations reporting/0014-16) journalise chaque tentative email/WhatsApp avec son motif — `envois-rapports/` lecture seule, filtrable `?statut=echec`.
- **CHT27** adds `reports_chantier.py` (`reports/chantier/`, responsable/admin) — an INSTRUMENTED chantier KPI cockpit: per-stage cycle time (médianes/moyennes, derived from the `InstallationActivity` chatter across SIGNE→PLANIFIE→EN_COURS→INSTALLE→RECEPTIONNE, never the double-duty milestone date fields), post-MES rework rate, and overdue chantiers; no `prix_achat`/margin. Frontend `PilotageChantiersPage` (`/reporting/pilotage-chantiers`).
- **NTJUR48 / NTDATA13.** 4 KPI contentieux (`juridique_dossiers_ouverts`/`_montant_en_jeu_total`/`_taux_gain`/`_delai_moyen_resolution`, `apps.juridique.selectors.kpis_juridiques`, EXCLUT toujours les dossiers confidentiels des agrégats) rejoignent le catalogue fermé `KpiAlerte.Kpi`. `KpiAlerte.source` (catalogue/métrique, NTDATA13) permet aussi de surveiller une **métrique nommée** de `semantic.MetricDefinition` (`metric_definition` FK vérifiée dans la société de l'appelant) plutôt qu'un KPI du catalogue fermé.

### parametres ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â company profile, business settings, WhatsApp templates
- **Fenêtres d'appel + cadences (MRY4/MRY8, 06/09/2026, migrations parametres 0081-0084)** — 9 champs de fenêtres d'appel sur `CompanyProfile` (pacing Ramadan réparé — le champ n'avait jamais existé), éditeur de cadences `/parametres/cadence-relance/` (PATCH in-place), audit du profil dérivé du modèle (96/102 champs journalisés, AUD807).
- **CompanyProfile** (one per company) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â identity + Moroccan legal IDs (`ice`, `identifiant_fiscal`, `rc`, `patente`, `cnss`, `rib`); branding (`logo_key`, `signature_key`, `couleur_principale`); `responsable_defaut_leads` FK (default lead owner); quote-gen knobs (`payment_terms` JSON, `quote_validity_days`, `tva_standard/panneaux`, ROI constants `onee_tarif_kwh`/`productible_kwh_kwc`/`rendement_global`, `remise_max_pct`, `discount_approval_threshold`, `agricole_pump_hours`); `default_installer` FK (default technician for new chantiers, N66; NULL = creator is responsable); sales commission (`commission_mode` off/pct_devis/par_kwc + `commission_valeur`, sensitive/admin-only, N99); referral toggle (`referral_enabled` bool + `referral_reward`, N98); silent DGI export master switch (`dgi_export_actif` bool, **default OFF**, N105 ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â arms the ventes `dgi/` capability, invisible while off); `doc_prefixes`/`doc_numbering` JSON. **SCA27** adds `site_web` (migration 0055) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â the tenant's own site URL, consumed by the quote-engine residential PDF footer/legal band instead of a hardcoded founder fallback (byte-identical repli when empty).
- **TauxTVA / ConditionPaiement / UniteMesure** (ARC23/24/27, `models_taxes.py`/`models_payment_terms.py`/`models_units.py`) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â per-company referentials, each a `core.TenantModel` subclass (redeclares `company` for its own `related_name`, ARC1 motif): `TauxTVA` (code/libelle/taux, Moroccan rates 20/14/10/7/0, one `defaut=True`) feeds the default rate at devis/facture creation without rewriting an already-frozen document rate; `CompanyProfile.tva_standard` (existing knob, editable in ParamÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¨tres) takes PRECEDENCE over the referential's seeded default. `ConditionPaiement` (libellÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©/dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©lai jours/fin de mois/escompte %) sources the default `Facture.conditions_paiement` label and mirrors `stock.Fournisseur`'s existing numeric fields. `UniteMesure` (code/libellÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©) mirrors `stock.Produit.unite_stock` (which stays master). All three purely additive/declarative ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â absent referential falls back to prior hardcoded behaviour; seeded at signup, backfillable via `backfill_conditions_paiement`/`backfill_unites_mesure` management commands.
- **MessageTemplate** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â WhatsApp templates by `cle` (devis/facture/relance), `corps_fr` + `corps_darija`.
 **VISITE-CADENCE (15/09/2026, migration parametres 0089)** : + clés `visite_proposition` / `visite_confirmation` (défauts FR + darija « à faire relire », zéro chiffre, `{conseiller}`/`{date_visite}` — miroir `docs/crm/messages_meryem.md` re-dérivé par tests).
- **EmailTemplate** (FG17, in `models_email.py`) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â editable e-mail templates by `cle` (devis/facture/relance/notification): `sujet` + `corps` with the same placeholder whitelist as WhatsApp (`{civilite}{nom}{reference}{lien}{n}`), `unique_together company+cle`. Helpers `EmailTemplate.get_template`/`render` (tolerant) for the future automation-email rewire (intentionally NOT wired yet). Endpoints `email-templates/` (CRUD) + `email-templates/effective/` (defaultsÃƒÂ¢Ã…Â Ã¢â‚¬Â¢overrides) + `email-templates/bulk/` (upsert), writes audited.
- **Realisation (catalogue « Réalisations », 08/09/2026, `models_realisations.py`, migration 0086)** — UNE installation réelle par ligne (`titre`/`ville`/`puissance_kwc`/`mise_en_service`/`url_page`/`lien_suivi`/`actif`), socle `TenantModel`, unicité `(company, url_page)` ; ville canonisée au `save()` par le gazetier (`villes_resolution.corriger_ville`), mise en service ramenée au 1er du mois. Endpoint `realisations/` (lecture tout rôle, écriture admin/responsable ; `company` forcée côté serveur, jamais lue du corps). Sélecteur PUR `selectors.realisation_pour_lead(lead)` — même ville d'abord (`Lead.ville_reference` prime sur `ville`), sinon la plus proche à ≤ `RAYON_PREUVE_KM` (60 km, haversine sur les coordonnées du gazetier) — consommé par `crm.services.message_pour_etape` pour remplir `{mois_preuve}`/`{ville_preuve}`/`{lien_preuve}` de la touche `j4_preuve` ; sans réalisation les trois restent vides et la phrase est OMISE (MRY13). Écran : Paramètres → Réalisations (`pages/parametres/RealisationsSection.jsx`).
- **SettingsAuditLog** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â who changed which setting field.
- **StatutConfig** (N58, in `models_statuses.py`) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â per-company display overlay for chantier/SAV/bon-de-commande statuses: `domaine` + canonical `cle` + `libelle` + `ordre` + `actif` (`unique_together company+domaine+cle`). Display-only ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â canonical keys & state machines stay in their source models; defaults read live from `Installation.STATUT_ORDER`/`Ticket.STATUT_ORDER`/`BonCommande.Statut` (`statuses_defaults.py`), so output is byte-identical until edited.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/parametres/`): `GET /`, `PUT/PATCH /update/`, `POST /upload-logo|upload-signature/`, `DELETE /delete-logo|delete-signature/`, `GET+PUT/PATCH /messages/`, `GET /audit/`; `statuts/` ViewSet (N58) + `statuts/effective/?domaine=` (full ordered effective list) + `statuts/bulk/` (upsert a domaine). Reads `GET /` and `GET /messages/` are open to any role; every write/audit endpoint (incl. `statuts/` writes) is the Administrateur + Responsable tier (`IsAdminOrResponsableTier`), limited tier blocked.
- **NTEXT19** adds `gabarits.py`/`views_gabarits.py` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â read-only custom document templates (`gabarits-document/`) + `gabarits-document/<code>/rendre/?cible_id=<id>` (renders the template as PDF for one target record). **NTEXT39** adds `gabarits-document/<code>/apercu/` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â renders the same template with SAMPLE data (no real record touched), streamed as `<code>-apercu.pdf`.
- **NTEXT19** adds `gabarits.py`/`views_gabarits.py` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â read-only custom document templates (`gabarits-document/`) + `gabarits-document/<code>/rendre/?cible_id=<id>` (renders the template as PDF for one target record).
- **ParametresPos / BoutiquePos** (NTRET8, `models_pos.py`) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â ParamÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¨tres ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ Point de vente section (`PointDeVenteSection.jsx`, embedded in `ParametresEntreprise`, no dedicated route). `ParametresPos` (one per company) carries `taux_horaire_comptoir` (counter labour rate, distinct from `CompanyProfile.taux_horaire_sav`); the line-discount threshold and printer/TPE config are REUSED as-is from `CompanyProfile.discount_approval_threshold` and `pos.ConfigMaterielPOS` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â never duplicated here. `BoutiquePos` marks an EXISTING `stock.EmplacementStock` (string-FK) as a physical point of sale, with its own receipt address/hours + `surface_m2` (NTRET16, feeds the POS retail dashboard's sales-per-mÃƒâ€šÃ‚Â² KPI). Endpoints: `GET /parametres/pos/` + `PUT /parametres/pos/update/`, `pos-boutiques/` ViewSet.
- **Licence tier (NTADM7/8, 2026-08-05)** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `CompanyProfile.plan` (nullable FKÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢`adminops.PlanLicence`, NULL = full access) + `nb_sieges_max` (nullable = unlimited); `parametres/feature_flags.py::has_feature(company, module_key)` (never restrictive by default ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â foundation only, nav masking stays on FG391/ODX); `services_licence.py` records plan changes in `SettingsAuditLog` + fires the `plan.changed` webhook; seat counting = `authentication.services.sieges_utilises`.

### scm ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â supply chain planning (Groupe NTSCM, new app)  *( `/api/django/scm/` )*
Demand forecasting, ABC classification, stock policies, a consolidated reappro dashboard, and a monthly S&OP cycle ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a PLANNING layer above `apps.stock`'s existing execution (basic reappro/RFQ/DemandeAchat/transfers/supplier scorecard); reads `apps.stock` only via its `selectors.py`/`services.py`, never a model import.
- **PrevisionDemande** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â per produit/segment/period (`YYYY-MM`) demand forecast; `methode` (moyenne_mobile/tendance/saisonnier/manuel). **EvenementDemande** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a signed % demand impact (promo/chantier majeur/rupture fournisseur/saisonnalitÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©) over a date window, applied by `services.generer_previsions`.
- **ClassificationABC** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â Pareto A/B/C ranking, recalculated server-side only (`selectors.classifier_abc`) and persisted HERE (not on `stock.Produit`, cross-app boundary) as a OneToOne. **PolitiqueStock** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â per-produit min/max/ROP/safety-stock policy; `service_level_pct` seeded from the ABC class (A=95/B=90/C=85) but stays editable; ROP/safety-stock derived fields written ONLY by `services.recalculer_politiques_stock`.
- **CyclePlanificationSOP** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â monthly S&OP cycle, a SEQUENTIAL state machine (brouillonÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢revue_demandeÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢revue_offreÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢revue_financeÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢rÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©union_rÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©conciliationÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢approuvÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢clos) advanced only via `services.avancer_statut_cycle` (no step-skipping) or reopened via `services.reouvrir_cycle` (admin-only, audited). **LigneDemandeSOP** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a FROZEN snapshot of consensus demand per produit, taken automatically on brouillonÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢revue_demande. **LigneOffreSOP** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â mirrored supply snapshot (stock disponible + capacitÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© appro fournisseur) vs the frozen demand, surfacing the offreÃƒÂ¢Ã‹â€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢demande gap.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/scm/`): `previsions-demande/` (+ `generer`), `evenements-demande/`, `classification-abc/` (+ `recalculer`), `politiques-stock/` (+ `recalculer`), `cycles-sop/` (+ `{id}/avancer-statut|reouvrir|historique|lignes-demande|ajuster-demande|calculer-offre|ecarts|impact-financier`), `tableau-bord-reappro/` (+ `creer-bcf`, groups lines by supplier into draft `stock.BonCommandeFournisseur`s via `stock.services`). Responsable/admin only. Frontend: `/scm/reappro` + `/scm/sop` (admin-only) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â see Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â§5.

### mrp ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â Production / MRP II (Groupe NTMFG, new app)  *( `/api/django/mrp/` )*
Generic manufacturing engine ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â work centers, routings, capacitated manufacturing orders, MRP net-requirements, finite-capacity scheduling, an MES shop-floor terminal, and standard/actual costing. Distinct and complementary to `installations`' existing lightweight kitting (`Kit`/`KitComposant`/`OrdreAssemblage`) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â never rebuilt here; reads `installations`/`stock` only via `selectors.py`/`services.py` or string-FK.
- **PosteDeCharge** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â work center (machine/ligne/manuel/sous-traitÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©), daily capacity + an INTERNAL hourly cost (never client-facing, like `Produit.prix_achat`); optional `sous_traitant` FKÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢`stock.Fournisseur`.
- **Gamme** + **OperationGamme** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a reusable routing (ordered poste+temps standard) for one produit, versioned; optional `kit_source` FKÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢`stock.KitProduit` read-only for backflush at OF closure when no `kit_ordre_assemblage` link exists.
- **OrdreFabrication** (OF) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â capacitated manufacturing order; EXTENDS (never duplicates) `installations.OrdreAssemblage` via an optional `kit_ordre_assemblage` link (when present, that model owns the stock movement). **OperationOF** + **PauseOperationOF** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â instantiated-from-gamme operations with MES start/pause/resume/finish timestamps + scrap tracking. **ReservationOF** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â internal component reservation bookkeeping (does NOT affect the cross-app `stock.services.available_quantity`, which stays driven by `installations` reservations only).
- **CoutStandard** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â versioned, immutable-once-created standard cost (matiÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¨re/main-d'Ãƒâ€¦Ã¢â‚¬Å“uvre/indirect %) per produit ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â strictly internal, never in a client document.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/mrp/`): `postes-charge/` (+ `{id}/oee`), `gammes/`, `operations-gamme/`, `ordres-fabrication/` (+ `{id}/confirmer|cloturer|annuler|dispo-composants`), `operations-of/` (+ `{id}/replanifier|demarrer|pauser|reprendre|terminer`), `couts-standard/` (+ `figer`), `mrp-run/`, `charge-postes/`, `analyse-couts/`, `oee-postes/`. Frontend: `/mrp/ordres-fabrication|gantt|terminal|oee|analyse-couts` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â see Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â§5.

### transport ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â transport orders (Groupe SUPPLY/NTLOG1-20, new app)  *( `/api/django/transport/` )*
EnlÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¨vement/livraison/inter-site/import/export transport orders, from planning through delivery, with freight cost tracking and carrier disputes. Cross-app links (`ventes`/`stock`/`installations`/`flotte`) are ALL opaque integer string-FKs (`<app>_<model>_id`), resolved at read time via the target app's selectors or a function-local `apps.get_model` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â never a static model import. History/attachments reuse the generic `records.Activity`/`records.Attachment` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â no new `*Activity`/`FileField`.
- **OrdreTransport** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `numero` (race-safe `core.numbering`); `type_flux` (enlÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¨vement_livraison/inter_site/import/export); its OWN `statut` machine (brouillon/planifiÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©/en_cours/livrÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©/annulÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©, independent of STAGES.py); `mode_transport` (flotte_propre/affrÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¨tement) + `mode_acheminement_physique` (route/mer/air, feeds the CO2 estimate). **LigneOrdreTransport** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â merchandise (produit opaque ref, poids/volume/valeur dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©clarÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©e). **EtapeTransport** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â enlÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¨vementÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢transitÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢livraison steps.
- **CoutFretReel** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â actual freight cost lines (transport/assurance/manutention/dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©douanement), the read point a future landed-cost extension (FG316) can consume. **LitigeTransport** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â an independent carrier-dispute state machine (ouvert/en_traitement/rÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©solu/rejetÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©), modeled on `litiges.Reclamation` without importing it; transitions log to the parent order's chatter. **ReserveReception** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a reception-time reserve (photos via `records.Attachment`) that automatically opens a `LitigeTransport`.
- **FacteurEmissionCO2** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â per-company, per-mode (route/mer/air) kg-CO2-per-tonne-km factor, editable in ParamÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¨tres.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/transport/`): `ordres-transport/` (+ `{id}/etapes|comparer-transporteurs|co2|livrer`), `lignes-transport/`, `etapes-transport/`, `couts-fret/`, `litiges-transport/` (+ `{id}/prendre-en-charge|resoudre|rejeter|reclamer-transporteur`), `reserves-reception/`, `facteurs-emission-co2/`. Frontend: `/transport/ordres` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â see Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â§5.

### douane ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â customs & import/export (Groupe NTLOG, new app)  *( `/api/django/douane/` )*
Currently EXPORT-only (NTLOG14): `DossierExport`/`PieceDossierExport`/`ParametresDouane`. **The import side does not exist here** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â NTLOG10's original plan conflicts with the pre-existing `installations.DossierImport` (FG315, owned by the PLAN_SERVICE domain); NTLOG10 stays `[BLOCKED]` pending a reconciliation that must edit `apps/installations` (out of this lane's write scope), and NTLOG11/12/13/21/22/26/30 all depend on it and are BLOCKED with it. **Trap for a future session: `douane.DossierImport` does NOT exist ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â do not assume it.**
- **DossierExport** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `numero` (race-safe); `incoterm` (EXW/FOB/CFR/CIF/DAP/DDP); optional `devis`/`facture` FK (read links into `ventes`/`facturation`); ports embarquement/dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©barquement, `pays_destinataire`, `devise`, `valeur_marchandise_devise`; its OWN `statut` (ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â _prÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©parer/DUM_dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©posÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©e/en_dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©douanement/levÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©/clÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â´turÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©), independent of any import statut. **PieceDossierExport** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â required/deposited document slots (facture_export/packing_list/certificat_origine/DUM_export/EUR.1), the file itself in `records.Attachment`.
- **ParametresDouane** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â one per company (`for_company` singleton pattern), `regime_douanier_par_defaut`, `alerte_expiration_jours` (J-30/15/7 default), `mention_estimation_droits` (label reserved for NTLOG13/30, both BLOCKED ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â nothing consumes it yet).
- Endpoints (`/api/django/douane/`): `dossiers-export/` (+ `export`), `dossiers-export-pieces/`, `parametres-douane/`. Frontend: `/parametres/douane` (ParamÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¨tres tab, responsable/admin) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â see Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â§5.

### ecommerce_connect ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â Shopify/WooCommerce connectors (Groupe NTRET18/19, new app)  *( `/api/django/ecommerce-connect/` )*
**[GATED: no API key/secret in .env]** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â every sync degrades to a total no-op (zero network calls); this module stores NO secret in the database, only non-sensitive metadata. `produit_id`/`facture_id` reference `stock.Produit`/`ventes.Facture` by opaque integer, never a cross-app FK. No frontend screen this batch.
- **ConnexionEcommerce** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â one per (company, plateforme shopify/woocommerce); `boutique_url`, `actif` toggle (does NOT bypass the missing-key no-op), last catalogue/orders sync timestamps.
- **ProduitSync** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â ERPÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬Âexternal product mapping; `vendable_en_ligne` opt-in lives HERE (not a new field on `stock.Produit`); sync statut (ok/erreur/en_attente).
- **CommandeSync** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a PAID external order processed into the ERP; idempotency key = (`connexion`, `external_order_id`) so an at-least-once webhook replay never creates a duplicate invoice.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/ecommerce-connect/`): `connexions/` (+ `{id}/sync-catalogue`), `produits-sync/`, `commandes/`; public HMAC-signed webhooks `shopify/webhook/commande/` and `woocommerce/webhook/commande/` (signature = authentication, no login).

### promotions ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â cart promotion engine (Groupe NTRET12/13/15, new app)  *( `/api/django/promotions/` )*
Configurable cart promotion rules, unique-code coupons, and gift cards ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â consumed by `apps.pos` at checkout (`services.evaluer_panier`, the ONLY entry point `pos.services` calls). The calculation itself lives in a pure, ORM-free `engine.py`; `services.py` is the ORMÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬Âengine bridge. `categorie`/`produit` reference `apps.stock` by string-FK only. No frontend screen of its own ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â consumed inside the `pos` coquille (Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â§5).
- **ReglexPromotion** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a configurable rule: `type_regle` (remise_%_produit/remise_montant_panier/n_pour_m/plage_horaire "happy hour"), `priorite` + `cumulable` (non-cumulable rules: only the most advantageous applicable one is kept), optional targeting (`categorie`/`produit`/`montant_min_panier`) and validity window/days/hours. The only model with a CRUD ViewSet ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â read open to any role (the till needs to know active rules), write responsable/admin.
- **CouponUnique** + **CouponUtilisation** (NTRET13) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a single-code coupon tied to ONE `ReglexPromotion`, `mode_limite` (unique_par_client/global), consumed via `services.consommer_coupon` (server-side, journaled ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â no standalone CRUD, only through `pos`'s `coupon` action). Distinct from `compta.CodePromotion` (generic marketing campaign code, no usage tracking).
- **CarteCadeau** (NTRET15) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a gift card, sold at the till (no stock line ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â not an inventoried product), usable as a partial/total payment method across multiple visits until exhausted; debited via `services.debiter_carte_cadeau` (`select_for_update`, never negative).
- Endpoints (`/api/django/promotions/`): `regles/` (+ `simuler`, a dry-run cart-promotion preview for the till, no sale created). Coupons/gift cards have no standalone CRUD ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â consumed through `pos`'s `{id}/coupon`, `emettre-carte-cadeau/`, `{id}/payer-carte-cadeau` actions.

### roles ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â RBAC  *( `/api/django/roles/` )*
- **Role** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `company` FK; `nom` (unique per company); `permissions` JSON (validated against canonical `ALL_PERMISSIONS`); `est_systeme` bool (system roles undeletable). Linked from `CustomUser.role`.
- 2026-06-18 (Feature D): `ALL_PERMISSIONS` expanded to a moduleÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬â€action grid + governance codes (`*_export`, `crm/ventes/sav_reassign`, `technicien_assign`, `prix_achat_voir`, `journal_activite_voir`, scope markers `records_scope_equipe`/`records_scope_sous_arbre`). **NTCPQ36** adds 4 CPQ permission codes: `cpq_regles_gerer`, `cpq_prix_contractuels_gerer`, `cpq_marge_voir` (ELEVATED tier, same palier as `marge_voir`/`prix_achat_voir`), `cpq_approbation_approuver`. `CANONICAL_SYSTEM_ROLES` seeds **seven** roles per company ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â Directeur, Administrateur (=Admin), Commercial responsable, Commercial, Technicien responsable, Technicien, Viewer ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â plus the legacy Responsable/Utilisateur kept for existing accounts. Seeder: `init_roles` (also maps ownersÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢Directeur, custom commercialÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢Commercial; N103: self-heals a drifted same-named system role to `est_systeme=True`). `role_tiers.py` now derives the tier from the authoritative permission signal first (`roles_gerer`ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢admin, `users_voir`ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢responsable) with the name mapping as fallback ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â so a Directeur/Administrateur whose seeded row drifted to `est_systeme=False` still resolves to the admin tier and keeps access to `/users/` and `/roles/` (N103 regression fix), without widening Commercial/Technicien/Viewer.
- Endpoints: Role ViewSet (CRUD, open to the Administrateur + Responsable tier via `IsAdminOrResponsableTier` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â limited tier blocked; delete blocked if system or in-use) + `permissions-disponibles/`.

### identity ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â security & access foundation (Groupe NTSEC, new app)  *( `/api/django/identity/` )*
Company-scoped security controls, all INERT by default (off-by-default `mode`/toggle, never blocks a request until explicitly configured).
- **Models** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `NetworkPolicy` + `IpAllowRule` (NTSEC11: per-company IP/CIDR allowlist, `mode` off/monitor/enforce, enforced by `middleware.NetworkPolicyMiddleware`), `TrustedDevice` (NTSEC14: "remember this device" ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â skips MFA on a non-expired/non-revoked trusted device only if the company opts in), `IdentityProvider` (NTSEC1: per-company SSO SAML/OIDC federation metadata), `ScimToken` + `ScimGroupMapping` (NTSEC5/6: SCIM 2.0 provisioning bearer token, hashed like `publicapi.ApiKey`; SCIM-groupÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢`roles.Role` mapping via string-id, no `roles.models` import), `BreakGlassGrant` (NTSEC22: time-boxed audited emergency Administrateur elevation that bypasses `enforce_sso`, gated `apps.identity.selectors.is_break_glass_active`).
- Endpoints: `network-policies/`, `ip-allow-rules/`, `trusted-devices/`, `providers/`; SCIM 2.0 `scim/v2/<company_slug>/Users|Groups` (token-authenticated, machine-to-machine); `service-accounts/` (NTSEC24, model lives in `publicapi`, exposed here); `login-banner/` (public); `break-glass/` and `posture/` (NTSEC27 consolidated security posture) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â both Directeur-only.

### accessreview ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â access governance (NTSEC19, new app)  *( `/api/django/accessreview/` )*
- **Models** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `AccessReviewCampaign` (periodic access-review campaign: pÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©rimÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¨tre all/role/module, dates, statut ouverte/ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚Â¦), `AccessReviewItem` (one row per in-scope user, attested or revoked by a manager ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â revocation removes the role via `apps.roles.services`, never a direct `roles.models` import), `SodRule` (separation-of-duties: a conflicting permission pair, `severite` info/warning/critique ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â critique can BLOCK a role assignment that would create the cumul).
- Endpoints: `campaigns/` (+ `{id}/attester/`), `sod-rules/` (+ `violations/`, `seed-standard/`). All `company`-scoped (`TenantModel`).
- **WIR136** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â mounting dedup: `identity`/`accessreview` were each mounted TWICE in `erp_agentique/urls.py` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `identity` once inside the shared `_APP_URLS` list (ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ both `api/django/` and `api/v1/`) and AGAIN standalone at `api/django/identity/` (harmless duplicate); `accessreview` was mounted ONLY standalone at `api/django/`, so it never got an `api/v1/accessreview/` mirror like every other app. Both are now solely in `_APP_URLS` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â same `api/django/ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚Â¦` paths as before, `accessreview` gains its missing `api/v1/` mirror, `frais` (new this run) joins the same list.

### audit ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â activity log (audit trail)  *( `/api/django/audit/` )*
- **AuditLog** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â company-scoped (server-forced, nullable for failed login); `user` FK (null=system) + `actor_username` snapshot; `action` (create/update/delete/status/login/logout/login_failed/pdf/email/whatsapp/export/accept/refuse); `content_type` + `object_id` + `object_repr` (link-back snapshot); `detail`; `timestamp` (UTC, bucketed in Africa/Casablanca at read time).
- Capture: `apps/audit/signals.py` (post_save/post_delete + status-change via pre_save cache) on the main business models, gated by `apps/audit/middleware.py` (records only inside a request ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ no seed/migration noise); login/logout in `authentication/views.py`, failed login via `user_login_failed`; key actions (PDF/export/WhatsApp) via explicit `recorder.record` calls. Best-effort ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â never blocks the request.
- Endpoints (gated on `journal_activite_voir`, Directeur-only by default): `stats/` (hourly buckets for a day, per-day for week/month, Casablanca, filterable), `entries/` (paginated filterable list, newest first), `meta/` (filter-bar data).

### records ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â generic activities + attachments  *( `/api/django/records/` )*
ContentType-based, attachable to Lead/Client/Installation/Ticket.
- **ActivityType** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â configurable types (Appel/Email/RelanceÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚Â¦), `delai_defaut_jours`.
- **Activity** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â generic FK target; `activity_type` FK; `due_date`; `assigned_to` FK; `done` bool + `done_at/by`; `auto_relance` bool (auto-synced from `Lead.relance_date`).
- **Attachment** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â generic FK target; `file_key` (MinIO); `phase` (avant/pendant/aprÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¨s for field photos).
- Endpoints: `activity-types/`, `activities/` (+ `mine/`, `{id}/done/`), `attachments/` (+ `{id}/download`, `attachments-count/`).
- **ARC30** switches `ALLOWED_TARGETS` from a literal set to a lazy union ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `core.platform`'s `record_targets` surface, computed on first use, never at import ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â with the 19 historical targets migrated into 8 new `platform.py` manifests (outillage/rh/qhse/kb/ged/flotte/gestion_projet/ao) plus crm/contrats/sav/stock/ventes/installations from ARC29; a drop-in replacement (in/iteration/len identical, non-regression tested). **ARC8/26/SCA4/37** guard logic (no new bespoke `*Activity` chatter, no new `FileField` outside `records.Attachment`/`ged.Document`, no hand-rolled `company` FK model, no hand-rolled "document mÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©tier" outside the SCA30 kit) lives in `apps/records/platform_guards.py` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â pure text/regex scanners against frozen baselines in `apps/records/platform_baselines/`, consumed by both `scripts/check_platform.py` (CI) and the Django test runner.

### customfields ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â admin-defined custom fields  *( `/api/django/custom-fields/` )*
- **CustomFieldDef** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `module` (LEAD/CLIENT/PRODUIT), `code` (slug), `type` (TEXT/NUMBER/DATE/CHOICE/BOOLEAN), `options` JSON, `obligatoire/visible_liste/actif`. Values live in each target model's `custom_data` JSON (no schema migration).
- **ARC14** adds a data-driven registry (`app_label`/`model` pairs, not a hardcoded module enum) with 2 pilots ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `contrats.Contrat`, `flotte.Vehicule` (each own a `custom_data` JSON field, migrations contrats/0038 + flotte/0057). **ARC31** switches the registry's central loader to `register_from_platform_manifests()` (`CustomfieldsConfig.ready()`), reading the `customfield_models` surface off `core.platform` manifests instead of explicit `register()` calls in each app's `ready()` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â the 8 native + 2 pilot models are unchanged, non-regression tested.
- **CustomObjectDef** + **CustomRecord** (XPLT16, previously undocumented here) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â no-code custom business objects: an admin defines an object (`code`/`libelle`/`icone`) whose fields are ordinary `CustomFieldDef` rows under `module='custom:<code>'` (full reuse, no second field-definition engine); each row of data lives in `CustomRecord.data`. NTEXT2/NTEXT3 auto-generate the list/form views from the field schema. Endpoints `objects/`, `custom-objects/<code>/records/(<id>/)`, `custom-objects/<code>/vue-liste/`, `custom-objects/<code>/vue-formulaire/`. **PACT140** gives it its first screens: admin `/parametres/objets-personnalises` (define objects/fields, admin-only) + one generic `/objets/:code` records screen serving every defined object.
- Endpoints: `definitions/` ViewSet.
- **NTEXT24** adds `blueprint.py` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â export/import of the platform CONFIG (custom objects/fields), idempotent by natural key (re-import never duplicates). **NTEXT34** adds `objets-catalogue/` (list installable custom-object templates) + `objets-catalogue/installer/<code>/` (install one into the tenant). **NTEXT38** adds `verrouille` bool (migration 0008) on `CustomFieldDef`/`CustomObjectDef` (admin lock against accidental edit/delete) + `verrouiller`/`deverrouiller` actions on `definitions/<pk>/` and `objects/<pk>/`; `audit_plateforme.py` reports it.
- **NTEXT1** adds a `FORMULA` field type — computed at READ time only (`services.evaluer_champ_formule` → `core.formula.evaluer_formule`, an AST-safe evaluator: never `eval`, no loops/imports; `prix_achat`/`marge` etc. stripped from context before evaluation), never persisted, never writable via the serializer. **NTEXT28** adds `ROLLUP` (`rollup_config={'objet_lie','cle_liaison','agg','champ'}`, aggregates linked `CustomRecord`s via `core.pivot._aggregate`, hard-capped `LIMITE_ROLLUP=5000`) — same read-only, never-persisted contract.
- **NTEXT9** adds `FieldRolePermission` (company, field_def, role_tier, `niveau`: masque/lecture/edition) — per-field visibility/edit gate by role tier (`services.niveau_pour_role`, defaults to `EDITION` when no row exists → zero behaviour change for undeclared pairs). Endpoint `permissions-role/`.

### tiers ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â cross-app party directory (ARC17, foundation app)  *( `/api/django/tiers/` )*
The Odoo `res.partner` equivalent: one identity record instead of the same name/coordinates/legal-IDs being re-entered in 5+ domain models. A FOUNDATION app (import-linter `tiers-is-a-base-layer` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â domain apps depend on it, never the reverse); this run only extends the pre-existing `Tiers` model + `TiersViewSet` (`ARC17`) with bridges and read-side reconciliation.
- **Tiers** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `company` FK; identity fields (`nom`/`prenom`/`raison_sociale`, `telephone`/`whatsapp`/`email`/`adresse`/`ville`/GPS, Moroccan IDs `ice`/`rc`/`identifiant_fiscal`/`cin`/`rib`); role flags `is_client`/`is_fournisseur`/`is_partenaire`/`is_soustraitant`; `type_tiers`.
- **ARC18** adds additive nullable `tiers` FKs (string-FK `'tiers.Tiers'`) mirroring identity ONE-WAY from `crm.Client` and `stock.Fournisseur` (migrations crm/0050, stock/0075) into a `Tiers` row, via `apps/{crm,stock}/tiers_bridge.py` + `attacher_ou_creer_tiers` (dedup by company-scoped email/ICE, else create ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â never duplicates; also persists the role flags on the create path, a real bug fix landed mid-run). **ARC19** extends the same bridge to `compta.Partenaire` (migration compta/0108) and `rh.DossierEmploye` (migration rh/0082). **ARC56** adds the bridge to `crm.Lead` (migration crm/0051) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â pre-conversion identity, so a lead already has a `Tiers` before it becomes a client. In every case the historical model stays the IDENTITY MASTER; `tiers` is a mirror only.
- **ARC20** adds read-only cross-referencing: `selectors.find_by_ice`/`find_by_email` (company-scoped lookups) and `selectors.find_duplicates` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a same-ICE/same-email cluster report (e.g. one party registered as both a Fournisseur and a Partenaire) surfaced at `GET tiers/doublons/` (admin-only, no fusion/write).
- **ARC21** is a DECISION, flag-gated OFF: `TIERS_SOURCE_ECRITURE` would make `Tiers` the write-source of identity instead of a mirror ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â documented in `docs/decisions/ARC21-tiers-source-ecriture.md`, not flipped in this run.
- `management/backfill_tiers` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â idempotent one-off command materializing `Tiers` bridges for existing rows across the four bridged models.

### migration ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â reprise de donnÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©es client (NTMIG)  *( `/api/django/migration/` )*
