Chargement en **remplissage-seul FORCÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â°** (`ecraser=False` codÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© en dur, aucun interrupteur exposÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© par l'API ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â une cellule vide ne remplace jamais une valeur saisie), via `apps/dataimport`. Connecteur Odoo (`charger-odoo`) et chargement par systÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¨me externe partagent le mÃƒÆ’Ã‚Âªme pipeline.
- `POST lots/<id>/{analyser,charger,charger-odoo,reconcilier,deroger,terminer}`, `POST projets/<id>/terminer`, `GET projets/<id>/rapport` (PV PDF de reprise). ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â°crans `/migration` (liste) et assistant.
- **Garde-fous d'intÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©gritÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© :** `projet`/`entite` figÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©s aprÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¨s crÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©ation (un lot ne peut pas ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Âªtre rattachÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© au projet d'une AUTRE sociÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©tÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©) ; un projet clÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â´turÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© et un lot dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©jÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â  chargÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© sont indÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©lÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©biles (le PV client ne peut pas ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Âªtre effacÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©) ; une dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©rogation ne survit pas ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â  un rechargement ; l'`external_system` porte l'id de projet (un rollback ne peut pas supprimer les lignes d'un autre projet de la mÃƒÆ’Ã‚Âªme sociÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©tÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©).
- **Ordre + kits (NTMIG3/6-14/20/36/37).** `dependances.py` — graphe de dépendances entre entités (DAG, tri topologique) : `ordonner_lots(projet)`, `lot_precedent_bloquant`, `verifier_ordre_pret` (garde d'ordre de chargement). `kits/` (`odoo.py`/`sage.py`/`generique.py`) — mappings de colonnes prédéfinis par source×entité, fusionnés par `Kit`/`_registres()`. `transforms.py` — transformations de valeurs à la volée par mapping (`normaliser_telephone_ma`, `parser_montant_virgule`, `parser_date_multi_format`, `mapper_statut`, `appliquer_ligne`). `POST lots-migration/<id>/annuler/` (NTMIG6, rollback — jamais un hard-delete de repli). `GET kits/<source>/<entite>/gabarit/` (NTMIG20, CSV vide + exemple). `POST projets-migration/<id>/reconcilier-soldes/` (NTMIG37, lecture seule — écart de solde client source vs recalculé, sans conditionner chargement/clôture).
- `validation.py` + `stockage.py` (dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©pÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â´t objet MinIO des fichiers source) : **NTMIG32** `POST lots/<id>/valider-source/` valide le fichier source AVANT chargement (colonnes/types attendus). **NTMIG33** `POST lots/<id>/reprendre/` reprend un lot interrompu ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â  son offset (`offset_reprise`, migration 0003) sans rejouer les lignes dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©jÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â  traitÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©es. **NTMIG34** `GET projets/<id>/estimation/` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â estimation d'effort + checklist de points d'attention avant de lancer une reprise. **NTMIG38** `POST projets/<id>/migrer-a-blanc/` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â rÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©pÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©tition ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â  blanc (dry-run complet) sans ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©criture dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©finitive. `tasks.py` + beat `migration.purger_fichiers_migration` purge les fichiers source expirÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©s (migration 0002).
- **NTMIG-playbooks.** **PlaybookInstance** (`TenantModel`) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â instantanÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© FIGÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â° des ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©tapes d'un `kb.KbArticle` playbook au moment de l'instanciation (une ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©dition ultÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©rieure de l'article ne modifie pas une instance dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©jÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â  en cours). **DeploiementPartenaire** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â trace un dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©ploiement rÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©alisÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© par un partenaire certifiÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©, alimente `crm.Partenaire.nb_deploiements_reussis` via `crm.services.poser_compteur_deploiements` (jamais un `.update()` direct depuis cette app). `certification.py` (scoring de certification partenaire), `playbooks.py`, `manage.py seed_playbooks` (idempotent). Endpoints `playbook-instances/` (+ `instancier/`, `{id}/cocher/`, `{id}/terminer/`), `deploiements-partenaire/`, `certification/<partenaire_id>/score/`. **NTMIG24-31 (31/08)** : les playbooks voyagent dans le `ConfigPackage` adminops (clé `playbooks`, export/import/dry-run via `kb.selectors.playbooks_config_snapshot`/`kb.services.appliquer_playbook_config`) ; onglet « Playbook » interactif dans le détail d'article kb (`PlaybookPanel.jsx`) ; annuaire `GET annuaire-partenaires-certifies/` + écran `/admin/partenaires-certifies` (IsDirecteurOuAdmin) ; beat quotidien `migration.alerter_certifications_expirantes` (dédup par lien) ; **ParcoursCertificationPartenaire** (migration 0006) — parcours de certification d'un partenaire, spécialité PROPOSÉE à la clôture puis validée par un admin via `valider-specialite` (`crm.services.ajouter_specialite_partenaire`), jamais auto-attribuée.

### dataimport ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â CSV/XLSX import  *(`ImportJob`/`ImportJobRow`, `/api/django/imports/`)*
- `POST dry-run/` (preview + column mapping), `POST commit/`, `GET export/{entity}/`. Targets: leads, clients, products.
- **Garde d'ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©crasement (vague 2, 2026-07-31).** En mode `maj`/`upsert` l'import ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â°CRASAIT les champs dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©jÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â  remplis sans confirmation. DÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©sormais : `ecraser` dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©faut **False** (remplissage seul ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â un champ vide se remplit, un champ rempli est PRÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â°SERVÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â°) ; `dry-run/` renvoie `conflits` (par ligne : cible + `ecrasements[{champ, ancienne, nouvelle}]` + `remplissages`) donc l'utilisateur voit AVANT ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©criture ce qu'un fichier pÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©rimÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© remplacerait ; l'exÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©cution journalise la valeur PRÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â°CÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â°DENTE de chaque champ modifiÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© (`ImportJobRow.modifications`/`refuses`, + une ligne `AuditLog` par enregistrement via la primitive existante). Migration `dataimport/0004` additive.
- **ARC13** adds `parsing.py` `iter_rows(file_bytes, filename)` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a generic CSV/XLSX row reader (encoding detection utf-8/latin-1, `,`/`;` separator, normalized headers) that `services.parse_rows` now delegates to (zero behaviour change for the 6 historical FIELD_MAPS targets) and that any tabular-file reader outside `dataimport` can reuse instead of hand-rolling `csv.reader`/`openpyxl`; 3 pilot call sites migrated (rh/contrats views). **ARC32** makes `TARGETS` a lazy union of `FIELD_MAPS ÃƒÂ¢Ã‹â€ Ã‚Âª declared_import_specs()` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â the `import_specs` surface declared by `core.platform` manifests (6 owning apps: crm already declared, stock/sav/flotte/contrats/rh extended), bridged via `export_registry.declared_import_specs()`; a set-literal non-regression test asserts the 8 historical targets plus any newly-declared target.

### semantic — couche sémantique : métriques nommées et gouvernées  *(`MetricDefinition`, `/api/django/semantic/`)*
- **MetricDefinition / MetricDefinitionVersion** (`TenantModel`, `cle` unique par société) — NTDATA7: une métrique se définit UNE fois (`dataset` + `mesure` JSON: agrégat simple `{"field","agg"}`, formule `{"formula","aggregates":[...]}` évaluée par `core.formula` AST, ou adaptateur `{"adapter": "compta.dso"}` pour un calcul qui vit déjà dans une app métier — jamais réécrit en SQL). NTDATA8 `services.resolve_metric(company, user, cle, filters=, group_by=, period=)` traduit la définition en `core.data_explorer.run_query`; division par zéro / population vide / champ gated → `None`, jamais un zéro inventé. NTDATA11 `adapters.py` — registre en mémoire peuplé par les apps depuis leur `ready()` ; `semantic` n'importe aucune app métier.
- **NTDATA9 — versionnage.** `snapshot_metrique(definition)` fige `(dataset, mesure, filtres, unite)` dans `MetricDefinitionVersion`, numéro = dernier+1 SOUS VERROU (jamais `count()+1`), IDEMPOTENT par contenu. `GET semantic/metriques/<id>/versions/`.
- **NTDATA41/42 — série, variation, anomalie.** `selectors.serie_temporelle` dérive la trajectoire d'une métrique ; périodes vides OMISES (jamais 0). `selectors.variation_pct` = Δ% entre les 2 dernières périodes COMPLÈTES (`None` si < 2 points). NTDATA42 `tasks.detecter_anomalies_metriques` (beat hebdo) réutilise le scorer générique `core.anomaly.scan_for_outliers` (z-score, seuil 2,5, min 6 points) et écrit dans `core.AnomalyFlag` (table générique déjà existante, jamais une seconde).
- **NTDATA43 — lignage.** `selectors.lineage(company, cle, user=)` rend TOUT DÉRIVÉ : dataset source + app propriétaire, filtres réellement appliqués, champs lus, nombre de lignes agrégées (`None` — jamais 0 — si désenregistré/gated). `GET semantic/metriques/<cle>/lignage/`.
- **NTDATA34 — widget dashboard métrique.** `services.resolve_widget_metrique` est injecté par `semantic.apps.ready()` dans `core.dashboard_data.register_metric_resolver` (`core` ne peut pas importer `semantic`, contrat import-linter) ; widget de type `TYPE_METRIQUE` ; couche sémantique absente → widget en ERREUR NOMMÉE, jamais un tableau vide.
- Toutes les lectures bornées à `request.user.company`, réservées `IsResponsableOrAdmin`.

### dataquality — qualité de données : règles, complétude, dédoublonnage, golden records  *(`RegleQualite`, `/api/django/dataquality/`)*
- **RegleQualite / ResultatQualite** (`TenantModel`) — NTDATA14: règles company-scopées sur un dataset+champ enregistré, types `non_vide`/`format`(regex)/`plage`/`unicite`/`reference_valide`, sévérité `info`/`avertissement`/`bloquant` (MESURE seulement, ne bloque aucune écriture). NTDATA15 `ResultatQualite` = un enregistrement PAR ÉVALUATION (jamais un écrasement), `taux_conformite` NULLABLE, échantillon plafonné à 50 ids. `GET dataquality/rapport/` (`?entite=`, `?evaluer=1`) + beat quotidien 05:45.
- **NTDATA16/21 — complétude & santé.** `selectors.completude_module`/`completude_entite` — score de champs CRITIQUES renseignés par entité métier. `GET dataquality/sante/` (NTDATA21) — 3 indicateurs réels (complétude globale, règles bloquantes en violation, doublons en attente) + `disponible=false` (aucune règle configurée) → carte masquée plutôt que trois zéros trompeurs.
- **NTDATA17/18/19/20 — dédoublonnage + fusion supervisée.** `dedoublonnage.grouper_doublons` détecte des groupes candidats (LECTURE SEULE). `services.fusionner_clients/fournisseurs/produits` NEUTRALISE (jamais supprime) les doublons et repointe tout ce qui les référençait. `GET dataquality/doublons/<entite>/` + `POST` pour fusionner. **NTDATA20 — PropositionFusion** — file de revue humaine (`statut` en_attente/fusionne/ignore, DÉFINITIFS), empreinte = identifiants triés du groupe ; `POST fusions/scanner/` (repeuple), `POST fusions/<id>/ignorer|fusionner/`.
- **NTDATA22/23/24/26 — golden record + survivorship.** `GoldenRecord` (`TenantModel`, unique `company+entite+cle_metier`) — couche de LECTURE consolidée (jamais de mutation des sources). `RegleSurvivorship` — stratégie gagnante par (entité, champ) : `plus_recent`/`plus_complet`/`source_prioritaire`/`plus_frequent` ; une valeur VIDE ne gagne jamais contre une valeur renseignée. Le NOM n'est jamais une clé métier (ice/téléphone/email/sku le sont). `services.consolider_golden` (`golden-records/consolider/?entite=`) + beat hebdomadaire. `GoldenRecordViewSet` lecture seule.
- Toutes les vues réservées `IsResponsableOrAdmin`, bornées à `request.user.company`.

### contact ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â public contact form  *(no models, `/api/django/contact/`)*
- `POST /` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â landing-page contact form; **parked by default** (returns 404 unless `CONTACT_FORM_ENABLED=1`).

### monitoring ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â production supervision (N50/N51/N52)  *( `/api/django/monitoring/` )*
- Models: `MonitoringConfig` (per installed-system provider + credentials, enabled), `ProductionReading` (manual/auto yield), `UnderperformanceFlag`, per-company settings (threshold % + auto-ticket toggle, default OFF).
- Swappable provider interface (registry + `NoOpProvider` default + `FusionSolarProvider` skeleton that no-ops without credentials; no new dependency).
- `configs/` (+ `providers/`, `{id}/sync-now/`), `readings/` (list + manual entry), `settings/`. Under-performance auto-creates an idempotent SAV ticket when enabled.
- **Groupe NTNRG.** **SlaDisponibilite** (NTNRG14) + **CertificatCarbone** (NTNRG27) models (migrations monitoring/0005-0006). **NTNRG32** adds `ProductionReading.motif_limitation` (curtailment reason). Endpoints `configs/benchmark/` (NTNRG33, classement RELATIF du parc par PR — percentile, jamais un seuil absolu), `configs/{id}/rapport-garantie-pdf/` (NTNRG11, rapport contractuel mensuel de garantie de performance), `configs/{id}/attestation-carbone-pdf/` + `configs/attestation-carbone-client-pdf/` (attestation CO2 imprimable).

- **Dossiers transverses (NTWFL17-22/29, 20/09/2026)** — `core.Dossier` (type libre par société + statut + échéance + propriétaire) avec `DossierLien` (cible générique contenttype+object_id — jamais une FK métier), `DossierChecklistItem`, `DossierActivity` (chatter dédié + rappel d'échéance dédupliqué `dernier_rappel_echeance_le`, événement `dossier_echeance_depassee` sur le bus), lien `workflow_instance` (NTWFL20) et **DossierModele** instanciable (NTWFL29, copie checklist + démarrage workflow configuré). Vues dans `core/views_dossiers.py` (routes `core/urls_dossiers.py`, montées par include), écrans frontend liste/détail/kanban/vues sauvegardées.

### statuspage — page de statut publique multi-région (Groupe NTOBS, new app)  *( `/api/django/statuspage/` )*
Modèles PUBLICS, majoritairement SYSTÈME (`company` nullable — la quasi-totalité des lignes ont `company=None`, visibles de tous les tenants). Statuts des composants dérivés de `core.health.check_services()` par un job Celery beat toutes les 5 min (`tasks.py`) — jamais un appel synchrone côté public. Texte narratif des incidents (titre/updates/post-mortem) TOUJOURS rédigé par un humain, jamais généré depuis `core.health` (règle checked-facts-only).
- **ComponentStatus** (**NTOBS1**) — `nom`, `region` (libre, ex. « EU-West/Hetzner »), `statut` (`operational`/`degraded`/`partial_outage`/`major_outage`), `derniere_verification`. **IncidentPublic** — `titre`, `composants` (M2M), `severite` (`mineure`/`majeure`/`critique`), `statut` (`investigating`/`identified`/`monitoring`/`resolved`), `region`, `debute_le`/`resolu_le`, plus **NTOBS2** `postmortem_markdown` + `postmortem_publie_le` (publication séparée de la clôture, réservée Directeur/Administrateur via `publier-postmortem/`, uniquement sur un incident `resolved`). **IncidentUpdate** — mise à jour horodatée liée à un incident (`statut`, `message`, `horodatage`).
- **UptimeDayBucket** (**NTOBS14**) — agrégat quotidien PRÉ-CALCULÉ par composant/région (`statut_pire_du_jour`, `echantillons_total`/`echantillons_operationnels`, `pct_disponible_jour` mesuré par échantillonnage, jamais recalculé depuis des logs bruts), peuplé incrémentalement par le même beat 5 min ; alimente la frise 90 jours.
- **StatusSubscriber** (**NTOBS15**) — abonnement public par e-mail (double opt-in : `confirme=False` jusqu'au clic sur le lien envoyé), `region_filtre`, `token_desabonnement` unique servant AUX DEUX usages (confirmer et se désabonner).
- Endpoints public (AllowAny, throttled 60/min lecture ou 5/h écriture) — `public/` (statut agrégé, cache mémoire 60 s), `public/incidents/` (historique 90 jours), `public/incidents/<pk>/` (détail + post-mortem si publié), `public/uptime-90j/` (frise pré-calculée), `public/abonner/`, `public/confirmer/<token>/`, `public/desabonner/<token>/`. Interne (`IsAuthenticated` + Directeur/Administrateur) — `incidents/<pk>/publier-postmortem/`.

### mlops — versions de modèles de scoring + paramètres par société (NTAI27/28/30, new app 20/09/2026)  *( `/api/django/mlops/` )*
Petite app Technique (`installable=False`) : elle ne PRÉDIT rien elle-même — elle gouverne les scorers heuristiques de `core/` (win_probability, churn_risk, payment_delay, stock_reorder, anomaly).
- **ModeleML** — versions nommées par scorer (une seule version `actif` par (company, scorer), contrainte base) avec `params_json` ; `selectors.params_actifs(company, nom)` (repli défauts du scorer) est enregistré au boot comme resolver de `core.score_params` (`apps.py ready()`), donc chaque scorer core lit ses seuils PAR SOCIÉTÉ sans que core importe mlops. **FeatureVector** (NTAI30) — matérialisation quotidienne (beat `mlops.recompute_features`, queue scheduled) de features par lead (canal/priorité/issue/ancienneté).
- `backtest.py` (NTAI28) — rejoue `core.win_probability` sur les leads signés/perdus (précision/rappel/AUC, implémentation pure) ; `retard_paiement` s'abstient (`disponible=False`) tant qu'aucune date de paiement réelle n'est accessible depuis ce périmètre.
- Endpoints : `modeles/` (+ `activer/`), `backtests/`.

### notifications ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â unified notification engine (N75)  *( `/api/django/notifications/` )*
- **Canaux MRY (06/09/2026, migrations notifications 0050-0052)** — activité WHATSAPP typée par touche de cadence, `lead_new` email par défaut, digest/escalade premier-contact en minutes ouvrées.
- Models: `Notification` (company + recipient-scoped), `NotificationPreference` (per userÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬â€event channel toggles in_app/whatsapp/email). Service `notify()` is best-effort, respects preferences, reuses existing channels (no-op when unconfigured).
- `notifications/` (+ `unread-count/`, `{id}/read/`, `read-all/`), `preferences/`. In-app bell in the header + `/parametres/notifications`.
- **MessageAccueil (MSGACC, fondateur 15/09/2026 — migrations notifications 0058-0059)** : un message posé pour UN employé, affiché EN PLEIN ÉCRAN à la première ouverture de l'ERP à partir d'une date+heure choisies — **PAS une notification** (aucun EventType, aucune ligne du centre, aucun push). Modèle `company`/`destinataire`/`auteur SET_NULL`/`visible_a_partir_de` (db_index)/`corps`/`lu_le`. Endpoints `messages-accueil/` : `a-lire/` (les miens, échus, non lus), `{id}/lu/` (destinataire seul, idempotent), create (tier responsable/admin, erreurs par champ), liste des envoyés + DELETE si non lu. Data migration 0059 : le message « la visite entre dans le suivi commercial » seedé pour DEMAIN 07:00 Casablanca aux utilisateurs actifs qui font la relance CRM (admins ∪ owners de leads, sociétés non-démo, modèles historiques, idempotent, réversible par marqueur corps-exact) — inclut le lien `/ged` vers le guide. Frontend : `MessageAccueilModal` monté dans `main.jsx` hors router (un fetch par chargement, chemins internes cliquables via `router.navigate`), page d'envoi `/parametres/messages-accueil` (responsable/admin, date+heure, `MicDicteeButton`) ; composant partagé `components/MicDicteeButton.jsx` (dictée webkitSpeechRecognition fr-FR, feature-detect, aussi câblé sur la note de `PlanifierVisiteModal` et le conseil de closing du wizard visite).
- **ARC35** registers `EventType.CONTRAT_SIGNE` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â consumes the `contrat_signe` seam on the `core.events` bus, notifying the contract's creator (fallback managers) when a contract becomes fully signed; `contrats/receivers.py` additionally logs an ARC8 chatter note + a GED deposit on the same transition (best-effort, isolated failures logged separately per hook). **ARC37** makes `sav` and `gestion_projet` bus EMITTERS (not just consumers): `sav.services.emettre_changement_statut_ticket` fires `ticket_resolu`/`equipement_remplace` (ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ `EventType.SAV_TICKET_RESOLU`/`SAV_EQUIPEMENT_REMPLACE`), and a project statut change fires `projet_status_change` (ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ `PROJET_STATUT_CHANGE`). **ARC38** repatriates `qhse`'s `incident_declared` onto the `core.events` bus (double emission preserved for the existing local publicapi consumer, documented choice) via `qhse/receivers.py`. **ARC39** closes 3 previously-invisible notification gaps: `MONITORING_RAPPORT` (the periodic O&M report send, `monitoring/report.py`, stays a direct `EmailMessage` to the client but now ALSO notifies internal managers via `notifications.services`) and `PAIE_RIB_DIVERGENCE` (the ARC25 RIB-mismatch check already called `notify_many(...)` with this key ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â it was simply never a registered `EventType`, so it silently dropped; now persisted, zero caller behaviour change) + `PAIE_RUN_PRET` (a payroll run reaching `validee` now notifies payroll managers it is ready for the transfer order/closure).
- **CHT9** disentangles `CHANTIER_DUE` into 5 dedicated EventTypes — `INTERVENTION_ASSIGNEE`/`_REPLANIFIEE`/`_ANNULEE`, `TRANCHE_A_FACTURER`, `CHANTIER_MATERIEL_CONFIRME` — one business fact per key/preference (muting `CHANTIER_DUE` no longer silently mutes the other three); `installations.views.intervention` notifies the assigned technician on create AND reassignment via `INTERVENTION_ASSIGNEE`/`_REPLANIFIEE`.

### automation ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â no-code rules engine (N72/N73)  *( `/api/django/automation/` )*
- Models: `AutomationRule` (trigger + action config), `AutomationRun` (every run logged), `AutomationApproval` (owner-tier approval step). Fires on the app's own `post_save` signals, best-effort (never breaks the originating save); opt-in.
- `rules/` (+ `{id}/toggle/`), `runs/`, `approvals/` (+ `approve/`, `reject/`). ParamÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¨tres ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â« Automatisations Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â».
- **ARC34** adds a generic `RECORD_STATE_CHANGE` trigger type ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a registry-driven `(model, field)` whitelist read from each app's `platform.py` `automation_state_fields` manifest entry (`record_state_change_targets()`), with French validation at rule-creation time (serializer) and conditions evaluated via `core/rules.py` (FG367, never a second evaluator). 2 pilots emit through their app's `services.py` (`contrats.changer_statut` wrapper, `sav.emettre_changement_statut_ticket` at the guarded transition site) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â never a direct model-signal shortcut.
- **NTEXT6** adds `list_sources.py` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a CLOSED registry of list sources for a FOR_EACH loop step (never an arbitrary queryset). **NTEXT7** adds `AutomationScheduledStep` (a frozen context + resume rank + statut, `en_attente/traitee/echouee`) for a WAIT step, resumed by the `automation.process_due_automation_steps` beat task (every 5 min, `beat_tasks.py`). **NTEXT31** adds `AutomationRun.SIMULATION` statut + `rules/{id}/simuler/` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a dry-run (no side effect) evaluation of a rule.
- **NTEXT5** adds `si`/`sinon` branches: two `AutomationStep`s at the same `ordre` with `branche=SI`/`SINON` are mutually exclusive (`engine._resolve_step_skips` — any true `si` condition wins, else `sinon`); per-step `condition` (core.rules tree) still applies independently. **NTEXT8** adds `ActionType.SERVER_ACTION` (`action_config={'expressions':[{'field','value'}]}`) — each expression run through the same `core.formula` AST-safe engine against a read-only context (relations + prix_achat/marge/cout_horaire/company excluded), writable only through the existing `SET_FIELD_TARGETS`/`set_field_autorise` whitelist (AUD821).
- **NTEXT30** adds `AutomationRuleVersion` (rule, version, snapshot, auteur) — an automatic snapshot taken just before each rule edit; read-only `rule-versions/` (`?rule=<id>`) + `rule-versions/<id>/restaurer/` recreates that exact prior config on the live rule.
- **NTEXT27** adds `TriggerType.CUSTOM_RECORD_SAVED` — fires on `customfields.CustomRecord` create/update (`trigger_config={'object_code': 'x'}`, empty = any custom object), via a company-scoped `post_save` signal.

### publicapi ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â public REST API + webhooks (N89)  *( `/api/public/` data, `/api/django/publicapi/` management )*
- Models: `ApiKey` (hashed, scoped), `Webhook`, `WebhookDelivery`. `Api-Key` auth + per-key DRF throttle; read-only company-scoped `leads/devis/factures/chantiers` (never buy prices); HMAC-SHA256-signed webhooks on lead.created / devis.accepted / chantier.completed / facture.paid (httpx, best-effort). ParamÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¨tres ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â« API & Webhooks Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â». NTADM41/42 (2026-08-05): webhook events `plan.changed` + `sieges.quota_atteint` (never client data) and `GET /api/public/v1/licence/statut/` (scope `read:licence`, exactly plan_code/modules_inclus/sieges_max/sieges_utilises).
- **Dépréciation + rotation de clé (NTAPI2/5/23).** `deprecation.py`'s `ApiDeprecationHeadersMixin` posts the three RFC 8594 headers (`Deprecation: true` / `Sunset: <IMF-fixdate>` / `Link: …; rel="deprecation"`) whenever `core.api_deprecation.deprecation_pour()` (a foundation table, `core.ApiDeprecation`) matches the called path. `ApiKey.api_version` (NTAPI5, default `'v1'`) pins the version SERVED per key (`X-Taqinor-Api-Version` header, always posted). `ApiKey.rotate()`/`POST keys/<id>/rotate/` (NTAPI23) issues a replacement key and sets `expire_le` (+7 days default) on the old one — it keeps working with its own `Deprecation` header until then.
- **Error catalogue (NTAPI3/4).** `errors.py` gives `/api/public/v1/` a Stripe-like error envelope (`{"error": {"type","code","message","param","doc_url","request_id"}}`). `error_catalog.py` derives `GET /api/public/v1/errors/` directly from the handler's own code table — never a hand-duplicated list — and every error's `doc_url` points to its catalogue anchor.
- **Quotas & usage plans (NTAPI6/7/22).** `ApiKeyRateThrottle` enforces two independent bounds sourced from `core.ApiUsagePlan` (FG398) — per-minute rate/burst plus day/month VOLUME — and stamps `X-RateLimit-Limit/Remaining/Reset` (+`Retry-After` on 429) on every response; no plan for the company = historical no-op, zero headers. `ApiUsagePlanView` (`GET/PATCH /api/django/publicapi/plan/`, NTAPI7) surfaces `usage_jour`/`usage_mois` (NTAPI22) for the developer-portal quota display.
- **Webhook reliability (NTAPI8/9/10/11/12).** `retry.py` schedules long-tail retries at 1m/5m/30m/2h/6h (max 6). `delivery.py` adds a Stripe-style V2 signature header (`t=<epoch>,v1=<hex>`, NTAPI9) alongside the legacy format, plus an `Idempotency-Key` (= `event_id`, NTAPI10) shared by the original send and every retry. `webhook_health.py` (NTAPI11) auto-disables a webhook after N CONSECUTIVE failures in a sliding window (default 20/24h, one success resets the count) — reactivation is manual-only, `POST webhooks/<id>/reactiver/`. `webhook_filters.py` (NTAPI12) adds a per-event condition on `Webhook.filtres` (same `core.rules` engine as FG367), fail-open on a malformed filter.
- **Flux d'évènements par curseur (NTAPI17).** `events_feed.py` + `GET /api/public/v1/events/?after=<sequence>&limit=<n>` is the pull counterpart of webhooks; `sequence` is a dense per-company counter assigned highest-used+1 in a retry-on-race savepoint. The `read:events` scope only opens the channel — each event is still filtered by its family's own read scope.
- **Écritures brouillon devis/tickets (NTAPI18).** `POST /api/public/v1/devis-write/` (scope `devis:write`) creates only a `brouillon` Devis on an existing lead via `ventes.services.create_draft_devis_from_ocr` — never a line item, never a downstream status change (rule #4 preserved). `POST /api/public/v1/tickets-write/` (scope `tickets:write`) opens a corrective SAV ticket via `sav.services.create_corrective_ticket`.
- **OAuth2 client_credentials → ApiKey compagnon (NTAPI19).** `POST /api/public/v1/oauth/token/` exchanges an `OAuthClient`'s `client_id`/`client_secret` for a short JWT (HS256, default 1h TTL). `OAuthClient.issue()` mints a COMPANION `ApiKey` with the same scopes, so scope checks/quota/`X-RateLimit-*`/version/deprecation headers all work unmodified; effective scopes are the intersection of the token's granted scopes and the companion key's CURRENT scopes.
- **Discovery docs (NTAPI20/21/24).** `openapi.py` (NTAPI20) hand-builds the served OpenAPI 3.1 `openapi.json` document (no new dependency). `SandboxTryView` (NTAPI21) lets an admin "try it" from the docs console, authenticated by session against the company's own NTAPI27 sandbox. `public_changelog_views.py` (NTAPI24) serves `GET /api/public/v1/changelog/` off `core.ChangelogEntry`.
- **Bac à sable test/live (NTAPI26/27).** `ApiKey.environnement` (`'test'`/`'live'`, NTAPI26) picks a distinct key prefix (`tqk_test_…`/`tqk_live_…`); a `test` key is issued on a `SandboxTenant.sandbox_company` — an isolated twin company (NTAPI27), so isolation comes from ordinary `company_id` scoping. `POST /api/public/v1/sandbox/reset/` wipes and reseeds 3 demo leads, reserved to `environnement='test'` keys.
- **SDK Python généré (NTAPI28).** `manage.py gen_sdk_python --dossier docs/sdk/python` emits a dependency-free (stdlib-only) Python client DERIVED from `openapi.build_openapi_schema()` — never a hand-maintained endpoint list. No PyPI publish; nothing is written to disk until the command runs.
- **Partenaires EDI (NTAPI36).** `PartenaireEdi` registers a partner by GLN/DUNS with a `mapping_sku` dict. `edi_partners.py` translates both directions; an unmapped SKU is never blocking (passes through raw + a warning). Registry stays INERT — no message is generated/transmitted yet (the INVOIC/ORDERS/X12 generators are not built).
- **Favoris & vues sauvegardées en lecture seule (NTUX33).** `public_uxviews_views.py` adds `GET /api/public/v1/favoris/` (scope `read:favoris`) and `GET /api/public/v1/saved-views/` (scope `read:vues`), reusing `apps.uxviews.FavoriUtilisateur`/`SavedView` directly. `?owner=<id>` stands in for user consent: `favoris/` REFUSES with 400 without it (a favorite is always strictly personal); `saved-views/` defaults to team-shared views only, requiring `?owner=<id>` to see anyone's personal ones.
- **NTWFL3 — délégation de vacances branchée au moteur BPM.** `ApprovalDelegation.delegants_actifs_pour` (réutilise ce modèle existant XKB3, aucune nouvelle table) est enregistrée dans `core.workflow` via `register_delegation_resolver` depuis `AutomationConfig.ready()` — `core` ne connaît que le registre, jamais `apps.automation`.

### publicapi ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â public REST API + webhooks (N89)  *( `/api/public/` data, `/api/django/publicapi/` management )*
- Models: `ApiKey` (hashed, scoped), `Webhook`, `WebhookDelivery`. `Api-Key` auth + per-key DRF throttle; read-only company-scoped `leads/devis/factures/chantiers` (never buy prices); HMAC-SHA256-signed webhooks on lead.created / devis.accepted / chantier.completed / facture.paid (httpx, best-effort). ParamÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¨tres ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â« API & Webhooks Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â». NTADM41/42 (2026-08-05): webhook events `plan.changed` + `sieges.quota_atteint` (never client data) and `GET /api/public/v1/licence/statut/` (scope `read:licence`, exactly plan_code/modules_inclus/sieges_max/sieges_utilises).
- **NTAPI1 — racine versionnée `/api/public/v1/`.** Toutes les routes de données sont désormais montées sous le préfixe de version (`versioning.py` : version demandée = chemin, puis en-tête `X-Taqinor-Api-Version`, puis défaut) ; la racine historique SANS version (`/api/public/...`) reste servie 12 mois par `legacy_urls.py` (301 sur GET/HEAD, 308 sur écriture pour ne jamais dégrader un POST en GET, en-têtes `Deprecation`/`Sunset`) avant de disparaître.

### agent ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â agentic action catalogue (Group R, AG1)  *( `/api/django/agent/` )*
- No DB model ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â actions are declared in code via `apps/agent/registry.py` (`AgentAction`: key/label/description/inputs-schema/endpoint/method/required_permission/riskÃƒÂ¢Ã‹â€ Ã‹â€ internalÃƒâ€šÃ‚Â·outwardÃƒâ€šÃ‚Â·irreversible/confirm_summary). `GET actions/` returns the per-caller, company+permission-filtered catalogue (cross-tenant leakage tested). Domain apps register their actions in `ready()` (ventes/crm/stock/sav/installations `agent_actions.py`, AG4ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Å“AG9). Execution stays the JWT-relay pattern (Django re-checks permission+company); outward/irreversible actions go through the FastAPI proposeÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢confirm protocol.

### chat ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â internal team messaging Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â« Discuss Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â» (Group S)  *( `/api/django/chat/` )*
- Models: `Conversation` (dm/channel), `ConversationMember` (role/last_read_at/is_muted), `Message` (text/voice/system/record kinds, soft-delete, pin, reply_to), `MessageAttachment` (image/file/voice + transcript fields), `MessageReaction`, `MessageMention`, + generic shared-record link. Company **and** membership scoped everywhere (non-member 403, cross-tenant 404; company forced server-side). Endpoints: conversations (list/create/archive/read/unread/search/mute/members/leave), messages (`list?conversation=`/create/edit/delete/upload/react/pin/unpin/attachments-download/share-record via selectors). Notifications reuse `notify()` (CHAT_MESSAGE/CHAT_MENTION, mute-aware). Voice memos transcribed by a Celery task ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ FastAPI faster-whisper (S10/S11), flag `CHAT_TRANSCRIPTION_ENABLED`; v1 real-time is polling (WebSocket upgrade S21 is gated on provisioned infra). **NTMOB30** adds `views_transcription.py` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `POST chat/transcrire/` (multipart `file`), a SYNCHRONOUS generic wrapper around the same S10/S11 Whisper call (never a second client) for dictating a field note straight onto a lead/ticket chatter, no attachment stored; disabled ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ `{enabled: False}`, frontend falls back to keyboard.

### compta ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â Moroccan accounting (CGNC): chart, journals, ledger, statements  *( `/api/django/compta/` )*
- Double-entry bookkeeping on the CGNC plan comptable: journaux, **EcritureComptable**/**LigneEcriture** (grand livre), balance/CPC/bilan statements, lettrage. All `company`-scoped.
- **ExerciceComptable** (fiscal year) + **PeriodeComptable** (lockable month/period via `date_verrouillee`) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `services.cloturer_periode`/`rouvrir_periode` lock/unlock. Once a period is locked, `EcritureComptable`/`LigneEcriture` `save()/delete()` raise `ValidationError` (immutability), and `services.verifier_facture_modifiable` is a value-only guard ventes can call (no cross-app model import). **OD manuelles** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `services.creer_ecriture_od` posts a balanced entry with no source document, refused when the period is locked. **ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â€šÂ¬-nouveaux** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `cloturer_exercice` + `reporter_a_nouveaux` carry class 1ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Å“5 balance-sheet balances into the new exercise as one balanced opening entry (idempotent via `an_reporte`). Endpoints: `periodes/{id}/cloturer|rouvrir`, `exercices/ecriture-od`, `exercices/{id}/reporter-a-nouveaux`.
- **FG118 ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â Immobilisation** (fixed-asset register): `company` FK, `libelle`, `categorie` (vehicule/outillage/materiel/mobilier/informatique/autre), `cout` HT, `taux_tva`, `date_acquisition`, `actif`; read-only `montant_tva`/`cout_ttc` props. Company-scoped ViewSet `immobilisations/` (category filter + search).
- **FG119 ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â Amortissement**: **PlanAmortissement** (OneToOneÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢Immobilisation; `mode` lineaire/degressif, `duree_annees`, `base_amortissable`, frozen Moroccan CGI `coefficient_degressif`) + **DotationAmortissement** (per-year `montant`/`cumul`/`valeur_nette`, `posted`, FK `ecriture`). `services.generer_plan_amortissement` (idempotent; degressive switches to straight-line-of-residual) and `services.poster_dotation` (balanced ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©criture debit class-6 / credit class-28 ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â **respects the period lock**). Actions `immobilisations/{id}/plan-amortissement`, `dotations/{id}/poster`.
- **FG120 ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â Cession/rebut**: **CessionImmobilisation** (`type_cession` vente/rebut, `prix_cession`, computed `valeur_nette_comptable` = cost ÃƒÂ¢Ã‹â€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ cumulated FG119 amortization, signed `resultat_cession` plus/moins-value, `posted` + FK `ecriture`). `services.poster_cession` posts the balanced disposal ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©criture (reprise amortissements + sortie class-2 + rÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©sultat 6513/7513 + 3481 on sale) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â **respects the period lock** and marks the asset inactive. Actions `immobilisations/{id}/ceder`, `cessions/{id}/poster`.
- **ODX15 (2026-07 ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â moved OUT to `apps.frais`).** FG135/FG136/ZACC6/XACC27/XACC28 ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `NoteFrais`, `RapportNoteFrais`, `PlafondNoteFrais`, `BaremeIndemnite`, `IndemniteChantier` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â no longer live here: a state-only split (`SeparateDatabaseAndState`, `db_table` frozen on the historical `compta_*` names, no data moved) relocated the model CODE to `apps/frais` with a re-export shim left in `apps/compta/models.py` for existing importers. Both `/api/django/frais/ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚Â¦` (canonical) and the legacy `/api/django/compta/notes-frais|rapports-notes-frais|plafonds-notes-frais|baremes-indemnite|indemnites-chantier/ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚Â¦` (unchanged) serve the same ViewSets. Full detail moved to Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â§4 **frais**.
- **FG137 ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â DeclarationTVA** (prÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©paration de la dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©claration de TVA): `selectors.preparer_declaration_tva` aggregates from the GL over a period ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â TVA collectÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©e (4455/44552, crÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©ditÃƒÂ¢Ã‹â€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©bit) ÃƒÂ¢Ã‹â€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©ductible (3455/34552, dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©bitÃƒÂ¢Ã‹â€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢crÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©dit) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ `tva_a_declarer` = max(0, collectÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©eÃƒÂ¢Ã‹â€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©ductibleÃƒÂ¢Ã‹â€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢crÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©dit antÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©rieur) with the excess as `credit_reportable`; carries `regime` (mensuel/trimestriel) + `methode` (dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©bit/encaissement). `services.preparer_declaration_tva` freezes a `DeclarationTVA` snapshot (reference `TVA-` race-safe). ViewSet `declarations-tva/` (`preparer` action derives amounts from the GL ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â body can't impose them ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â + CSV `export`), Admin/Responsable. **FG138** adds `selectors.releve_deductions_tva` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â the DGI line-by-line deductible-VAT annex (one row per piÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¨ce: date/rÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©f/journal/tiers/base HT/TVA/taux, reconciles 1:1 with FG137's `tva_deductible`) at `etats/releve-deductions-tva/` (JSON or `?export=csv`, role-gated). No model ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â reuses the GL. **FG139** adds **RetenueSource** (Moroccan withholding tax / retenue ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â  la source on fees: `taux` ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬â€ `base` = `montant`, per piÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¨ce/tiers, ref `RAS-` race-safe) + `selectors.bordereau_versement_ras` (totals per prestataire + `total_a_verser`). No model ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â reuses the CPC. **FG141** adds `selectors.export_fec` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â the DGI FEC (fichier des ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©critures comptables): the 18 standard columns, one ordered row per LigneEcriture (dateÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢piÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¨ceÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢entry order), exercice-bounded, balance-verified, at `etats/export-fec/?exercice=` (JSON / `?export=fec` tab-delimited / `?export=csv`). No model. **FG142** adds `selectors.liasse_fiscale` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â the trousse liasse fiscale: assembles bilan + CPC + balance + the FG138 TVA annexe into one package (**reuses the standalone selectors, no recompute**), at `etats/liasse-fiscale/?exercice=` (JSON / `?export=csv` multi-section). No model. **FG143** adds `selectors.declaration_honoraires(company, annee)` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â the DGI annual ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©tat 9421 (fees paid to third parties), aggregated per-bÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©nÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©ficiaire from the FG139 RAS ledger (brut/retenue/net + IF/ICE + nb piÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¨ces), at `etats/declaration-honoraires/?annee=` (JSON / `?export=csv`, role-gated). ViewSet `timbres-fiscaux/` (+ `verser`, `?export=csv`). ViewSets `retenues-garantie/`, `cautions-bancaires/`. Migration compta/0121. IR SIMPL export (NTMAR13) is `[BLOCKED]` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â needs a paie IR-by-period selector (foreign app).
- **ARC19** adds a nullable `Partenaire.tiers` FK (migration compta/0108, `tiers_bridge.py`) mirroring identity one-way into `tiers.Tiers`. **ARC28** adds `apps/compta/platform.py` (`kpi_providers`: ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©chÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©ances d'effets 30j/dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©passÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©es, consumed by ARC40's federated KPI endpoint; `agent_actions_module` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â read-only effets/ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©chÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©ances actions).
- **NTFIN ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â consolidation grand groupe & multi-rÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©fÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©rentiel/analytique multi-axes** (migrations compta/0114ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Å“0115, all `TenantModel` + `_ComptaBaseViewSet`). Consolidation: **CycleConsolidation** (dated group cycle, statut ouvertÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢collecteÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢eliminationsÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢validÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢publiÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© + `verrouille` gate refusing aggregate mutation) with `EntiteConsolidation.cycle` FK; **LiasseRemontee** (per-entity balance snapshot, `services.collecter_balance_entite` idempotent via `balance_generale`, head-company collected too); `selectors.controles_collecte` (NTFIN3 blocking/warning anomalies); **MappingConsolidation** (localÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢group account, longest-prefix); `services.convertir_entite` (NTFIN5 closing/average-rate translation + CTA plug); **OperationInterco** + `apparier_intercos` (NTFIN6 reciprocal matching w/ cycle tolerance); **EcritureElimination** + `generer_eliminations_reciproques` (NTFIN7), `eliminer_marge_interne` (NTFIN8 unrealized stock margin + deferred tax), **EliminationTitres**/`eliminer_titres` (NTFIN9 goodwill), `calculer_interets_minoritaires` (NTFIN10); `selectors.bilan_consolide`/`cpc_consolide_v2` (NTFIN11, distinct from FG153) + `moniteur_consolidation` (NTFIN12). Endpoints under `cycles-consolidation/{id}/` (`ouvrir|verrouiller|collecter|controles-collecte|intercos|apparier|eliminations|generer-reciproques|interets-minoritaires|etats-consolides|moniteur`) + `liasses-remontee/`, `mappings-consolidation/`, `operations-interco/`, `marges-internes-stock/{id}/eliminer`, `eliminations-titres/{id}/eliminer`. Multi-rÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©fÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©rentiel: **ReferentielComptable** (CGNC/IFRS/GROUPE/FISCAL parallel books, one principal per company via `services.seed_referentiel_principal`) + `LigneEcriture.referentiel` (NULL = principal, `creer_ecriture(referentiel=)`) + `selectors.balance_par_referentiel`; **AjustementGaap**/`services.poster_ajustement_gaap` (GAAP delta posted only in the target book). Analytique multi-axes: **AxeAnalytique** (configurable dimensions) + `CentreCout.axe_ref`, **ImputationAxe** (one line ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ many axes) + `selectors.balance_analytique` (pivot axeÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬â€value) / `resultat_par_axe`. Endpoints `referentiels-comptables/` (+ `seed`), `ajustements-gaap/` (+ `poster`), `axes-analytiques/`, `imputations-axes/`, and `etats/balance-referentiel|balance-analytique|resultat-analytique/`.
- **NTFIN20ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Å“56 ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â allocations, engagement, close management, rapprochements, immos avancÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©es, IFRS 15 & ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©tats consolidÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©s** (migrations compta/0116ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Å“0120, all `TenantModel` + `_ComptaBaseViewSet`). Allocations/engagement: **CleRepartition**/**LigneCleRepartition** (bases d'allocation, `valider_cle_repartition` ÃƒÅ½Ã‚Â£=100 %), **RunAllocation**/`executer_allocation`+`reverser_allocation` (dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©versement rÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©versible), **AllocationRecurrente**/`generer_allocations_recurrentes` + cmd `generer_allocations_dues` (idempotent), **EngagementComptable** `engager`/`liquider` (encumbrance rÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©siduel) + `selectors.disponible_budgetaire`/`execution_budgetaire` + `verifier_disponible_engagement` (budgetÃƒÂ¢Ã‹â€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢engagÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©ÃƒÂ¢Ã‹â€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢rÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©alisÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©, blocage). Close management: **ModeleCloture**/**TacheClotureModele** (`seed_modele_cloture_mensuel`), **InstanceCloture**/**TacheCloture** (`instancier_cloture`/`cocher_tache_cloture`) + `pret_a_cloturer`, **AccrualCloture**/`poster_accrual` (OD + extourne J+1), **JustificationVariation** + `analyse_variation` (N vs N-1), `anomalies_ecritures`, `cockpit_cloture`, `ModeleEcriture.cloture`/`generer_od_cloture`. Rapprochements de bilan: **RapprochementCompte**/**LigneJustificationCompte** + `soumettre_/valider_/rejeter_rapprochement_compte` (revue 4 yeux, prÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©parateurÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â°Ã‚Â rÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©viseur), `ouvrir_rapprochement_compte` (report permanentes N-1), `rapprochements_en_retard`. Immos avancÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©es: **ComposantImmobilisation** (IAS 16), **DepreciationImmobilisation** `poster_depreciation_immobilisation`/`reprendre` (IAS 36), **MutationImmobilisation** `muter_immobilisation`, **ImmobilisationEnCours**/**LigneImmobilisationEnCours** `mettre_en_service_encours` (CIP), `registre_immobilisations` (multi-rÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©fÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©rentiel) / `projection_dotations`. IFRS 15: **ContratRevenu**/**ObligationPerformance** `allouer_prix_transaction` (prorata PVS), **EcheancierReconnaissance** `generer_echeancier_reconnaissance`/`reconnaitre_echeance` (solde PCA 487), `positions_contrat_revenu` (actif/produit diffÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©rÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©). ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â°tats consolidÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©s: `tableau_flux_consolide` (NTFIN50, indirecte, rÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©concilie trÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©so), `variation_capitaux_consolides` (NTFIN51), `annexes_consolidation` (NTFIN52), `comparatif_entites` (NTFIN53), `cycles-consolidation/{id}/export-liasse?export=xlsx` (NTFIN54, openpyxl ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â°Ã‚Â¥6 onglets), **EtapeAuditConsolidation** append-only hash-chaÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â®nÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©e `enregistrer_etape_audit_consolidation` (NTFIN55), `simuler_consolidation` (NTFIN56 what-if pÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©rimÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¨tre, sans ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©criture). Endpoints `cles-repartition/`(+`valider`), `lignes-cle-repartition/`, `allocations/`(+`executer`/`reverser`), `allocations-recurrentes/`, `engagements/`(+`liquider`/`verifier-disponible`), `modeles-cloture/`(+`seed`), `taches-cloture-modele/`, `instances-cloture/`(+`instancier`), `taches-cloture/`(+`cocher`/`generer-od`), `accruals-cloture/`(+`poster`), `justifications-variation/`, `rapprochements-compte/`(+`ouvrir`/`recalculer`/`soumettre`/`valider`/`rejeter`), `lignes-justification-compte/`, `composants-immobilisation/`(+`plans`), `depreciations-immobilisation/`(+`poster`/`reprendre`), `mutations-immobilisation/`, `immobilisations-en-cours/`(+`mettre-en-service`), `lignes-immobilisation-en-cours/`, `contrats-revenu/`(+`allouer`), `obligations-performance/`(+`generer-echeancier`), `echeances-reconnaissance/`(+`reconnaitre`), `cycles-consolidation/{id}/`(`tableau-flux|variation-capitaux|annexes|comparatif|etapes-audit|simuler|export-liasse`), and `etats/execution-budgetaire|analyse-variation|anomalies-ecritures|cockpit-cloture|pret-a-cloturer|rapprochements-en-retard|registre-immobilisations|projection-dotations|positions-contrat-revenu/`.
- **WIR107** exposes the pre-existing `ModeleEcriture`/`AbonnementEcriture` recurring-entry engine (XACC8, previously only driven by the `generer_ecritures_recurrentes` command/NTFIN32 close task) over REST for the Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â« ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â°critures rÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©currentes Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â» screen: `modeles-ecriture/` (+ `generer`, materializes one brouillon ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©criture), `lignes-modele-ecriture/` (`?modele=`), `abonnements-ecriture/` (+ `generer-dues`, replays the idempotent-per-period batch on demand). **WIR85** finally wires the XACC6 permanent-inventory auto-posting that had zero production caller: `stock.services.record_stock_movement` now emits a new `core.events.mouvement_stock_enregistre` signal on every `MouvementStock`, consumed here by `receivers._ecriture_pour_mouvement_stock` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ `poster_mouvement_stock` (entrÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©e/sortie/rebut/signed ajustement post; transfert never does ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â internal move, no value change). Still double-gated OFF by default (`COMPTA_AUTO_ECRITURES`/WIR24 toggle **and** `PlanComptable.inventaire_permanent`) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â without both opt-ins, unchanged (no ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©criture).

### frais ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â expense notes & site allowances (ODX15, new app)  *( `/api/django/frais/` )*
State-only split of FG135/FG136/ZACC6/XACC27/XACC28 OUT of `apps.compta` (migration `frais/0001_odx15_frais_split.py`, `SeparateDatabaseAndState`, `database_operations=[]`): every model **freezes its `db_table` on the historical `compta_*` name** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â no table renamed, no data moved, revert is a plain `git revert`. `apps.frais` never imports `apps.compta.models`; it reaches accounts/ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©critures/trÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©sorerie only via **string-FK** (`'compta.CompteComptable'`, `'compta.EcritureComptable'`, `'compta.CompteTresorerie'`) and calls `apps.compta.services` for every posting + the FG115 period lock. A re-export shim in `apps/compta/models.py` (`from apps.frais.models import ...`) keeps existing importers working. **Deliberately distinct from `rh.NoteDeFrais`** (an employee's self-service portal declaration, no GL posting) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â the two surfaces are never merged (see `docs/module-map.md`).
- **NoteFrais** (FG135) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â an employee's out-of-pocket expense claim: `company`+`employe` FK, `categorie`, `montant` TTC, `motif`, `justificatif` photo (MinIO FileField), reference `NDF-YYYYMM-NNNN` (race-safe). Cycle `brouillonÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢soumiseÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢validee`(posts debit charge 6143 / credit personnel-crÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©diteur 4432, **respecting the compta period lock**)`ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢remboursee`(debit 4432 / credit treasury GL) or `rejetee`. **XACC27** adds `hors_politique` (flagged when it beats a `PlafondNoteFrais.montant_max` for its `categorie` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â warning only, never blocking). **XACC28** adds billable-expense refacturation fields (`refacturable`, `taux_marge`, opaque `client_refacturation_id`/`facture_refacturation_id` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â string-refs, no ventes import). **ZACC6** adds an optional `rapport` FKÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢`RapportNoteFrais` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â once attached, the note is posted/reimbursed IN BLOCK by its report, never individually.
- **RapportNoteFrais** (ZACC6, Odoo Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â« Expense Report Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â» equivalent) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â groups N `NoteFrais` of one employee into one submission: validation posts ONE aggregated ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©criture (ÃƒÅ½Ã‚Â£ charges by compte / credit 4432), reimbursement settles in ONE payment. A note with `rapport=None` keeps its individual FG135 cycle untouched.
- **PlafondNoteFrais** (XACC27) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â per-company, per-`NoteFrais.categorie` spend ceiling referential (`montant_max`, optional `seuil_justificatif_obligatoire`); a category with no configured row is never flagged. **NTP2P11** (migration frais/0003) adds `jours_max_apres_depense` + `escalade_direction_au_dela_de` (both optional, unset = no-op): at `soumettre_note_frais`, `services.note_frais_warning_delai`/`note_frais_escalade_direction` stamp `NoteFrais.warning_delai` (non-blocking text) / `escalade_direction` (bool) server-side and journal them to `records.Activity` (best-effort, never blocks the submission). When `stock.AchatsParametres.sod_stricte` is on, the note's own creator cannot validate its DIRECTION escalation (`_verifier_separation_taches_note_frais`, reads `stock.selectors` only).
- **BaremeIndemnite** + **IndemniteChantier** (FG136) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â kilomÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©trique/per-diem site-trip allowances: `BaremeIndemnite` (per-company `taux_km` MAD/km + `per_diem` MAD/jour, one `defaut` active barÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¨me); `IndemniteChantier` computes `distance_km` via a LOCAL haversine copy (keeps `frais` decoupled from installations/sav) between GPS dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©part/chantier ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬â€ `taux_km` ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬â€ (`aller_retour` ? 2 : 1) + `per_diem` ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬â€ `nombre_jours`, all FROZEN at calc. Same `brouillonÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢soumiseÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢validee`(posts)`ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢remboursee`/`rejetee` cycle as NoteFrais, respecting the period lock. **CHT16** adds `installation_id` — a loose ref (`PositiveIntegerField`, never a FK) to the REAL `installations.Installation`, validated server-side via `installations.selectors.installation_scoped` (never an `installations.models` import); `libelle_chantier` stays the free-text fallback for pre-CHT16 rows.
- Endpoints (dual-served, byte-identical, at BOTH `/api/django/frais/ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚Â¦` and the legacy `/api/django/compta/ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚Â¦`): `notes-frais/` (+ `soumettre|valider|rejeter|rembourser`), `rapports-notes-frais/`, `plafonds-notes-frais/`, `baremes-indemnite/` (auto-demotes prior default), `indemnites-chantier/` (+ lifecycle actions). **NTP2P26/27** add `notes-frais/releve/` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a per-period statement of expense notes (PDF via `pdf_notes_frais.py` or `?export=xlsx`).

### marketing ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â campaigns, sequences, events, loyalty, surveys (ODX9/10)  *( `/api/django/marketing/` )*
Previously undocumented here (36 models, predates this run). State-only split OUT of `apps.compta` (**ODX9**, same `db_table='compta_<model>'`/`SeparateDatabaseAndState`/shim pattern as `frais`/`portail`); **ODX10** gave it its own `/api/django/marketing/ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚Â¦` prefix while the historical `/api/django/compta/ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚Â¦` routes keep serving the SAME ViewSets byte-identically (basenames prefixed `mkt-ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚Â¦` to avoid router-name collisions). `apps.marketing.selectors` is the ONLY sanctioned read surface for other apps (never a `marketing.models` import) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â **WIR96** (this run) is the first real cross-app consumer: `ouverture_partage_pour_token`/`relances_devis_abandonne`, read by `ventes.views.devis.suivi_partage` (see Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â§4 ventes), and the public proposal open-hook now writes `OuverturePartage` (was routed but previously never populated).
- **Models, by sub-domain** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â mailing (`Campagne`, `EnvoiCampagne` one row per recipient, `SuppressionMarketing` opt-out list, `ListeDiffusion`/`AbonnementListe`, `SegmentMarketing`, `RebondSoft`, `ApprobationEnvoiCampagne`, `DomaineEnvoi`); drip sequences (`SequenceRelance` triggered on a lead entering a funnel stage, `EtapeSequence`, `InscriptionSequence`, `ExecutionEtapeSequence`); link tracking (`LienTrackee`, `ClicLien`, `StatutEngagementContact`, **NTMKT18** `ScoreMaturite`/`VariationScoreMaturite` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â multi-signal lead maturity score + its change history); quote recovery/capture (`RelanceDevisAbandonne`, `OuverturePartage`, `FormulaireIntake` + landing capture, `MessageWhatsAppEntrant`, `AppelTelephonique`); satisfaction (`EnqueteNPS`, `AvisClient`, `Enquete`/`ReponseEnquete` a configurable survey engine); loyalty (`CompteFidelite`, `MouvementFidelite`, `RegleUpsell`); events (`TypeEvenement`, `EvenementMarketing`, `BilletEvenement`, `QuestionEvenement`, `CommunicationEvenement`, `InscriptionEvenement`); plus `SupportOffline` (QR print collateral) and `PostSocial` (social content calendar).
- **Journey graph (NTMKT12/14/15)** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â additive graph-routing layer over the existing linear `SequenceRelance`/`EtapeSequence` engine (a sequence with zero graph node still ticks exactly as before). `NoeudJourney` (dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©clencheur/attente(J+n)/**attente_jusqu_a** date-ou-crÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©neau-ouvrÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© (NTMKT14)/action/branche/sortie, free `config` JSON, `position_x/y` for the canvas) + `ArcJourney` (source/cible nÃƒâ€¦Ã¢â‚¬Å“uds, `condition` toujours/a_ouvert/a_clique/score_seuil/tag_present, `ordre` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â supersedes `EtapeSequence`'s single condition with N evaluated arcs). `ModeleJourney` (NTMKT15, a ready-to-instantiate pre-built graph template, complementing the 5 linear XMKT20 recipes) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â seeded by `manage.py seed_modeles_journey`. Endpoints `noeuds-journey/`, `arcs-journey/`, `modeles-journey/`; Celery beat `marketing.executer_journeys` (08:15, company-by-company).
- **Landing pages & content (NTMKT16/23/31)** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `VersionFormulaireIntake` (NTMKT16, an editorial version of a `FormulaireIntake` landing: titre/pitch/image, server-incremented `version`, `publie` flag ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â the public page always renders the latest published version). `BlocContenu` (NTMKT23, a reusable content fragment texte/image/cta; inserting COPIES it into the campaign body ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â editing the source never retro-edits a sent campaign). `ParametresMarketing` (NTMKT31, one singleton per company ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â expÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©diteur nom/email/domaine, additive silence-window hours + daily send cap, all-NULL = unchanged behaviour). Endpoints `versions-formulaire-intake/`, `blocs-contenu/`, `parametres/` (NTMKT31).
- **Ops/reporting additions** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `heatmap-engagement/` (NTMKT24, read-only dayÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬â€hour engagement heatmap), `campagnes/importer-couts/` (NTMKT26, CSV import of external Meta/Google Ads spend), `campagnes/{id}/rapport-pdf/` (NTMKT27, internal campaign-bilan PDF), `registre-consentement/export-pdf/` (NTMKT28, CNDP consent-register export), public `preferences/<token>/` (NTMKT22, self-service per-channel/per-list preference centre ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â never a blanket unsubscribe). `services.purger_tokens_expires` (NTMKT33, daily 03:00 beat, purges expired public unsubscribe/preference tokens) and `services.rappeler_approbations_envoi_en_attente` (NTMKT35, 4-hourly beat, one reminder per pending campaign-send approval past 24h ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â anti-duplicate).
- Endpoints (dual-served at both `/api/django/marketing/ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚Â¦` and the legacy `/api/django/compta/ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚Â¦`): `campagnes/`, `envois-campagne/`, `approbations-envoi-campagne/`, `listes-diffusion/`, `abonnements-liste/`, `segments-marketing/`, `sequences-relance/`, `etapes-sequence/`, `inscriptions-sequence/`, `relances-devis-abandonnes/`, `ouvertures-partage/`, `formulaires-intake/`, `messages-whatsapp/`, `appels/`, `enquetes-nps/`, `avis-clients/`, `comptes-fidelite/`, `mouvements-fidelite/`, `regles-upsell/`, `enquetes/`, `evenements-marketing/`, `inscriptions-evenement/`, `types-evenement/`, `billets-evenement/`, `questions-evenement/`, `communications-evenement/`, `supports-offline/`, `domaines-envoi/`; **NTMKT18-20/39-41** add `scores-maturite/<lead_id>/` (current maturity score + history), `attribution/comparaison/` (the 4 attribution models side-by-side for a signed devis), `campagnes/export/` (XLSX), `campagnes/<pk>/envois/export/` (CSV), `segments-marketing/<pk>/export/` (XLSX of resolved members), `evenements-marketing/<pk>/importer-inscrits/` (bulk CSV/XLSX import); public token/slug views `webhooks/brevo/`, `webhooks/sms-stop/`, `desinscription/<token>/`, `preferences/<token>/`, `double-optin/<token>/`, `r/<token>/` (tracked-link redirect), `enquetes-publiques/<token>/(+soumettre)`, `evenements-marketing/<id>/inscription-publique/`, `intake/<slug>/(+soumettre)`. **PACT106/107/108** give `avis-clients/` (FG239, + Google Reviews routing), `enquetes-nps/` (FG238, distinct from the Pulse eNPS survey) and `messages-whatsapp/` (FG207, read-only inbound log) their first screens (`/marketing/avis-clients`, `/marketing/enquetes-nps`, `/marketing/messages-whatsapp`). Frontend: `JourneyBuilder`/`LandingVersions`/`HeatmapEnvoi`/`UtmBuilder` (NTMKT25, campaign URL/UTM generator, no model) screens + guided `CampagneWizard`/`SequenceWizard`/`EnqueteWizard` (NTMKT29/30/32, opened from their list's Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â« (guidÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©) Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â» button) at `/marketing/{campagnes,sequences,enquetes}/nouveau` + `/marketing/parametres` (`MarketingParametres.jsx`) + `/marketing/generateur-url`. **NTMKT44/45** add `CampagneViewSetAudite`/`EnqueteNPSViewSetNotifiant` + `evenement_inscription_publique_notifiante` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â envelope subclasses/wrappers over the `apps.compta` originals (audit-log the real send / notify on an NPS detractor / notify on public event signup) registered ONLY on the marketing router; the legacy `/compta/ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚Â¦` routes keep serving the unmodified originals.

### flotte ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â fleet: vehicles + rolling equipment (FLOTTE1, new app)  *( `/api/django/flotte/` )*
- **Vehicule** (`company` FK; immatriculation, marque, modÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¨le, ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©nergie diesel/essence/ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©lectrique/hybride, kilomÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©trage, valeur, statut actif/maintenance/rÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©formÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©) and **EnginRoulant** (`company` FK; type nacelle/groupe ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©lectrogÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¨ne/chariot, compteur d'heures, marque, modÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¨le, valeur, statut). Company-scoped ViewSets (company forced server-side, an injected body `company` is ignored) at `vehicules/`, `engins/` with ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©nergie/statut/type filters + search. Uses only the `authentication.Company` string FK ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â no domain-app imports. **FLOTTE3** adds `Vehicule.emplacement_stock_id` (PositiveInteger, NOT a cross-app FK) referencing a `stock.EmplacementStock`; validated same-company + labelled via a function-local `apps.stock.selectors.get_emplacement_scoped` call (degrades to `#id`; never imports stock models). **FLOTTE6** adds **ReferentielFlotte** (editable per-company lookup lists: `domaine` type_vehicule/type_engin/energie/categorie_permis, `code`/`libelle`/`ordre`/`actif`, unique company+domaine+code) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â additive (hardcoded choices untouched) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â plus an idempotent `seed_referentiels_flotte` command. ViewSet `referentiels/` (`?domaine`/`?actif`). **FLOTTE5** adds **ActifFlotte** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a unified asset reference linking entretien/sinistre/document to EITHER a `Vehicule` OR an `EnginRoulant` via one model (exactly-one-target + same-company enforced in `clean()`/`save()`); company-scoped ViewSet `actifs/` (`?type_actif`) + selectors for cross-app reads. **FLOTTE13** adds fuel/energy consumption: `selectors.consommation_vehicule` computes **L/100 km and kWh/100 km** plein-to-plein from `PleinCarburant` + odometer (per-segment, divide-by-zero guarded, L vs kWh kept separate), surfaced read-only at `pleins/consommation/?vehicule=<id>` (`IsAnyRole`, 400 missing/non-int param, 404 cross-company). No migration. **FLOTTE14** adds **CarteCarburant** (fuel card: `numero`, `plafond`, optional `vehicule`/`conducteur` FK) + `selectors.anomalies_pleins` flagging four families per plein ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `km_recul` (odometer decreasing), `km_saut` (>5000 km jump), `conso_aberrante` (>2ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬â€ the vehicle's median baseline, reusing FLOTTE13's conso), `plafond_depasse` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â at `cartes/anomalies/?vehicule=` (read action, `IsAnyRole`). ViewSet `cartes/`. **FLOTTE15** adds **PlanEntretien** (preventive maintenance via an `ActifFlotte` FK ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â reaches both Vehicule km and EnginRoulant heures): triggers by `intervalle_km`/`intervalle_jours`/`intervalle_heures` + last-done refs + alert margins; `selectors.plans_entretien_status` computes next-due vs current km/date/hours (due/upcoming/ok). ViewSet `plans-entretien/` (+ `echeances/` read action). **FLOTTE16** adds **EcheanceEntretien** (a generated due-maintenance record per plan: `due_le`/`due_km`/`due_heures`, `statut` a_faire/planifie/fait) + `services.generer_echeances_entretien` (idempotent ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â no duplicate OPEN ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©chÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©ance per plan; best-effort alert via `notifications.notify`) + a `manage.py generer_echeances_entretien` command. ViewSet `echeances-entretien/` (read any role, `generer` write action; POST-create disabled). Migration flotte/0014. **FLOTTE17** adds **Garage** (atelier: nom/adresse/tÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©lÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©phone) + **OrdreReparation** (repair order on an `ActifFlotte`: garage, description, `cout_main_oeuvre`+`cout_pieces`ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢derived `cout_total`, `statut` ouvert/en_cours/cloture, optional `EcheanceEntretien` link); ViewSets `garages/`, `ordres-reparation/` (+ `couts/` summary, `cloturer/` which solde the linked ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©chÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©ance). Migration flotte/0015. **FLOTTE18** adds **Pneumatique** (tire: position/dimension/montage/dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©pose/statut/coÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â»t) + **PieceFlotte** (part: dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©signation/rÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©f/quantitÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©/coÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â»t, optional OrdreReparation link); ViewSets `pneumatiques/`, `pieces/` + a per-vehicle `synthese/` summary. Migration flotte/0016. **FLOTTE19** adds **EcheanceReglementaire** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â generic regulatory deadlines (visite technique/assurance/vignette/carte grise/taxe ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â  l'essieu) on an `ActifFlotte`, with a today-injectable status (a_jour/a_renouveler/expire) and `expirantes/?within=N`; distinct from the FLOTTE16 maintenance ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©chÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©ances. ViewSet `echeances-reglementaires/`. Migration flotte/0017. **FLOTTE20** adds **BaremeVignette** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â an editable per-company TSAV grid (ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©nergie ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬â€ CV bracket ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ montant, per year) + `Vehicule.puissance_fiscale` + `selectors.calcul_tsav` (electric exempt, no bracket ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ None) + an idempotent standard-grid seed. ViewSet `baremes-vignette/` + `vehicules/{id}/tsav/`. Migration flotte/0018. **ARC14** adds `Vehicule.custom_data` (JSON), the second `customfields` registry pilot alongside `contrats.Contrat`. **ARC28** adds `apps/flotte/platform.py`.

### ged ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â document management / DMS (GED1, new app)  *( `/api/django/ged/` )*
- Governed DMS reusing `records.storage` (MinIO `file_key`). **Cabinet** + **Folder** (self-FK tree with a materialized `path` recomputed in `save()`/`services.move_folder`, sub-tree prefix rewrite + cycle refusal), **Document** (lives in a Folder) + **DocumentVersion** (`file_key`, SHA-256 `checksum` for dedupe via `services.find_duplicate`, server-set incremental `version`, `uploaded_by`). All `company`-scoped (company/created_by/uploaded_by forced server-side). Endpoints: `cabinets/`, `folders/` (+ `descendants`), `documents/`, `document-versions/`. **GED4** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â move (dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©placement) over HTTP: `POST folders/{id}/deplacer/` (body `{parent}`, reparent/to-root, anti-cycle + cross-cabinet refusal via `services.move_folder`) and `POST documents/{id}/deplacer/` (body `{folder}`, via `services.move_document`); destination always resolved inside the caller's company (404 cross-tenant). **GED14** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â inline same-origin preview: `GET document-versions/{id}/apercu/` streams the document bytes through Django (PDF/image/text ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ `Content-Disposition: inline`, else attachment; `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`), gated as a READ action (`IsAnyRole`, like list/retrieve) so read-only roles can preview. **GED17** adds a document lifecycle: `Document.statut` (**brouillonÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢revueÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢approuvÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢archivÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢obsolÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¨te**, default brouillon) with a guarded `LIFECYCLE_TRANSITIONS` state machine in `services.change_lifecycle_status` (illegal/unknown/same-status ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ `ValueError`, cross-company ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ `PermissionError`, `select_for_update`), exposed read-only on the serializer and advanced only via `POST documents/{id}/cycle-vie/` (responsable/admin) + a `?statut=` filter. Local GED statuses ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â **separate from the STAGES.py funnel**. **GED18** adds **DemandeApprobation** (review/approval workflow): `demandeur`/`approbateur`, `statut` en_attente/approuve/rejete; `services.request_review` opens a demande + moves brouillonÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢revue, `approve_demande`/`reject_demande` **reuse the GED17 `change_lifecycle_status`** (no duplicate state machine) to advance revueÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢approuvÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© / back to brouillon; guards duplicate-pending / already-decided / cross-company. Actions `documents/{id}/demander-revue|demandes` + ViewSet `demandes-approbation/` (`approuver`/`rejeter`). **GED19** adds **AclGed** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â per-folder/document ACL: exactly-one target (folder XOR document, two CheckConstraints), principal = `utilisateur` and/or `role`, `niveau` lecture/ecriture/gestion, `herite` flag. `selectors.acl_effective` walks up the materialized `Folder.path` (document override > nearer folder > ancestor only when `herite`; most-permissive at equal scope; admin always gestion), softly wired into `documents_visible_to_user` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â **backward-compatible: no ACL rows ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ behaviour unchanged**. Migration ged/0013. **GED20** adds **PartageGed** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a tokenized public document share (`token` via `secrets`, `expires_at`, hashed `password_hash`, `quota_max` + `telechargements` counter, `actif` kill-switch); a PUBLIC `AllowAny` endpoint `GET /api/django/ged/public/<token>/` resolves **by token only** (never trusts request company/identity), streams the document, atomic quota-conditional increment, and returns 404 (unknown/revoked) / 410 (expired/quota) / 403 (missing/wrong password). Management ViewSet `partages/` (+ `revoquer/`) is fully multi-tenant. Migration ged/0014. **GED21** adds watermarking & diffusion control: flags `Document.watermark_diffusion` + `PartageGed.watermark` and `services.apply_watermark` (image via Pillow ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â already a dep; PDF via PyMuPDF imported lazily, graceful degrade if absent ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ original bytes), wired into the GED14 `apercu` + GED20 public download (no-watermark path byte-identical). Category DEP but NO hard new dependency. Migration ged/0015. **GED22** adds **PolitiqueRetention** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â document retention policies (durÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©e de conservation + action ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â  l'ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©chÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©ance, default the NON-destructive `signaler`) + `selectors.documents_echus(company, today)` (most-specific policy, today-injectable) + a `lister_documents_echus` command. Never auto-deletes passively. ViewSet `politiques-retention/` (+ `echus`). Migration ged/0016. **GED23** adds **ArchivageLegal** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â legal probative-value write-once archiving (SHA-256 integrity hash, best-effort MinIO object-lock with graceful degrade ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â no hard dep). Once archived, app-layer immutability blocks edit/delete/new-version/move/lifecycle/check-out/check-in (all ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ 403, never 500); the ArchivageLegal row is create-only. ViewSet `archivages-legaux/` (+ `documents/{id}/archiver-legalement`). Migration ged/0017.
- **Guide visite seedé (VISITE-CADENCE, 15/09/2026, migration ged/0048)** — `services.seed_guide_visite` dépose le PDF « Guide — La visite technique dans le suivi commercial » (fixtures HTML+PDF, contenu audité claim-par-claim contre le code) dans Documentation → Guides de chaque société non-démo, idempotent par source via `deposit_document`, BEST-EFFORT (ne fait jamais échouer un migrate) ; rattrapage `manage.py seed_guide_visite`.
- **WIR163** finally gives the GED19 `AclGed` model a ViewSet ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `acls/` (was model-only/no REST surface before). **WIR164** exposes the pre-existing XGED16 `TamponSociete` (per-company custom document stamp, on top of the 3 hardcoded systÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¨me stamps) via `tampons-societe/` (was model-only before too). **WIR73** schedules the GED7 `migrate_attachments_to_ged` importer (idempotent by company+file_key) as a weekly Celery beat (`ged.migrer_pieces_jointes`) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a `records.Attachment` created after the last MANUAL import previously never reached GED on its own. **WIR165** gives `core.events.document_produit` its first real emitter: `apps/ventes/utils/pdf.py generate_facture_pdf` sends it (`source='ventes_facture'`) right after storing the legacy invoice PDF; the receiver stays a no-op until an admin creates a `RoutageDocumentaire(source='ventes_facture', ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚Â¦)` for their company. *(Note: this section's endpoint/model inventory pre-dates several XGED*-prefixed features ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â e.g. `coffres/`, `demandes-document/`, `regles-dossier/`, `regles-approbation/`, `validations-ocr/`, `annotations/`, e-signature champs/signataires, quotas ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â that already exist in `apps/ged/urls.py` but were never written up here; out of scope for this pass.)*
- **NTDOC32** adds `selectors.duree_retention_applicable(company, type_document='')` — lecture seule, durée de rétention (jours) de la politique GED22 active la plus spécifique/stricte pour une catégorie, `None` si aucune ne s'applique. Point d'entrée cross-app pour une app qui conserve ses propres pièces (ex. `contrats` — dépôts de contrepartie) sans avoir de `ged.Document` ni durée codée en dur.

### rh ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â human resources: employee master (FG154, new app)  *( `/api/django/rh/` )*
- **DossierEmploye** (`company` FK; employee record). **FG155** adds the employment-contract layer: `type_contrat` (TextChoices CDI/CDD/ANAPEC/stage/intÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©rim) + `contrat_date_debut`/`contrat_date_fin` (nullable dates; empty `date_fin` = open-ended/CDI). Company-scoped ViewSet (`employes/`) with `@action cdd-a-echeance/?within=N` (default 30 days) returning only same-company CDDs whose `contrat_date_fin` falls within the window. **FG156** adds the mandatory Moroccan payroll identity fields to `DossierEmploye`: `cnss`/`cimr`/`amo`, `situation_familiale` (cÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©libataire/mariÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©/divorcÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©/veuf), `nombre_enfants` (IR deductions) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â all nullable (CIN/RIB already existed). **FG166** adds **Pointage** (clock-in/out: `company`+`employe` FK, `type_pointage` arrivÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©e/dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©part/complet, server-set `heure_arrivee`/`heure_depart`, optional GPS, computed `duree_minutes`) with actions `pointages/pointager-arrivee` + `pointages/{id}/pointager-depart` (server timestamp; ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ COMPLET + duration once arrival is set). `IsResponsableOrAdmin`-gated. **FG172** adds the skills matrix: **Competence** (per-company catalogue, `domaine` pose_structure/raccordement_dc/raccordement_ac/mes_onduleur/pompage/soudure/autre, unique `(company, code)`) + **CompetenceEmploye** (one row per `(employe, competence)`, `niveau` 0ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Å“4 Non-acquisÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢Expert, server-set `evalue_par`/`evalue_le`). ViewSets `competences/`, `competences-employe/` (+ `matrice/` grid action, `?domaine`/`?niveau_min` filters), admin-gated, company forced server-side; duplicate-code ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ clean 400. **FG173** adds **Habilitation** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â electrical authorizations (NF C 18-510: B0/H0/B1V/B2V/BRÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚Â¦) per employee with `organisme`, `date_obtention`, `date_validite` (expiry) and a computed `valide` flag; ViewSet `habilitations/` + `expirantes/?expire_within=N` (expiring-soon + already-expired), unique `(employe, type_habilitation)`. Distinct from the FG172 competence matrix. **FG174** adds **Certification** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â the non-electrical family (travail_hauteur/harnais/caces_nacelle/secourisme_sst/conduite/autre) per employee with `organisme`, `date_validite` + computed `valide`; ViewSet `certifications/` (+ `expirantes/?expire_within=N`), unique `(employe, type_certification)`. Modelled distinctly from the FG173 electrical Habilitation. **FG175** adds `selectors.echeances_rh` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a unified expiry-alert engine unioning expiring Habilitations + Certifications + DocumentEmploye within X days (normalized `{type, employe, libelle, date_validite, jours_restants}`, `today` a param) at `echeances/?within=N`, plus a `manage.py alertes_expiration_rh` command dispatching one notification per ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©chÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©ance via the shared `notifications.notify` service (function-local import). No model. **FG176** adds `selectors.verifier_habilitation_requise(company, employe, type_requis)` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ `{autorise, manquantes, expirees, message}` (reuses FG173's validity rule; `INTERVENTION_HABILITATIONS` map) + endpoint `employes/{id}/verifier-habilitation/?type=&intervention=`. A SOFT guard (reports; enforcement at assignment stays in installations). No model. **FG177** adds **VisiteMedicale** (occupational-health visits per employee: `date_visite`, `prochaine_visite`, `aptitude` apte/restrictions/inapte, mÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©decin/organisme, computed `a_jour`); ViewSet `visites-medicales/` (+ `expirantes/?expire_within=N`), and feeds FG175's `echeances_rh` with a `visite_medicale` family. Migration rh/0017. **FG178** adds **EpiCatalogue** (PPE catalogue: `type_epi` casque/harnais/gants_isolants/chaussures/lunettes/autre) + **DotationEpi** (nominative issue: `employe`, `epi`, `taille`, `date_dotation`, `date_renouvellement`, `quantite`); ViewSets `epi-catalogue/`, `dotations-epi/` (+ `a-renouveler/`, `employe/`), feeds FG175. Migration rh/0018. **FG179** adds EPI life/expiry tracking: `EpiCatalogue.duree_vie_mois`/`intervalle_controle_mois` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ derived `DotationEpi.date_peremption`/`date_prochain_controle` (end-of-month-clamped month math), computed `perime`/`a_controler` (`today`-injectable), endpoint `dotations-epi/a-remplacer-controler/?expire_within=N`; feeds FG175 with `epi_peremption`/`epi_controle` families. Migration rh/0019. **FG180** adds **EmargementEpi** (signed EPI-handover acknowledgement, loi 53-05 typed name + server-side IP/user-agent evidence) + `accuse_remise`/`date_accuse` on DotationEpi; `services.emarger_dotation` records it (company + acting user server-side, accusÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© frozen at first signature); actions `dotations-epi/{id}/emarger|emargements`. No external e-sign dep. Migration rh/0020. **FG181** adds **AccidentTravail** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â workplace-accident register (race-safe `AT-` reference, date/lieu/employÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©/gravitÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©/arrÃƒÆ’Ã‚Âªt+jours/photo, CNSS declaration flags) + a CNSS CSV export (`?export=csv`). ViewSet `accidents-travail/`. Migration rh/0021. **FG182** adds **PresquAccident** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a lightweight near-miss register (race-safe `NM-` ref, lieu/gravitÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© potentielle/mesure corrective, server-side declarant; no injured person/CNSS ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â distinct from FG181) + a stats-by-gravitÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© selector. ViewSet `presqu-accidents/`. Migration rh/0022.
- **Groupe NTHCM (people/talent management).** **NTHCM1** adds `DossierEmploye.manager` (self-FK, nullable, ligne hiérarchique réelle — `clean()` refuse le cycle et le manager d'une autre société). **NTHCM4** adds `Poste.effectif_budgete` (headcount planning, 0 = aucune limite; comparatif via `selectors.effectif_poste`). **NTHCM5/7/8** add **CycleRevisionSalariale**/**EnveloppeManager**/**PropositionRevision** (campagne de révision salariale par manager, enveloppe budgétaire). **NTHCM8/10/12/13/14** add les **OKR** (**ObjectifEntreprise**/**KeyResult**/**OkrIndividuel**/**KeyResultIndividuel**), l'évaluation **9-box** (**EvaluationNeufBox**), la planification de succession (**PosteCle**/**PlanSuccession**) et l'**EnqueteEngagement** (+ `ReponseEnquete`/`ParticipationEnquete`/`PlanActionEngagement`). **NTFSM26** adds `DossierEmploye.zone_intervention` (champ libre, filtre de l'écran `/dispatch/equipe` — `EquipePage.jsx`). Migrations rh/0085-0095. **NTHCM16** adds **FeedbackContinu** (`de`/`pour` FK employé, `type` reconnaissance/axe_amelioration/coaching, `visible_par_pour` + `partage_avec_manager` flags governing visibility; auto-adressage refusé, `de` posé serveur) — ViewSet `feedbacks-continus/`, open to `IsAnyRole` (queryset restricts scope, not the gate). **NTHCM17** adds the training-path trio **ParcoursFormation** (titre/obligatoire/`poste_cible`/`departement_cible` targeting) → **EtapeParcours** (ordered steps, `type_contenu` session/quiz/document_kb/lien_externe, exactly one pointer populated) → **ProgressionParcours** (per-employee `etapes_completees` M2M; `pourcentage`/`statut`/`date_completion` always DERIVED via `services.recalculer_progression_parcours`, unique per company+parcours+employe) — ViewSets `parcours-formation/`, `etapes-parcours/`, `progressions-parcours/` (+ `{id}/completer-etape/`, `{id}/devalider-etape/`). **NTHCM19** adds `ReglageRH.rappel_parcours_apres_jours` (default 14) + `services.assigner_parcours_obligatoires` (idempotent auto-assignment of mandatory parcours by poste/département targeting at hire) and the daily `rappels_parcours_formation` Celery task/management command. **NTHCM21** makes a `termine` transition deliver/extend the titled `habilitation_type`/`certification_type` (`Habilitation`/`Certification` types, `validite_mois`) via `services.delivrer_titre_parcours` reusing the XRH34 `prolonger_titre_rh` mechanism — no-op for untitled parcours, never re-fires on retake. **NTHCM23/24** add the shared `ActeurTache` TextChoices (rh/manager/it/employe_lui_meme) to `ElementIntegration`/`ElementIntegrationEmploye` (onboarding, `elements-integration/`, `elements-integration-employe/` + self-service `mes-taches-onboarding`) and `ElementSortie` (offboarding, `elements-sortie/` + `en-retard`) — `acteur_type`/`delai_jours`/`echeance` resolved server-side via `services.resoudre_acteur_tache` (manager = `DossierEmploye.manager`, NTHCM1;
