IT = `ReglageRH.contact_it_defaut`); unresolved targets stay unassigned (signal, never a guessed fallback), and `selectors.offboarding_en_retard` ranks IT tasks first (security-critical). **NTHCM25** adds `services.notifier_tache_assignee` (best-effort, deduped once/day/task via the shared `notifications.notify`) fired on task creation and swept daily by `notifier_taches_integration_sortie`. **NTHCM27** adds `DossierEmploye.genre` (Genre TextChoices femme/homme/autre, blank default) and `selectors.analytics_diversite` — aggregated gender/seniority split with a `SEUIL_ANONYMAT_DIVERSITE=5` floor (segments under 5 people are masked, never a nominative list). **NTHCM28** adds `selectors.taux_absenteisme` — unified absenteeism rate (congé/maladie/accident_travail/non_justifié, from `DemandeConge`+`AccidentTravail`+`IncidentPresence`) with monthly breakdown and a guarded `taux_pct=None` on zero-denominator, plus `comparaison_absenteisme` (department vs company). **NTHCM29** adds `selectors.correlation_formation_performance` — before/after `EvaluationEmploye.note_globale` average around a parcours' `date_completion`, masked under `SEUIL_ECHANTILLON_CORRELATION=3` comparable employees, always carrying the non-causal disclaimer "Évolution observée, non causale.". NTHCM16/17/19/21/23/24/27 land in migrations rh/0096-0102, served `Administrateur/Responsable`-gated off **AnalyticsRhViewSet** (`analytics/diversite/`, `analytics/absenteisme/`) except FeedbackContinu (open to all roles). Three self-contained frontend screens ship alongside: **NTHCM2**'s `Organigramme.jsx` (`/rh/organigramme`, server-computed hierarchy tree off `employes/organigramme/`), **NTHCM6**'s `CalibrationRevisions.jsx` (`/rh/calibration-revisions`, cross-manager salary-review distribution view gated on `salaires_voir`), and **NTHCM9**'s `OkrDashboard.jsx` (`/rh/okr`, self-service "mes OKR/mon équipe/entreprise" off `okr-individuels/tableau-de-bord/`).
- **ARC19** adds a nullable `DossierEmploye.tiers` FK (migration rh/0082, `tiers_bridge.py`) mirroring identity one-way into `tiers.Tiers`. **ARC28/33/40** add `apps/rh/platform.py` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `agent_actions_module` (read-only employÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©s + demandes de congÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© actions) and `kpi_providers` (effectif actif + absences en cours, consumed by the ARC40 federated KPI endpoint).

### paie ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â Moroccan payroll engine (Groupe PAIE, new app)  *( `/api/django/paie/` )*
Full CNSS/CIMR/AMO/IR payroll run, distinct from `rh.DossierEmploye` (the employee master `paie` reads via `ProfilPaie`, string/FK-id, never a duplicate identity). `company`-scoped throughout, additive-only.
- **ParamÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©trage versionnÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `ParametrePaie` (PAIE2: SMIG/SMAG, plafond CNSS, taux CNSS/AMO/taxe formation professionnelle, versioned per `date_effet` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â history never edited) + `BaremeIR`/`TrancheIR` (PAIE4: versioned IR tax brackets) + `Rubrique` (PAIE6: configurable pay-slip line items) + `StructurePaie`/`StructurePaieRubrique` (named rubrique bundles applied to a profil).
- **Profil employÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `ProfilPaie` (PAIE8: salary/contract payroll profile), `RubriqueEmploye` (PAIE9: recurring per-employee rubriques), `RegimeMutuelle`/`AdhesionMutuelle` (private health-insurance plan + enrolment).
- **Cycle mensuel** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `PeriodePaie` (PAIE10: one monthly run, statut-gated) + `ElementVariable`/`TypeEntreePonctuelle` (PAIE11: one-off variable entries e.g. overtime/bonus) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ `BulletinPaie`/`LigneBulletin` (PAIE17: the frozen/immutable payslip snapshot once validated) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ `CumulAnnuel` (PAIE27: per-employee YTD brut/net-imposable/IR/CNSS/congÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©s) + `ProvisionPaieMensuelle` (13th-month/severance accrual) + `VentilationAnalytiquePaie` (fixed analytic cost-center split).
- **Recouvrement & virement** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `AvanceSalarie` (PAIE28: salary advance/loan + monthly deduction), `SaisieArret` (PAIE29: statutory wage-garnishment, seizable-quota guarded), `OrdreVirement`/`LigneVirement` (PAIE30: bank payroll-transfer file).
- **DÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©claratif** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `EcheanceDeclarative` (statutory filing deadlines) + `DepotBDS` (CNSS BDS deposit).
- Endpoints: `parametres/`, `baremes/`, `rubriques/`, `types-entree-ponctuelle/`, `profils/` (+ `synchroniser-salaire`, `attestation`, `stc`/`stc-pdf` solde-de-tout-compte, `simulation`, `registre-conges`, `historique-carriere`), `rubriques-employe/`, `structures/` (+ `appliquer`), `regimes-mutuelle/`/`adhesions-mutuelle/`, `periodes/` (+ `run-gratification`, `importer-elements-rh`, `reporter-elements`, `cloturer`, `livre-de-paie`, `cout-global`, `cout-employeur`, `journal-ventile`, `provisions`, `rapprochement-gl`), `elements-variables/`, `bulletins/` (+ `bulletin`, `declaration-cnss`, `declaration-cimr`, `fichier-cimr`, `fichier-damancom(-strict)`, `mouvements-cnss`, `etat-ir`, `etat-charges`), `cumuls-annuels/`, `avances/`, `saisies/`, `ordres-virement/`, `lignes-virement/`, `echeances-declaratives/` (+ `echeances`, `notifier-echeances-retard`), `mes-bulletins/` (employee self-service payslip vault). **PACT95** gives `types-entree-ponctuelle/` (`TypeEntreePonctuelle`, ZPAI9) its first screen, `/paie/types-entree-ponctuelle`. GL reconciliation + PDF slips reuse `core.pdf.render_pdf` (ARC11) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â no bespoke PDF plumbing. **WIR89** adds `selectors.divergences_cnss_periode` + `services.controler_coherence_cnss` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â the CNSS-number analogue of the pre-existing ARC25 RIB-divergence check (compares `ProfilPaie.numero_cnss` against `rh.DossierEmploye.cnss` via `apps.rh.selectors`, a signal only ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â never a fusion): run automatically inside `declaration_cnss`, best-effort, and notifies payroll managers (`notify_many`, event key `paie_cnss_divergence`) only when a genuine mismatch exists.
- **Groupe NTPAY (multi-pays + déclaratif).** **SchemaComptablePaie** (NTPAY2) + **DepotDeclaratif** (NTPAY5) models. **NTPAY7/8** add **PaysPaie** + le moteur multi-pays `services.MOTEURS_PAYS` (registre par `PaysPaie.cle_moteur`, un pays sans moteur déclaré retombe sur `CODE_PAYS_DEFAUT` — comportement Maroc inchangé). Migrations paie/0048-0051. Endpoints `bulletins/{id}/bordereau-cnss/` (bordereau CNSS imprimable, `?export=pdf` ou `?export=telepaiement` pour le fichier de télépaiement) et `profils/{id}/certificat-travail/`. Écrans `/paie/plan-comptable` (`PlanComptablePaie.jsx`) et `/paie/pays` (`PaysPaie.jsx`).
- **Groupe NTPAY (RH/déclaratif, suite).** **NTPAY13** freezes payroll **devise** at snapshot time: `PeriodePaie.devise` + `BulletinPaie.devise` (both default `MAD`, migration paie/0052) derive from `ProfilPaie.pays.devise` (NTPAY7/8) via `services.devise_du_profil`, never converting — `services.devise_virement_periode` reads only VALIDÉ virement-payable bulletins of a `PeriodePaie` and raises a French `ValidationError` if it finds more than one devise. **NTPAY14/15** add a profile-less **simulateur d'embauche**: `services.simuler_cout_embauche` (`profils/simulation-embauche/`, gated `salaires_voir`) replays the real brut→net engine with zero DB writes, then `builders.render_lettre_offre_pdf` (`profils/lettre-offre/`) turns it into a candidate-facing **lettre d'offre** PDF showing only brut/net proposed — `CLES_COUT_INTERNE` keeps charges patronales/provisions/coût total employeur strictly internal, never printed. **NTPAY16** adds `selectors.cockpit_conformite_paie` (`periodes/conformite/`) — a **conformité** aggregate merging overdue/upcoming `EcheanceDeclarative`, missing `DepotDeclaratif` proof, un-validated `BaremeIR`/`ParametrePaie`, and late-closing periods; `conforme` is true only when all lists are empty. Screen `features/paie/ConformitePaie.jsx`. **NTPAY17** adds `selectors.comparer_baremes` (`baremes/{id}/comparer/`) — an in-memory **comparateur de barèmes** impact preview (net/IR/coût employeur) between two versioned `BaremeIR`/`ParametrePaie` jeux, nothing persisted. **NTPAY18** extends the XPAI5 charges report: `bulletins/{id}/etat-charges/?detail=1` (JSON) / `?export=pdf` prints the 5-organisme **état des charges** (CNSS/AMO/IR/CIMR/mutuelle) from VALIDÉ bulletins only. **NTPAY19** adds `selectors.rapport_masse_salariale` (`periodes/rapports/masse-salariale/?debut=&fin=&group_by=departement|site`, `?export=csv|pdf`) — **masse salariale** synthesis over a period window. **NTPAY20** adds `builders.registre_remunerations_context`/`render_registre_remunerations_pdf` (`cumuls-annuels/registre-remunerations/?annee=&export=pdf`) — the legally-required annual **registre des rémunérations**, one row per salarié paid that year, read straight from `CumulAnnuel`. **NTPAY21** adds a signed-token **wizard-publication** on `BaremeIRViewSet` (`baremes/{id}/wizard-publication/`, no model, `TimestampSigner` 1h TTL): `etape='apercu'` runs the NTPAY17 comparator and returns a `jeton_apercu`; `etape='publication'` refuses without that token and requires `valide_par_fondateur=true`. Wizard in `features/paie/PaieParametres.jsx`. **NTPAY22** adds `services.checklist_cloture`/`verifier_cloture_autorisee` (`periodes/{id}/checklist-cloture/`, wired into `cloturer/`) — a **checklist de clôture** (avances/saisies-arrêt retenues, écarts M/M-1 sans anomalie, échéances à jour, ordre de virement généré) ; any point `en alerte` blocks closing unless a non-empty `motif_acquittement` is given. Wired into `features/paie/PaieRunWizard.jsx` step 5. **NTPAY23/24** add company-wide **paramétrage** paie: `ParametragePaieCompany` (migration paie/0053, one row/société, `parametrage/` + `courant/`) holds nullable `jour_virement_defaut`, `compte_emetteur`, `gabarit_telepaiement_cnss`, `devise_defaut`, `seuil_ecart_net_pct`, `rappel_retroactif_automatique` (default off — every setting stays unset until the founder sets it). `GabaritDeclaratif` (migration paie/0054, `gabarits-declaratifs/`) makes the SIMT/télépaiement-CNSS fixed-width layouts EDITABLE (`structure_json`), strict fallback to the hardcoded layout when no active gabarit exists. Screen `features/paie/PaieParametres.jsx`. **NTPAY25/26** add two Celery beats: `tasks.rappeler_echeances_declaratives` (daily 06:45) notifies payroll managers at J-7/J-3/J-0 of an undeposited `EcheanceDeclarative`, idempotent per échéance+seuil via a chatter marker; `tasks.recalculer_cumuls_annuels` (monthly, 2nd at 02:20) re-diffs each `CumulAnnuel` against its VALIDÉ bulletins, correcting ONLY genuine drift with a dated/motivated chatter line — never a silent mutation.

### gestion_projet ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â project management (PROJ1, new app)  *( `/api/django/gestion-projet/` )*
- **PeriodeVerrouilleeTemps** + **PortailProjetToken** + **RecurrenceTache** (previously undocumented here) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â timesheet month-locks, the project client-portal share-link tokens, and recurring-task templates; endpoints `periodes-verrouillees-temps/`, `portail-tokens/`, `recurrences-tache/`. **NTPRJ3** adds `selectors.projets_en_production(company)` — lecture seule, projets `en_cours`/`en_pause` d'une société ; point d'entrée cross-app pour la régularisation WIP/PCA de `compta` en fin de période, sans jamais importer `gestion_projet.models`. **PACT78** gives all three their first screen, `/projets/parametres` (Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â« ParamÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¨tres avancÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©s Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â»).
- **Projet** + **ProjetChantier** (`company`-scoped). **PROJ2** adds **ProjetLien** (`company` + `projet` FK; `type_cible` devis/facture/ticket/achat, `cible_id` target PK, cached `libelle`) linking a project to other apps' documents by **string-FK only** (no real cross-app FK). Endpoints: `projet-liens/` (CRUD, `?projet=`/`?type_cible=` filters) + `projets/{id}/liens/` (enriched). `selectors.liens_enrichis` enriches devis links via a function-local `apps.ventes.selectors.devis_card` call and degrades to the stored label otherwise (cross-app boundary respected; import-linter clean). **PROJ3** adds a project-lifecycle state machine on `Projet` (`statut` brouillonÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢planifieÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢en_coursÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â¡Ã¢â‚¬Å¾en_pauseÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢termine, annule from any non-terminal ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â **independent of `STAGES.py`**, rule #2) via actions `planifier`/`demarrer`/`mettre-en-pause`/`reprendre`/`terminer`/`annuler` (illegal ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ 400; statut read-only outside actions) + a **ProjetActivity** transition log (`historique/`). **PROJ4** adds **PhaseProjet** (project WBS: `type_phase` etude/appro/pose/mes/reception ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â own enum, not STAGES; prÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©vu/rÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©el dates, `statut`, `avancement_pct` 0-100; unique projet+type_phase) + `services.instancier_phases_standard` (5 ordered phases, idempotent). ViewSet `phases/` + action `projets/{id}/instancier-phases`. **PROJ14** adds delay detection: `selectors.retards_projet` + `GET projets/{id}/retards/` classifying unfinished tasks and unreached milestones as `en_retard` (past due) or `a_risque` (due within `seuil_jours`, default 7) with `retard_jours` (no migration). **PROJ18** adds `selectors.plan_de_charge` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â per-resource capacity (working days ÃƒÂ¢Ã‹â€ Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ IndisponibilitÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬â€ hours/day) vs allocated (AffectationRessource charge, direct + team-split, pro-rated to the window) over a period with a `surcharge` flag + `utilisation_pct` (None when capacity 0); endpoint `ressources/plan-de-charge/?debut=&fin=`. No model. **PROJ19** adds `selectors.conflits_affectation` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â double-booking: same `RessourceProfil` allocated to ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â°Ã‚Â¥2 `AffectationRessource` whose windows overlap (direct + via ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©quipe; bonus: allocation during an IndisponibilitÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©), at `ressources/conflits-affectation/?debut=&fin=`. No model. **PROJ20** adds `selectors.nivellement_charge` (resource levelling) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â proposes moving direct affectations off over-allocated RessourceProfil to under-loaded ones without creating a PROJ19 conflict (read-only), at `ressources/nivellement-charge/`. No model. **PROJ21** adds **BudgetProjet** + **LigneBudgetProjet** (categorie materiel/main_oeuvre/sous_traitance/divers, montant_prevu, optional quantite/pu) + `selectors.budget_total` (total + par_categorie, all 4 categories present); ViewSets `budgets/`, `lignes-budget/` + a `/total/` action. Migration gestion_projet/0013. **PROJ22** adds `selectors.couts_engages_vs_reels` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â committed/actual project cost vs the PROJ21 budget per category (labour from internal AffectationRessource quantized to 2dp; matÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©riel/sous-traitance via ProjetLien with graceful degrade ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â no cross-app amount selector exists yet), ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©cart + ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©cart % (divide-by-zero guarded), at `projets/{id}/couts-engages-reels/`. No model. **ARC22** adds an additive nullable `SousTraitant.fournisseur` FK (migration gestion_projet/0042) bridging the project's local subcontractor notebook to the DC34 unified `stock.Fournisseur` master; `services.creer_sous_traitant_via_master` is the recommended creation path (calls `stock.services.create_sous_traitant` via a function-local import, never `apps.stock.models`) + `management/backfill_sous_traitant_fournisseur` for existing rows. **WIR87** closes the ARC22 gap for good: `SousTraitantViewSet.perform_create`/`perform_update` are now the ONLY write path, always setting `fournisseur` on create and freezing the duplicated columns (nom/specialite/contact/telephone/email/actif) on both sides of every update ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â they can no longer diverge; the canonical screen (`RisquesPage.jsx` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ Sous-traitance tab) no longer even calls this local endpoint, reading/writing the `stock.Fournisseur` master directly via `installations/sous-traitants/`. **CHT13** fixes the portfolio margin in `tableau_portefeuille` — revenue is now the REAL invoiced CA (`_revenu_projet_cross_app`), resolved via BOTH the `ProjetLien(devis)` path and the `ProjetChantier.chantier_id` path (batch `installations.selectors.devis_ids_des_chantiers`), matching the per-project `pnl_projet` figure (was silently 0 for chantier-only-linked projects). **CHT18** — `creer_projet_depuis_devis` now attaches the devis's `Installation` (if any) as a `ProjetChantier` in the SAME call, ending the separate frontend `projet-chantiers` POST.

### qhse ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â quality / health / safety / environment (QHSE1, new app)  *( `/api/django/qhse/` )*
- NCR/CAPA (non-conformities + corrective/preventive actions), `company`-scoped. **QHSE2** adds the ITP (inspection & test plan) templates: **PlanInspectionModele** (code/nom/actif) + **PointControleModele** (FK plan; `phase`, `type_releve` mesure/visuel/document/essai, `hold_point` bool, `ordre`). ViewSets `plans-inspection/`, `points-controle/` (company forced server-side; a point is validated to share its plan's company ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ 400 otherwise). **QHSE3** adds an idempotent `seed_itp_solaire` management command (per-company or `--company`) seeding 3 solar ITP templates (rÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©sidentiel rÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©seau / autoconsommation indus-com / pompage agricole), 7 points each, hold-points on Raccordement + Mise en service. **QHSE4** adds the APPLIED instance: **PlanInspectionChantier** (FK template `PlanInspectionModele`, `chantier_id` string-FK, `statut`) + **ReleveControle** (FK point; `valeur`, `conforme` NullBoolean, `photo_key` MinIO, `releve_par`); `services.instancier_plan_chantier` materialises one relevÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© per template point (idempotent, backfills). ViewSets `plans-chantier/` (+ `instancier`), `releves/`. `IsResponsableOrAdmin`-gated. **QHSE19** adds **RetourClientQualite** (client quality satisfaction): `note_satisfaction` 1ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Å“5 + `commentaire`, string-id cross-app links `chantier_id`/`client_id` (no model import), `traite` bool, `selectors.satisfaction_moyenne` + ViewSet `retours-client/` (+ `moyenne/` action, `?chantier_id`/`?traite` filters). **QHSE20** adds `selectors.iso9001_readiness` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a read-only Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â« ISO 9001 readiness Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â» dashboard: weighted global score + 6 criteria mapped to ISO 9001:2015 clauses (NCR closed 10.2, CAPA on-time 10.2, audits 9.2, procedures published 7.5, ITP coverage 8.5/8.6, client satisfaction 9.1.2), divide-by-zero guarded, at `iso9001-readiness/` (responsable/admin). No model. **QHSE21** adds **EvaluationRisque** (document unique d'ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©valuation des risques: `reference` `DUER-` race-safe, `statut` brouillon/validee/archivee, string-ref `chantier_id`) + **LigneEvaluationRisque** (poste/activitÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©/danger, `gravite`ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬â€`probabilite` (1ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Å“5) = stored `criticite`, mesures, risque rÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©siduel). ViewSets `evaluations-risque/` (+ `criticite/` summary), `lignes-evaluation-risque/`, role-gated. Migration qhse/0014. **QHSE22** adds the document-unique gate: `selectors.document_unique_valide(company, chantier_id)` (True iff ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â°Ã‚Â¥1 validated EvaluationRisque with lines) + `services.exiger_document_unique` (raises `ValidationError` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â consumed by `installations` to gate the pose transition) + endpoint `evaluations-risque/document-unique-statut/?chantier_id=`. chantier_id is a string-ref (no installations import). No model. **QHSE23** adds **PermisTravail** (work permit: hauteur/consignation_elec/point_chaud/espace_confine, server-set race-safe `PT-` reference, string-ref `chantier_id`, validity dates, `valider`/`cloturer` actions). ViewSet `permis-travail/`. Migration qhse/0015. **QHSE24** adds **ConsignationLoto** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a lockout-tagout electrical-isolation record on a QHSE23 `PermisTravail` (point de consignation, cadenas/ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©tiquette, vÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©rif absence tension, server-set race-safe ref, statut consignÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©e/dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©consignÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©e) with a `deconsigner` action. ViewSet `consignations-loto/`. Migration qhse/0016.
- **ARC10** makes NCR closure the pilot domain consumer of the generic `core` BPM engine: `services.demarrer_workflow_cloture_ncr` installs the FG369 `cloture_ncr` template idempotently per company and attaches a `WorkflowInstance` to the NCR via contenttypes (qhse drives the engine; `core` never imports qhse) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a two-step agentÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢responsable approval cycle; the rule going forward is any NEW multi-step approval uses this engine, never an ad hoc one. **ARC38** repatriates `incident_declared` onto the `core.events` bus (see notifications paragraph). **ARC28** adds `apps/qhse/platform.py`.

### contrats ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â contracts (CONTRAT1, new app)  *( `/api/django/contrats/` )*
- **Contrat** (`company`-scoped). **CONTRAT3** adds **PartieContrat** (`company` + `contrat` FK `related_name='parties'`; `type_partie` client/prestataire/temoin/garant/autre, `nom`, `fonction`, `email`, `telephone`, `ordre`) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â the parties/signatories of a contract. ViewSet `parties/` (CRUD, `?contrat=` filter; a party is validated same-company as its contract ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ 400). The "ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â°Ã‚Â¥2 signatories" rule lives in `Contrat.valider_parties()` for finalization (not enforced at create). **CONTRAT4** adds **ContratLien** (string-FK devis/lead/installation/maintenance, like ProjetLien) with `selectors.liens_enrichis` enriching via function-local `ventes`/`crm`/`installations` selectors (sav degrades to stored label). **CONTRAT5** adds `Contrat.sav_contrat_maintenance_id` (PositiveInteger, string-id to `sav.ContratMaintenance` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â additive, no sav import, validation deferred until a sav selector exists). **CONTRAT6** adds `Contrat.confidentialite` (public/interne/confidentiel, default interne) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â CONFIDENTIEL contracts are visible only to Administrators, gated in `get_queryset` on the authoritative `user.menu_tier` (not the unreliable `role_legacy`/Role-FK divergence). `IsResponsableOrAdmin`-gated. **CONTRAT13** adds **RegleApprobation** (approval rule by `type_contrat` and/or `montant_min`/`montant_max` bounds + `niveau_approbation`/`nombre_approbateurs`/`priorite`/`actif`) with `selectors.resoudre_regle_approbation` (most-specific wins: exact type > narrowest bounded interval > prioritÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© > id). ViewSet `regles-approbation/` + `GET /resoudre/?montant=&type_contrat=`, company forced server-side (never body-set). **CONTRAT14** adds **EtapeApprobation** (internal approval workflow): `services.lancer_workflow_approbation` instantiates one step per the matching RegleApprobation's `nombre_approbateurs` (via the `resoudre_regle_approbation` selector), and `approuver_etape`/`rejeter_etape` advance it sequentially (out-of-order ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ 400, relaunch refused). Statuses are local (en_attente/approuve/rejete) and the workflow **never mutates `Contrat.statut`**. Actions `contrats/{id}/lancer-approbation|etapes-approbation|approuver-etape|rejeter-etape`. **CONTRAT15** adds **ContratActivity** (chatter/journal): auto-logs statut/confidentialitÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© transitions + the approval-workflow steps (LOG, with `field`/`old_value`/`new_value` snapshots in TextField) plus manual notes; actions `contrats/{id}/historique` (most-recent-first timeline) + `noter`. Acting user + company always server-side. **CONTRAT16** adds **SignatureContrat** (in-app e-sign, loi 53-05 typed name): `signataire_nom` + server-side evidence (`ip_adresse`/`user_agent`/acting user), `role_signataire`, `methode`; `services.signer_contrat` records it, logs via the chatter, and flips `Contrat.statut`ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢signÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© through the existing state machine **only when all required parties (client+prestataire) have signed**. Unique `(contrat, role_signataire)`. Actions `contrats/{id}/signer|signatures`. No external e-sign provider. Migration contrats/0013. **CONTRAT17** chains an auto signÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢actif: once all required parties have signed, `signer_contrat` advances the contract to `actif` via the existing state machine **iff `date_debut` is null or ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â°Ã‚Â¤ today** (future start stays `signe`), logged in the CONTRAT15 chatter (`today` injectable). No model. **CONTRAT18** adds **VersionContrat** (immutable contract-render versioning): server-incremented `version` (`select_for_update` max+1, never count()+1), frozen `contenu` + optional MinIO `fichier_key`; `services.creer_version` snapshots on demand + auto-snapshots on the signÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© transition (best-effort, CONTRAT16/17 preserved); read-only retrieval viewset `versions/` (paginated). Migration contrats/0014.
- **Revenus rÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©currents** (NTSUB1-4, previously undocumented here) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a sellable-offer catalogue: **PlanAbonnement** (Zuora/Chargebee-style Product Catalog, distinct from the pre-existing `PlanRecurrent` billing-cadence-only model) + **AddOnAbonnement**/**AbonnementAddOnLigne** + **PalierUsage**, nullably referenced by `Contrat.plan_abonnement` (`services.appliquer_plan_abonnement` snapshots price/cadence onto the contract at attach time, never a live link). **SequenceDunning**/**EtapeDunning** (NTSUB8) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â configurable multi-step unpaid-invoice dunning sequences, also nullably attached. Endpoints `plans-abonnement/`, `addons-abonnement/`, `addon-lignes/`, `paliers-usage/`, `sequences-dunning/`, `etapes-dunning/`. **PACT138** gives the subscription catalogue its first screen (`/contrats/abonnements`); **PACT139** gives dunning sequences theirs (`/contrats/relances-impayes`).
- **SCA35** adopts the `core.documents` kit's TRANSITIONS read-contract on `Contrat` WITHOUT inheriting `DocumentMetier` itself: `Contrat` keeps its own `company` FK (historical `related_name='contrats'`) and `date_creation` rather than switching to `TenantModel`'s generic accessor (no functional gain, would break the historical accessor); a read-only `TRANSITIONS`/`transitions_permises` property exposes the EXISTING `machine_etats._transitions()` state graph (CONTRAT12) in the kit's expected shape ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `services.changer_statut` (the `machine_etats.changer_statut` alias) stays the one write path, still enforcing the "ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â°Ã‚Â¥2 parties" guard the generic kit doesn't know about. **ARC14** adds `Contrat.custom_data` (JSON), the first `customfields` pilot alongside `flotte.Vehicule`. **ARC28/33/40** add `apps/contrats/platform.py` (`agent_actions_module` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â read-only list + `contrat_voir`; `kpi_providers`).
- **Groupe NTDOC — négociation par redlines.** **DocumentContrepartie** (NTDOC1) + **LienDepotContrepartie** (lien tokenisé de dépôt, dépôt public `apps/contrats/public_urls.py::depot/<token>/`) — la contrepartie externe dépose sa version, comparée au dernier rendu interne (`comparer-contrepartie`). **CommentaireRedline** (NTDOC3) — tous résolus avant clôture de négociation. `Clause.obligatoire_pour_types` (NTDOC5, JSON) + `Contrat.Statut.EN_NEGOCIATION` (NTDOC4). Actions `contrats/{id}/` : `contreparties` (GET/POST), `contreparties/{cid}/comparer`, `contreparties/{cid}/archiver`, `creer-lien-depot`, `demarrer-negociation`, `cloturer-negociation`, `wizard-negociation/etapes`, `clauses-manquantes`, `wizard-clauses-manquantes`, `commentaires-redline`. Migrations contrats/0047-0050.
- **Groupe NTSUB (SaaS/abonnements).** **ParametresAbonnement** (NTSUB24, singleton par société, `parametres-abonnement/`) + **CompteurUsageArchive** (NTSUB26) + **MetriquesSaasCache** (NTSUB27). Migrations contrats/0051-0053. **NTSUB20** adds `pdf_location.render_releve_abonnement_html`/`generate_releve_abonnement_pdf` (`templates/pdf/releve_abonnement.html`) — relevé d'abonnement imprimable (échéances, add-ons, compteurs, paiements) via `contrats/{id}/releve-pdf/`, jamais un PDF devis. Écran `/parametres/abonnements` (`AbonnementsParametresPage.jsx`).
- **Groupe NTDOC (suite, batch 3) — parapheur, déviations, CLM.** **NTDOC7** adds internal e-signature routing: `EtapeApprobation.assigne_a` (nullable FK to `AUTH_USER_MODEL`, `SET_NULL`, migration 0054) puts a pending step into a person's parapheur without restricting who may decide it; `services.assigner_etape` only allows (re)assignment while the step is `en_attente` (logged to the CONTRAT15 chatter), and `selectors.contrats_en_attente_de_parapheur` is the read-only "what's waiting for me" queue. `services.signer_lot_parapheur` batch-signs by reusing `signer_contrat` (CONTRAT16) item-by-item and is **explicitly never all-or-nothing**: each contract is attempted independently outside one enclosing transaction, so a mid-batch failure is reported per-item without rolling back the batch's earlier successes. Actions `contrats/assigner-etape/` + `contrats/parapheur/signer-lot/`.
- **NTDOC6 — déviations de clauses.** `services.detecter_deviations`/`selectors.contrats_en_deviation` compare a contract's `surchargee` (overridden) `ClauseContrat` text against its library source wherever `Clause.obligatoire_pour_types` (NTDOC5) makes that clause mandatory for the contract's `type_contrat`; a genuine divergence feeds the dashboard's additive "Déviations" card (`DeviationsCard`) and `contrats/{id}/deviations/` (per-clause `difflib` diff, library vs contract text).
- **NTDOC20 — préavis par type.** **ParametreRenouvellement** (`type_contrat` + `delai_avant_echeance_jours`, unique per company+type, migration 0055) replaces the single flat 30-day `semer_alertes_echeances` window with a per-type lead time; a type with no row keeps the historical 30-day default.
- **NTDOC29 — ParametresCLM** (singleton per company, migration 0056, `parametres-clm/courant/` GET/PATCH, lazily created on first access): `resolution_commentaires_obligatoire` (NTDOC4's strict redline guard, default on), `negociation_obligatoire_avant_signature` (default off), `duree_defaut_expiration_salle_donnees_jours` (default 30), `parapheur_notification_quotidienne` (default off, unwired to any beat yet).
- **NTDOC18 — matrice des obligations.** `Obligation.preuve_document` (nullable FK to `ged.Document`, `SET_NULL`, string-FK style — no `ged.models` import, migration 0057); an obligation marked done with no proof attached stays fully valid, never blocking. `selectors.matrice_obligations` / `contrats/{id}/matrice-obligations/` renders the redevable×statut compliance matrix.
- **NTDOC19 — pipeline de renouvellements** (no model). `selectors.pipeline_renouvellements` / `contrats/pipeline-renouvellement/` groups the quarter's renewals by month, reusing `contrats_a_renouveler` (CONTRAT21) as its only data source; flags `preavis_depasse` when a contract's notice deadline (`date_fin − preavis_jours`, CONTRAT20) has passed and `preavis_traite` is still false. Contract sample `contract_samples/pipeline_renouvellement.json` (PACT10) fixes the aggregate's shape before server and Kanban screen diverge. Parapheur/matrice-obligations/pipeline-renouvellement/ParametresCLM are backend-only so far — only Déviations has a wired screen. Not found under any name in `apps/contrats`: sections conditionnelles dans les gabarits, et un type/clause « TSA secours » — ni l'un ni l'autre n'est construit.

### datarooms — salles de données sécurisées (viewer tokenisé, filigrane, journal) — new app, Groupe NTDOC  *( `/api/django/datarooms/` )*
Une **SalleDeDonnees** (NTDOC11) est une collection thématique de documents GED déjà existants — jamais une copie ni un déplacement : `SalleDeDonneesDocument` (`salle`, `document` string-FK vers `ged.Document`, `ordre`, `visible`) est une simple table de liaison, lue via `apps.ged.selectors` (jamais un import de `ged.models`).
- **SalleDeDonnees** — `nom`, `description`, `dossier_source` (FK `ged.Folder` optionnelle), `deal_type` (texte libre), `statut` (`ouverte`/`fermee`), `expires_at` (expiration globale, évaluée à la lecture). **NTDOC15** adds `source_type` (`lead`/`chantier`/`contrat`) + `source_id` (string-ref, lien retour depuis la fiche source) and the `creer-depuis-source/` action. **NTDOC16** adds `fermee_le`/`fermee_par` — fermer une salle révoque TOUS ses accès viewer d'un coup, non réversible (seule une réouverture admin la rend exploitable ; les viewers doivent être réinvités).
- **AccesSalleDonnees** (**NTDOC12**) — un viewer NOMMÉ par salle : `nom`, `email`, `token` (unique, `secrets.token_urlsafe(32)`, même générateur que `ged.PartageGed.token`), `expires_at` PAR VIEWER (distincte de l'expiration globale de la salle), `revoque`, `derniere_consultation`. Le jeton est l'UNIQUE secret de l'endpoint public — aucune identité/société n'est jamais lue de la requête.
- **NTDOC13** — le document servi au viewer public est filigrané À LA VOLÉE avec son nom/email/horodatage (`services.watermark_label_viewer` + `ged.services.apply_watermark`, GED21) ; le binaire GED n'est jamais modifié. **NTDOC14** — chaque consultation est journalisée en réutilisant `ged.services.journaliser_acces` (GED35, aucun nouveau modèle de journal), taguée `source_ref="datarooms.acces:<id>"`. **NTDOC16** — `export-audit/` génère un PDF de gouvernance interne (documents + viewers + journal complet).
- Endpoints (authentifiés, `datarooms_voir`/`datarooms_gerer`) — `salles/` (+ `creer-depuis-source/`, `documents/`, `ajouter-documents/`, `retirer-document/`, `fermer/`, `rouvrir/`, `journal/` avec `?format=csv`, `export-audit/`), `salle-documents/`, `acces/` (+ `revoquer/`). Public (AllowAny, throttled 30/min, `X-Robots-Tag: noindex`) — `public/<token>/` (sommaire salle + documents visibles) et `public/<token>/documents/<document_id>/` (document filigrané, 404 jeton/document introuvable, 410 expiré/salle fermée).

### grc — governance, risk & compliance / GRC & Privacy (Groupe NTGRC, new app)  *( `/api/django/grc/` )*
Satellite app (`module_manifest`: key `grc`, category Technique, no hard `depends`) that EXTENDS the RGPD/loi 09-08 foundation already in `core` (`core.RegistreTraitement`/`core.ConsentRecord`/`core.DataSubjectRequest` + the `core.dsr`/`core.retention` registries) rather than duplicating it — `grc` reads every other app only via its `selectors.py`/`services.py` or a text `*_ref` string-FK, never a model import. `GrcConfig.ready()` wires two cross-app hooks: `services.register_erasure_guard()` registers `grc_legal_hold` on `core.dsr` (**NTGRC8** — a person under an active legal hold can't be erased, even on a legitimate rights request) and `receivers.py` subscribes to the `core.events` bus (`lead_created`/`devis_accepted`) to alert the DPO when a person whose consent was withdrawn is treated again (**NTGRC9**) — `grc` never imports `crm`/`ventes`.
- **NTGRC1-3 — data-subject rights (DSR), spread across apps.** The DSR machine itself (`core.DataSubjectRequest`: 30-day legal deadline, identity-verification step, illegal-transition guard) lives in `core` (foundation); `crm`/`stock`/`ventes` each ship a `dsr_provider.py` (**NTGRC1**) that `core.dsr` aggregates for the real erasure/anonymisation. `grc` owns the PUBLIC intake: `public_views.deposer_demande_droit`/`suivre_demande_droit` (**NTGRC2**, `POST`/`GET /api/django/grc/public/demande-droit/[<token>]/`, `AllowAny` + `AnonRateThrottle` 10/h and 60/h, honeypot field, server-side IP/UA proof, opaque `token_suivi`) let anyone deposit or track a rights request without an account, resolved to a `Company` by its public `slug` — never an internal id.
- **PolitiqueRetentionObjet** (**NTGRC4**, `politiques-retention-objet/`) — a per-`type_objet` (lead/client/facture/ticket SAV/ligne d'audit) retention duration in months + `action_echeance` (signaler/anonymiser/archiver), unique per company+type; it parametrizes the `core.retention` sweeps that business apps run themselves — `grc` says WHAT and HOW LONG, never executes a delete. **JournalDestruction** (**NTGRC5**, `journal-destruction/`) is an APPEND-ONLY log — its `save()`/`delete()` raise `JournalDestructionError` (403, no PUT/PATCH/DELETE on the viewset) — written by `services.journaliser_destruction`, called by the DSR providers and by retention sweeps at every real purge/anonymise/archive; it stores only a type, a technical id, a motif and a SHA-256 `empreinte_avant` fingerprint, never the personal data itself.
- **ViolationDonnees** (**NTGRC6**, `violations-donnees/`, reference prefix `VD`, race-safe numbering via `core.numbering`) is the REGULATORY breach register (loi 09-08/RGPD) — distinct from `qhse` risks and from `IncidentSecurite`: `date_echeance_72h` is stamped once from `date_detection` and never recomputed, and `services.changer_statut_violation`/`notifier_cndp` gate the ouverte→en_analyse→notifiee→cloturee lifecycle (terminal; illegal transitions → 400). Actions `echeance-depassee/`, `changer-statut/`, `notifier-cndp/`, and **NTGRC7**'s `generer-dossier-notification/` (GET/POST) which renders the 8 mandatory CNDP-notification rubrics to PDF via `core.pdf.render_pdf` (never the quote engine — rule #4) and fires `core.events.document_pdf_generated`. **LegalHold** (**NTGRC8**, `legal-holds/` + `objets-sous-hold/`) is the TRANSVERSE séquestre beyond `ged.LegalHold`'s single-document scope: a JSON `perimetre` of `{type_objet, filtre}` resolved by target-app selectors, `est_actif()` gating on `statut` + date window, and `motif_hold_pour_personne` is the function registered as the `core.dsr` erasure guard.
- **Enterprise risk (ERM) — NTGRC13-15.** **RisqueEntreprise** (`risques-entreprise/` + `matrice/?residuelle=1`, reference `RQ`) carries BOTH inherent and residual probability×impact (1-5 grid, recalculated server-side on every save, never trusted from the client) — distinct from `qhse.EvaluationRisque` (workplace H&S). **PlanTraitementRisque** (`plans-traitement-risque/` + `en-retard/`) is the assigned/dated remediation action per risk, its lateness always recomputed from `echeance`, never read off a stale `statut`. **RevueRisque** (`revues-risque/` + `risques-a-revoir/`) is a periodic-review journal that pushes `prochaine_revue` back onto the risk from the service layer.
- **Internal controls (SOX-lite) — NTGRC16-18.** **ControleInterne** (`controles-internes/`, unique `code` per company, domaine/type/frequence + a `FENETRE_JOURS` freshness window per frequency) describes a control with NO result; **TestControle** (`tests-controle/` + `controles-a-tester/`) records one execution (échantillon, conclusion, `piece_preuve_key` — a MinIO/GED storage key, never the file) and, when `resultat='deficient'`, can open a `risque_ouvert` FK back onto `RisqueEntreprise`. **DeficienceControle** (`deficiences-controle/`) is the auditor-facing finding (gravité/remédiation/échéance) linked to a risk and to a `qhse.ActionCorrectivePreventive` by TEXT ref only.
- **Internal policies & proof-of-read — NTGRC19-21.** **PolitiqueInterne** (`politiques-internes/`) is a live draft until `publier/` (action) freezes it into an immutable **PolitiqueVersion** snapshot (`versions/`; its `save()`/`delete()` raise `PolitiqueVersionError`) and server-increments `version`. **AttestationPolitique** (`attestations-politique/` + self-service `attester/`, open to any `IsAuthenticated` user) is the loi-53-05 proof of an employee reading one specific FROZEN version — server-stamped timestamp/IP/user-agent, a typed `nom_saisi` as the signature gesture, unique per (politique, version, employe_ref) so a double-click never inflates the attestation rate; `employe_ref` string-FKs `rh.DossierEmploye`. **NTGRC21**'s `manage.py relancer_attestations` command (+ `selectors.attestations_manquantes`) nudges whoever hasn't attested the current published version, deduped per (person, policy, version, day) — sharing its dedup key with NTGRC34's daily sweep.
- **Supplier compliance questionnaires — NTGRC22-24.** **QuestionnaireFournisseur** (`questionnaires-fournisseur/`, type sécurité/rgpd/qualité/rse) + **ReponseQuestionnaire** (`reponses-questionnaire/`, `conforme` a NULLABLE 3-state boolean so unanswered ≠ non-conforme) is the art.-28/loi-09-08 subprocessor-diligence trail; `services.recalculer_questionnaire` derives the `score` (% conforme) and auto-advances envoyé→en_cours→complété server-side, never rétrograding a validé/refusé verdict. **ModeleQuestionnaire** (`modeles-questionnaire/`) is a reusable JSON question trame that `instancier_questionnaire` COPIES (never references) into each new questionnaire. **NTGRC24** adds a no-account public portal: `lien-public/` (action) issues/renews an opaque `token_acces` (renewing INVALIDATES the old link) and `public_views.questionnaire_public` (`GET`/`POST /api/django/grc/public/questionnaire/<token>/`, throttled 30/h) lets the supplier answer directly.
- **IncidentSecurite** (**NTGRC25**, `incidents-securite/`, reference `INC`) is the SECURITY incident register — deliberately distinct from `ViolationDonnees` (a lost encrypted laptop is an incident with no breach; a misdirected email is a breach with no incident). `services.changer_statut_incident` drives ouvert→en_cours→résolu→clos (terminal), and per **NTGRC26** every transition is auto-logged plus manual `noter/` notes into the PLATFORM chatter (`records.Activity` via `ChatterViewSetMixin`/`records.services.log_activity`/`log_note` — no fourteenth homegrown `*Activity` model), exposed both generically (`chatter/historique|noter`) and under NTGRC26's own `historique/`/`noter/` action names. `escalader-violation/` (action) creates the linked `ViolationDonnees` (idempotent — an already-escalated incident refuses a second one) once personal data is actually confirmed touched.
- **AnalyseImpactDPIA** (**NTGRC27**, `analyses-dpia/` + `traitements-sans-dpia/`) is the art.-35 impact assessment for a `core.RegistreTraitement` line (string `traitement_ref`): `necessite_dpia=False` is a tracked, assumed decision (never a blank), and `services.valider_dpia` refuses to validate without atténuation measures, or — when `risque_residuel='eleve'` — without a DPO `avis_dpo`. **FluxDonnees** (**NTGRC30**, `flux-donnees/` + `hors-maroc/`) maps WHERE data actually goes (vs. the RoPA's WHY): `transfert_hors_maroc` is an asserted flag, never inferred from a country string, and each flow can carry a `garanties` safeguard (CCT/adéquation/dérogation).
- **DPO cockpit, compliance score, e-discovery & audit dossier — NTGRC28-32.** `GET tableau-bord-dpo/` (**NTGRC28**) returns SEVEN read-only counters in one call (open DSRs + days remaining, un-notified 72h violations, this month's consent withdrawals, un-attested policies, controls due, critical residual risks, DPIA-missing treatments) — deliberately no personal data, only counts/deadlines. `GET score-conformite/` (**NTGRC29**) computes a weighted 0-100 maturity score across six criteria (RoPA 20/contrôles 20/DSR 15/politiques 15/violations 15/DPIA 15), each carrying a real numerator/denominator in its `commentaire` so a bad score is actionable, not just discouraging. `GET/POST e-discovery/` (**NTGRC31**) searches a CLOSED set of scopes (`crm_client`/`crm_lead`/`audit_log`/`ged_document` — the last ACL-respecting, user-scoped) via each target's own `selectors.py`, and a `POST` carrying a `legal_hold` object can seal the gelable results into a new `LegalHold` in the same call. `GET dossier-conformite/` (**NTGRC32**) streams a timestamped ZIP of six CSV exports (RoPA, risk register, control tests, published policies, destruction journal, violations) plus a SHA-256 manifest with a reproducible `empreinte_globale` — never a `Produit.prix_achat`, never raw personal data.
- **CadreConformite**/**ExigenceCadre** (**NTGRC33**, `cadres-conformite/` + `couverture/`/`exigences/` actions, `exigences-cadre/`) is a multi-framework registry (ISO 27001, loi 09-08, RGPD, SOX allégé, ISO 27701) mapping each requirement to a `ControleInterne` by string ref, with THREE coverage states (couvert/partiel/non_couvert — `selectors.taux_couverture` reports both a strict `taux_pct` and a partial-credited `taux_avec_partiel_pct`, deliberately named apart so nobody quotes one for the other). **NTGRC34**'s Celery-Beat task `grc.rappels_grc` (`tasks.py`, relaying `manage.py rappels_grc`) sweeps five deadline families daily (DSRs, 72h violations, risk reviews, control tests, policy attestations), idempotent per (recipient, deep-link, day). **SousTraitantRGPD** (**NTGRC35**, `sous-traitants-rgpd/` + `sans-clause/`) is the art.-28 subprocessor list (finalités, catégories de données, localisation, `clause_signee`), string-linked to `stock.Fournisseur` and to its `QuestionnaireFournisseur` diligence record.
- All list/detail viewsets are `IsAdminOrResponsableTier`-gated except the self-service `attester/` action (`IsAuthenticated`) and the three unauthenticated `public/` endpoints. No frontend screens exist for `grc` yet — the app is backend-only as of this batch.

### kb ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â knowledge base (KB1, new app)  *( `/api/django/kb/` )*
- **KbArticle** (`company`-scoped; `statut` brouillon/publie/obsolete). **KB2** adds **KbArticleVersion** (`company` + `article` FK `related_name='versions'`; server-incremented `version` via `select_for_update` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â never count()+1; `titre`/`contenu`/`auteur` snapshot). Actions `articles/{id}/publier/` (statutÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢publie + snapshot) and `articles/{id}/nouvelle-version/`; a version is also snapshotted on every article update. Read-only `versions/` viewset (company-scoped, `?article=` filter). **KB3** adds full-text-ish search (`?search=` over titre/corps/categorie/tags) + `?categorie=`/`?tag=`/`?statut=` filters on the article viewset, applied after company scoping (no cross-tenant leak; reuses existing fields, no migration). **KB4** adds **KbArticleLien** (string-FK produit/equipement/type_intervention, like ContratLien) with selector enrichment (produit via `stock.selectors`; others degrade) + a reverse lookup `article-liens/articles/?type_cible=&cible_id=`. **KB7** adds **KbArticleAcl** (role-tier ACL, niveau lecture/edition) + **KbLecture** (read tracking). `selectors.visible_articles_qs` filters the article queryset by ACL ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â **backward-compatible: an article with no ACL row stays visible to all, admin always sees all**; `marquer-lu` (idempotent) + `resume-lecture` actions + ACL management viewset `article-acls/`. Migration kb/0005. **NTMIG-playbooks** adds `KbArticle.type_article` + `contenu_structure` (JSON playbook steps, mirrored on `KbArticleVersion`) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â the source a `migration.PlaybookInstance` snapshots at instantiation; selectors `phases_playbook(article)`/`playbooks_qs(company)`.
- **NTSRV18/20 — suggestions dans le ticket SAV.** `selectors.articles_pour_contexte(company, categorie_ticket=…)` (lecture, sert le contexte ticket sans import `apps.sav`) + action `creer-depuis-ticket` (crée un brouillon d'article à partir d'un ticket résolu). Compteurs KCS additifs sur **KbArticle** : `nb_vues_depuis_ticket` (ouverture depuis une suggestion contexte-ticket) et `nb_resolutions_attribuees` (agent coche « cet article a résolu le ticket », câblage côté `apps.sav`) — distincts de `vues`, incrémentés via `F()`.

### litiges ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â disputes / claims (LITIGE1, new app)  *( `/api/django/litiges/` )*
- **Reclamation** (`company`-scoped; `statut` ouverte/en_traitement/resolue/rejetee). **LITIGE2** adds a server-enforced state machine (actions `prendre-en-charge`/`resoudre`/`rejeter`, illegal transitions ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ 400; statut read-only outside actions) plus a chatter **ReclamationActivity** (`company` + `reclamation` FK; `type` log/note, `old_value`/`new_value`/`message`/`auteur`) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â auto-logs each transition and manual notes via `noter/`; timeline via `historique/`. Acting user + company always server-side. **LITIGE6** adds `selectors.tableau_bord_litiges(company, debut, fin)` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a disputes dashboard aggregating existing Reclamation data: counts by statut, total `montant_conteste`, and average resolution delay (from the `resolue` chatter log, divide-by-zero guarded ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ None), at `reclamations/tableau-bord/`. No model.

### adsengine ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â Meta Ads autonomous engine (Groupe ENG/ADSENG, new app)  *( `/api/django/adsengine/` )*
An in-ERP ads engine that runs Meta (Facebook/Instagram) campaigns on the company's own behalf ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â guardrail-gated proposeÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢approveÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢apply, never a black box. **Company-scoped and OFF by default**: with no `MetaConnection` row the whole app is inert (no sync, no spend, no writes). **Every campaign the engine creates is born `PAUSED`** (`meta_client.py`, rule #3) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â nothing goes live without a human flipping it.
- **Models** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `MetaConnection` (per-company Meta creds/ad-account, encrypted at rest), `GuardrailConfig` (spend/CPA/frequency ceilings gating every write), `EngineAction` (the proposeÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢approveÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢apply spine ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â every mutating step is a row here first), `EngineAlert` (guardian-detected anomalies), `CreativeAsset` + `CreativePolicy` (ad creative library + compliance rules), `AdCampaignMirror`/`AdSetMirror`/`AdMirror` (read-mirror of live Meta objects), `InsightSnapshot` (daily performance pull), `WeeklyBrief` (auto-generated FR ops summary), `Experiment`/`ExperimentArm`/`ArmDailyStat`/`DecisionLog` (A/B test + bandit decision trail), `RulePolicy`/`AnomalyEvent`/`PacingState` (rules-engine + budget pacing), `CreativeGenerationBatch`/`CreativeBacklogItem` (creative production queue), `FlightPlan`/`FlightPhase` (launch sequencing + preflight), `ReconciliationSnapshot` (Meta-vs-ERP spend/lead reconciliation); **ADSDEEP P0-P3**: `InsightBreakdown` (age_gender/platform/region/hourly demographics), `AdCreativeMirror` (per-ad creative body/video/image/preview mirror), `MetaLeadMirror` (leads-per-ad, fed by the `meta_lead_captured` domain event + pull-sync, phone-key attribution to Odoo signatures per ad). `InsightSnapshot` gained typed columns (impressions/reach/clicks/link_clicks/conversations/leads_count/video_metrics). **ASG/AGEN/SIG (Assumption Engine)**: `AssumptionNode` (the living belief tree ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â per-class weekly-forgetting Beta posteriors ÃƒÅ½Ã‚Â±/ÃƒÅ½Ã‚Â² + prior ÃƒÅ½Ã‚Â±ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã¢â€šÂ¬/ÃƒÅ½Ã‚Â²ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Å¡Ã¢â€šÂ¬, half-lives 8/13/26 wk, parent + `invalidation_links` DAG, season tags, statut assumed/testing/validated/stale/retired), `FactTable`/`FactEntry` (versioned brouillon/publiÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©e checked-facts table ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â the ONLY source of publishable numbers for grounded generation, one published table/company), plus audit columns on `CreativeGenerationBatch` (fact_table_version/claim_verdicts/template_quarantined) and fixed health-score weights on `GuardrailConfig`.
- **Key modules** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `meta_client.py` (the PAUSED-only Meta Marketing API client); `services.py` (the proposeÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢approveÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢apply `EngineAction` spine, guardrail-gated dispatch); `guardrails.py`; decision engine (`bandit.py` Thompson beta-binomial, `allocation.py`, `rewards.py`, `authority.py`, `mde.py`); guardian (`rules_engine.py`, `anomaly.py`, `rule_templates.py`, `watchdog.py`, `alerts.py`); treasury (`pacing.py`, `budget_applier.py`, `identity.py`, `launch_templates.py`); creative (`rotation.py`, `recombine.py`, `backlog.py`, `flightplan.py`, `dco.py`, `creative_factory.py`); attribution (`reconciliation.py`, `capi_crm.py`, `reporting.py`, `attribution.py`, `metrics.py`);
