### cpq ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â Configure-Price-Quote enterprise (Groupe NTCPQ, new app)  *( `/api/django/cpq/` )*
Satellite app UPSTREAM of the quote PDF ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â models/rules/config only, NEVER renders a client PDF (rule #4: the premium engine at `apps/ventes/quote_engine` + `/proposal` stays the only client-quote path). String-FK to ventes/stock/crm; company-scoped.
- **Models** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `OptionProduit`/`ContrainteCompatibilite` (product option groups + INCOMPATIBLE/REQUIERT/RECOMMANDE constraints, NTCPQ1), `RegleProduitCPQ` (product rules evaluated through `core.rules.evaluate_condition_group`, NTCPQ2; **NTCPQ21** adds `bloquante` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a triggered rule is a warning badge by default, only a `bloquante=True` rule invalidates the configuration), `OffreGroupee`/`LigneOffreGroupee` (cascaded-price bundles, NTCPQ3), `PrixContractuel` (per-client/product negotiated price, priority-1 in `prix-applicable`, NTCPQ5), `SeuilMargeFamille` (min-margin guard per category ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â INTERNAL flag `marge_sous_seuil`, never client-facing, NTCPQ6), `RegleApprobationRemise`/`EtapeApprobationDevis` (discount-depth approval matrix modelled on `contrats.RegleApprobation`, NTCPQ7), `QuestionConfigurateur`/`SessionConfigurateur`/`ReponseConfigurateur` (guided configurator, NTCPQ9), `ClauseCGV` (dynamic deal-type clauses snapshotted onto `Devis.clauses_appliquees` at send, NTCPQ11), `ProduitEquivalent` (per-tier ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©conomique/standard/premium product-substitution rule powering the variant generator, NTCPQ16), `ParametresCPQ` (NTCPQ30, per-company CPQ settings singleton, OneToOne `company`, extends `core.models.TenantModel`).
- **reports.py** (NTCPQ19/22/23/24) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a module SEPARATE from `selectors.py` on purpose (avoids reopening the `ventes.models ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ cpq.selectors ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ ventes.selectors` import cycle); reads `ventes` only via `apps.ventes.selectors`. `suggestions_produit` (cross-sell/upsell, max 3, `RECOMMANDE` constraints then co-purchase frequency, purely suggestive), `feuille_configuration` (internal technical config sheet, never client-facing), `marge_sous_seuil` dashboard (staff), `rapport_conformite` (config compliance-rate report).
- Endpoints: `valider-compatibilite/`, `regles/` (+`evaluer/`), `offres-groupees/{id}/appliquer/`, `prix-contractuels/`, `clauses-cgv/` (NTCPQ12), `produits-equivalents/` (NTCPQ16), `devis/{id}/variantes/` (NTCPQ16, generates the tier variant devis), `devis/{id}/feuille-configuration/` (NTCPQ22), `suggestions/` (NTCPQ19), `marge-sous-seuil/` (NTCPQ23), `rapports/conformite/` (NTCPQ24), `parametres-cpq/` (NTCPQ30, singleton ViewSet), `prix-contractuels/import-csv/` (NTCPQ41), `rapports/approbations/` (NTCPQ25, discount-approval history), `rapports/catalogue-regles/` (NTCPQ42, read-only compatibility-rule catalogue export), `devis/{id}/comparaison-variantes/` (NTCPQ26, INTERNAL variant comparison sheet, never client-facing), `devis/{id}/relancer-approbation/` (NTCPQ28), `configurateur/demarrer|{s}/repondre|{s}/resultat|{s}/generer-devis/`. Devis-side actions `approuver-etape/`/`rejeter-etape/` live in `apps/ventes/views.py` (call `cpq.services`).
- **PACT118** adds `apps/cpq/bulk_targets.py`, registered from `CpqConfig.ready()`: the generic `core.bulk_edit` registry (`GET core/bulk-edit/targets/`, `POST core/bulk-edit/appliquer/`) previously had **zero registered targets in production** (only the socle's own tests called `register_bulk_target`). CPQ is the first real caller, declaring 3 company-scoped targets with narrow field whitelists (never a price field) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `cpq.offre-groupee` (`actif`), `cpq.question-configurateur` (`actif`/`ordre`), `cpq.contrainte-compatibilite` (`type`/`message_utilisateur`).

### territoires ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â territory assignment engine (Groupe NTCRM, new app)  *( `/api/django/territoires/` )*
Sits ABOVE the existing round-robin (XSAL11): a lead is routed by territory rules first, else falls back byte-identically. No crm-model imports (function-local reads + string-FK).
- **Models** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `Territoire` (GEO/SEGMENT/SECTEUR + criteria JSON), `TerritoireRegle` (priority-ordered condition via `core.rules`, action = assign to team/user), `TerritoireMembre` (member + race-safe rotation counter, quota). `services.assigner_lead_territoire` (select_for_update, never a modulo on count()).
- Endpoints: `territoires/` CRUD, `territoires/{id}/resoudre/?lead_id=` (non-mutating preview), `couverture/?jours=30` (**NTCRM25**, `selectors.rapport_couverture` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â recent leads via `crm.selectors.leads_recents_pour_couverture` matching NO active territory rule, counted par rÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©gion/segment; read-only, Responsable/Admin, no rotation side effect). Frontend `TerritoryCoverageWidget.jsx`.

### contacts ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â multi-role client contacts (Groupe NTCRM, new app)  *( `/api/django/contacts/` )*
Additive org-chart of buying contacts per client; string-FK to `crm.Client`, keeps Client's existing single-contact fields (implicit principal when none exists).
- **Models** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `ContactClient` (nom/poste/email/tel/whatsapp, `role_achat` DECIDEUR/INFLUENCEUR/UTILISATEUR/GATEKEEPER/SPONSOR, single-principal guard via `clean()`).
- Endpoints: `contacts-client/` CRUD (filter `?client=`).

### onboarding ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â product onboarding Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â« Premiers pas Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â» (Groupe NTDMO, new app)  *( `/api/django/onboarding/` )*
Light app: global checklist catalogue + company-scoped progress, auto-completed via the `core.events` bus (never a cross-app model import).
- **Models** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `OnboardingChecklistItem` (stable key, target role, order, target screen), `OnboardingProgress` (company+user+item, `complete_le`/`ignore_le`, unique together). **NTDMO14** adds `ProductTourStep` (catalogue of Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â« product tour Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â» spotlight steps ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `tour_key`+`ordre`, CSS `selecteur`, `ecran_cible`; `company` NULL = global catalogue entry, same socle divergence as `OnboardingChecklistItem`) + `TourProgress` (per-user completion, `vu_le` NULL = never shown/finished; only an explicit Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â« Revoir Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â», NTDMO16, resets it). **NTDMO28** adds `OnboardingChecklistItem.masque_pour` (M2MÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢`authentication.Company`) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â additive per-company hiding of a global catalogue item, never deletes it for other companies.
- **Wiring** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `receivers.py` subscribes in `apps.py::ready()` to existing domain signals (devis_sent/devis_accepted/facture_payee, **WIR59** adds `intervention_completed` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ the Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â« Suivre votre 1er chantier Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â» item, which previously had an empty `event_key` and could never auto-complete) to auto-tick items.
- Endpoints: `onboarding/progress/` (list, `{id}/ignorer/`, `ignorer-tout/`); `onboarding/tours/` (NTDMO14, catalogue + per-user vu/non-vu in one call) + `{tour_key}/vu/` (idempotent) + `{tour_key}/revoir/` (NTDMO16 reset); `onboarding/items-masques/` (NTDMO28, + `masquer/`/`demasquer/`). Frontend `components/ProductTour.jsx` (spotlight player) + `pages/parametres` Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â« Visites guidÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©es Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â» block (revoir button per tour).

### agriculture ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â farms, plots, campaigns, agronomic inputs (NTAGR*)  *( `/api/django/agriculture/` )*
Vertical agribusiness. **NTAGR1** adds **Exploitation** (farm master) + **Parcelle** (cultivable plot: surface/GPS geometry/crop/soil/statut). **NTAGR2** adds **CampagneCulturale** (sowingÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢harvest cycle per plot: culture/variety/dates/yield/statut) with `clean()` guard: one plot ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â°Ã‚Â¤1 EN_COURS campaign at a time. **NTAGR3/NTAGR6** adds **EtapeCampagne** (dated campaign steps: semis/traitement/irrigation/dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©sherbage/fertilisation/rÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©colte; cost + optional intrant link) + `check_dar_guard()` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â blocking pre-harvest-delay (DAR) compliance on phyto treatments (ONSSA conformity, lÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¨ve `ValidationError` if traitement applies within DAR days of harvest date, never silent). **NTAGR5** adds **IntrantAgricole** (agronomic attributes on stock.Produit: catÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©gorie semence/engrais/phyto, dose/ha, DAR jours, matiÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¨re active/AMM ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â produit_id string-ref to stock, unique, never import stock.models). **NTAGR9** adds seasonal labour: **EquipeSaisonniere** (team) + **PointageAgricole** (daily timesheet: equipe/travailleur_nom/campagne/parcelle/date/tÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢che/journÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©es/taux ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â optional employe_id string-ref to rh.DossierEmploye; informative only, no payroll effect). All `company`-scoped. ViewSets `exploitations/`, `parcelles/`, `campagnes/`, `etapes-campagne/`, `intrants-agricoles/`, `equipes-saisonnieres/`, `pointages/`, `lots-recolte/`. **WIR141** exposes the pre-existing NTAGR14 irrigation-cost selectors (`cout_irrigation_campagne`, `volume_irrigation_solaire_campagne` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â until now with no REST caller) at `campagnes/{id}/cout-irrigation/` (paid-irrigation cost + solar-pumped volume over the campaign window). **LotRecolte** (NTAGR15/16, previously undocumented here) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a harvest batch tied to `stock` (never a second lot-tracking system), traced via `stock.selectors.trace_serie(company, numero_lot=...)`. **PACT79** gives it its first screen (`/agriculture/lots-recolte`) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â the 12th agricultural resource, until then the only one with neither route nor menu entry.

### btp_chantier ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â BTP/EPC vertical: site issues, requests, document approvals, journals  *( `/api/django/btp-chantier/` )*
Project-execution traceback for EPC/BTP contractors. **NTCON1** adds **ReserveChantier** (site issue/finding: gÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©o-located on plan, statut, severity, photo/document refs). **NTCON2** adds **RFI** (request-for-info: dated, destinataire, topic, response statut). **NTCON3** adds **VisaDocument** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â technical document approval workflow tied to ged.DocumentVersion: statut en_attente/visÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©/rouvert, signataire, `clean()` enforces visa prerequisite before document advance (ged.Statut brouillonÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢revue path). **NTCON5** auto-opens expired visas on ged new version (via a receiver wired in `apps.ready()`, function-local `apps.get_model('ged.DocumentVersion')`, never imports ged.models). **NTCON4** adds **JournalChantier** (site journal entries: date/auteur/ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©vÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©nement/photo). All `company`-scoped, string-refs to installations.Chantier (`chantier_id`, never imports installations.models). ViewSets `reserves-chantier/`, `rfi/`, `visas/`, `journal-chantier/`.
- **Groupe NTCON14-25 — lots + réception + sécurité.** **Lot**/**LotTache** (NTCON14, `lots/`) découpent un chantier en lots planifiables ; `lots/{id}/checklist/` (NTCON19, **LotChecklistItem**) gate la réception d'un lot derrière une checklist 100 % cochée. **PPSPSChantier**/**PPSPSSignature** (NTCON16, `ppsps/`) — plan particulier de sécurité et de protection de la santé, signable. **AbonnementRapportPhoto** (NTCON18, `abonnements-rapport-photo/`) — abonnement au photo-rapport hebdomadaire, envoyé par le beat Celery `btp_chantier.rapport_photo_hebdo`. **ParametresBtpChantier** (NTCON25, singleton `parametres/`). Endpoints additionnels : `chantiers/<id>/{cloture-btp,rapport-avancement,export-dossier-btp,intervenants,penalites-par-lot,planning-lots}/`. Migrations btp_chantier/0007-0011. Écrans `/btp-chantier/{cockpit,cockpit/:chantierId,nouveau,cloture,planning-lots,parametres}` (`ChantierBtpCockpit`/`NouveauChantierBtpWizard`/`ClotureChantierBtpWizard`/`PlanningLots`/`ParametresBtp`).
- **Groupe NTCON26-37 — permissions fines, archivage, cache pénalités, import/export, webhooks, chatter, KPI, capture terrain.** **NTCON26** adds `permissions.py`: six fine action codes (`btp_reserve_creer`/`_lever`, `btp_rfi_repondre`, `btp_visa_approuver`, `btp_avenant_approuver`, `btp_dgd_finaliser`) enforced on top of `btp_voir`/`btp_gerer`; legacy accounts without a fine Role keep access via the platform's `_user_has_or_legacy`. **NTCON27** adds `ReserveChantier.archivee`/`archivee_le` (flag, never delete — a levée reserve carries proof/signature): `selectors.reserves_archivables` (per-company threshold, `ParametresBtpChantier.delai_archivage_reserves_levees_mois`, default 24 months) + `services.archiver_reserves_levees` + a daily beat; archived rows drop out of default lists but stay reachable via `?archivee=`. Migration `0012_ntcon27_archivage_reserves`. **NTCON28** adds `Lot.penalite_calculee_cache`/`penalite_calculee_le` (denormalized snapshot of the NTCON15 penalty calc): `selectors.penalites_par_lot_cache_ou_calcul` serves the cache when fresher than 36h else recalculates; `chantiers/{id}/penalites-par-lot/` GET (cache-or-calc, `?frais=1` bypasses)/POST (forced recalc), reporting `source: 'cache'|'calcul'`. Migration `0013_ntcon28_penalite_cache`. **NTCON29** adds `lots/import/` (multipart CSV/XLSX, reusing `apps.dataimport`, tolerant header aliasing). **NTCON30** adds `reserves-chantier/export/` + `rfi/export/` (CSV/XLSX) for MOE/client reporting, excluding all internal cost fields. **NTCON31** adds a read-only public-API scope `read:btp` (reserves/RFI/visas/DGD, no cost data) plus four `core.events` signals (`btp_reserve_levee`, `btp_rfi_repondu`, `btp_visa_approuve`, `btp_dgd_finalise`) emitted best-effort, consumed by `apps.publicapi` for webhooks — never a `publicapi` import from this app. **NTCON32** wires the generic chatter (`records.Activity`, ARC8) onto ReserveChantier/RFI/VisaDocument/AvenantChantier/DecompteGeneral (`historique/`+`noter/`); every status transition also logs to `audit.AuditLog` (best-effort). **NTCON33** routes BTP notifications through `notifications.resolve_recipients`/`notify_many` (falls back to active company managers) instead of a hardcoded recipient. **NTCON34** adds `selectors.kpis_btp` (open reserves/late RFI/pending visas/cumulative penalty exposure — `None`, never a misleading `0`) wired into `apps.reporting.kpi_alertes` for threshold alerting (migration `reporting/0020_ntcon34_kpi_btp`). **NTCON36** registers two offline op-types (`btp.reserve.creer`, `btp.journal.entree_du_jour`) in the generic `apps.offlinesync` engine (NTMOB1) — last-write-wins, photos upload separately via `records.Attachment`; first screen `/btp-chantier/terrain` (`pages/btp/TerrainCapture.jsx`). **NTCON37** adds `VisaDocument.derniere_relance_retard` (same one-relance-per-day pattern as RFI's NTCON4) reminding both the assigned reviewer and their manager. Migration `0014_ntcon37_relance_visas`. **NTCON35** (e2e réserve→levée→export test) has no code — flagged `[BLOCKED]` in `docs/plans/PLAN_VERTICALS.md`: it surfaced a real product bug (no photo-upload for the "après" step `lever/` requires) routed to ERROR_PLAN.

### esg ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â ESG/sustainability reporting (cross-app, liÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© qhse via selectors)  *( `/api/django/esg/` )*
ESG/durability consolidated reporting (GRI-lite framework, aggregation, target tracking). Multi-app reader (qhse, rh, stock, installations raw indicators via function-local selectors ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â never imports their models). **NTESG1** adds **PeriodeReportingESG** (frozen reporting period: annÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©e, trimestre/semestre/annÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©e, `date_debut/fin`, scope perimeter, statut brouillon/finalisee/archivee). **NTESG2** adds **CatalogueIndicateurESG** (GRI-lite catalogue: code, libelle, unite, scope S/E/G, formula description ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â company master copy per scope). **NTESG3** adds **ObjectifESGTrajectoire** (target + yearly milestones per indicator per period: objectif_2030, annee_baseline, trajectory points `{annee, valeur}`). Selectors aggregate `qhse` raw HSE data + rh attrition/training + installations energy-yield into normalized tiles. ViewSets `periodes-esg/`, `catalogue-esg/`, `objectifs-esg/` (read any-role on finalized periods). All `company`-scoped.

- **NTESG16** adds **FacteurEmissionReference** (versioned emission-factor library: `categorie`/`unite`/`valeur`/`source`/`date_maj`/`version`/`actif` — each update creates a new `version` via `services.creer_version_facteur`, never overwritten in place) at `facteurs-emission/` (+ `historique/`). **NTESG20** adds **ParametresESG** (per-company singleton `OneToOneField(company)`: `seuil_alerte_derive_pct` consumed by `services.alerter_derive_trajectoire`, `pilote_esg` as the default alert recipient, `frequence_reporting`, `ponderation_badge_maturite` JSON — server-validated in `clean()` to sum to exactly 100 across `couverture`/`cibles`/`trajectoire`) at `GET/PUT/PATCH parametres-esg/` (write restricted to Directeur/Administrateur; frontend `pages/parametres/ParametresEsgPage.jsx`). **NTESG18** adds a 4-step "Clôture de période ESG" wizard (`pages/esg/WizardClotureEsg.jsx`) driven by `periodes-esg/<id>/prerequis-cloture/` (`couverture`, `comparaison`, `avertissements[]` vs `bloquants[]` — only real inconsistencies block; low coverage merely warns) and `periodes-esg/<id>/rapport-pdf/?apercu=1` (the NTESG4 PDF renderer, served inline as a preview — no second render path). **NTESG19** adds a 3-screen "Créer un objectif de trajectoire" wizard (`pages/esg/WizardObjectifTrajectoire.jsx`) driven by `objectifs-esg/codes-disponibles/` (only catalogue codes with a real underlying indicator, `objectifs_actifs[]` per code pre-blocking a duplicate target year).

### hospitality ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â hotel/riad/restaurant vertical: rooms, reservations, guest folios, housekeeping  *( `/api/django/hospitality/` )*
Vertical for hospitality operators. **NTHOT1** adds room/unit master: **TypeChambre** (category: Standard/SuiteÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚Â¦, capacity) + **Chambre** (physical unit: type/statut libre/occupÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©e/sale/en_nettoyage, optional rate-link). **NTHOT2** adds rate calendars: **PlanTarifaire** (pricing grid: per room-type, per date-range, per occupancy-basis). **NTHOT3** adds guest lifecycle: **Reservation** (dates/guest/room/statut en_attente/confirmÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©e/arrivÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©e/annulÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©e, optional client_ventes_id string-ref) + actions `arrivee/` `depart/` (state machine). **NTHOT4** adds unified **Folio** (guest bill: reservation, room-charges + additional services, montant_ttc, statut brouillon/emise/payÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©e/en_retard). **NTHOT5** adds housekeeping: **TacheMenage** (cleaning task: chambre, date_prevue, priorite, statut, agent assignÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©, durÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©e, notes). All `company`-scoped. ViewSets `types-chambre/`, `chambres/`, `plans-tarifaires/`, `reservations/`, `folios/`, `taches-menage/` + a `tableau-bord/` dashboard (overview: taux_occupation, revenu_jour, taches_en_retard).

### immobilier ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â real-estate portfolio, leases, rent accounting, GMAO  *( `/api/django/immobilier/` )*
Vertical for property management (fonciÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¨res, syndics, facility managers). Hierarchical patrimony **Site** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ **BÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¢timent** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ **Niveau** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ **Local** (unit type/surface/tantiÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¨mes/statut libre/louÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©/en_travaux); all `company`-scoped with materialized FK chains. **NTPRO2** adds **Locataire** (tenant: type_locataire particulier/societe, coordinates, optional client_ventes_id string-ref to crm.Client via `selectors`, never imports crm.models). **NTPRO3** adds **Bail** (lease: type habitation/commercial, dates, loyer_ht/charges/depot_garantie, statut brouillon/actif/preavis/resilie/expire + snapshot nom bailleur/locataire at signature, NTPRO5 deposit lifecycle: recu/restitue with retenue justifiÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©e). **NTPRO4** adds **RevisionLoyer** (immutable rent-revision history: date_effet/ancien/nouveau loyer, indice, taux_variation ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â audit trail). **NTPRO6** adds **EcheanceLoyer** (monthly rent due: bail/periode/montant_loyer_ht/charges/total, statut a_emettre/emise/payee/impayee/relancee, optional facture_ventes_id string-ref). **NTPRO8** adds **RelanceLoyer** (delinquency escalation distinct from ventes relances: niveau 1-3, canal whatsapp/email/courrier, template). Unique(bail, periode_debut) on EcheanceLoyer. ViewSets `sites/`, `batiments/`, `niveaux/`, `locaux/`, `locataires/`, `baux/`, `echeances-loyer/`, `relances-loyer/`, `etats-lieux/`, `pieces-etat-lieux/`, `elements-etat-lieux/`. **WIR148** schedules the pre-existing NTPRO6 `services.generer_echeancier` as a daily Celery beat (`immobilier.generer_echeances_loyer`, same autodiscovered-task pattern as `ged.migrer_pieces_jointes`) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â active baux' rent due-dates previously only generated via a manual `manage.py generer_echeances_loyer` run. **EtatLieuxImmo** + **PieceEtatLieux** + **ElementEtatLieux** (NTPRO15/16, previously undocumented here) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â move-in/move-out inspection reports (roomÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢element breakdown, before/after comparison photos). **PACT77** gives it its first screen (`/immobilier/etats-lieux`).
### extensions ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â extension marketplace catalogue (NTEXT13, new app)  *( `/api/django/extensions/` )*
A GLOBAL, read-only registry of installable no-code extension packages (templates describing the custom objects / automation rules / reports a tenant could install). Not tenant business data ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â company-less by design (reviewed global, in `tenant_exempt_models.txt`/`tenant_view_allowlist.txt`); per-tenant installation is a separate future brick (NTEXT14).
- **Models** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `ExtensionPackage` (code unique, nom, version, categorie, `manifest` JSON, no company FK).
- Endpoints ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `GET extensions/catalogue/` (read-only list of global packages). **NTEXT25** adds `journal.py` + `GET extensions/journal/` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a company-scoped unified timeline of platform execution events (admin). **NTEXT40** adds `statistiques.py` + `GET extensions/statistiques/` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â platform-usage stats (admin cockpit) for the requester's own company.

### sante ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â cabinet/clinic (agenda, admission, acts nomenclature, patient/third-party billing) (NTSAN1, new app)  *( `/api/django/sante/` )*
Administrative-only healthcare vertical (no clinical medical data): multi-praticien scheduling, admission, act catalogue, patient/tiers-payant invoicing. Every model `TenantModel`-scoped, cross-tenant isolation tested.
- **Models** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `Praticien` (**WIR92** adds an optional `employe_id` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â string-ref to `rh.DossierEmploye`, resolved lazily via `apps.rh.selectors`, never an `rh.models` import ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â for a salaried practitioner's HR/payroll link; additive, no lien = unchanged behaviour), `Salle`, `Patient` (numero_dossier via `core.numbering`), `RendezVous` (overlap guard), `Admission`, `ActeMedical`, `Convention`, `GrilleTarifaire`, `ActeRealise` (tarif snapshot), `PriseEnCharge`, `FactureSante` (tiers-payant/patient split), `PaiementSante`.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/sante/`): CRUD viewsets per model + agenda calendar filters; cross-app `Client` resolution via `crm.selectors` string-FK. **CycleSterilisation** + **InstrumentSterilise** (NTSAN23/24, previously undocumented here) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â autoclave cycle traceability + a lightweight instrumentÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬Âpatient M2M; a non-conforming cycle blocks use. Endpoints `cycles-sterilisation/`, `instruments-sterilises/`. **PACT114** gives it its first screen (`/sante/sterilisation`, open to all roles) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â the app had no API client and no screen for this before.

### veille_ao ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â veille appels d'offres : voir les avis publics, capter les tuyaux privÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©s (Groupe VAO, new app)  *( `/api/django/veille_ao/` )*
App NEUVE et dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©libÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©rÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©ment SÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â°PARÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â°E d'`apps/ao` (dont la chaÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â®ne de migrations est rÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©servÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©e au groupe AOF ; cÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â´tÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©cran `features/ao/**` appartient ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â  AOF, la veille a `features/veille_ao/`). **Ne promet JAMAIS l'exhaustivitÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©** : l'AO fondateur (FRDISI) n'a jamais ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©tÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© publiÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â c'ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©tait une consultation privÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©e sur invitation ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â d'oÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¹ DEUX portes, l'automatique (portail public) et la manuelle (tuyau partenaire), la seconde ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©tant la seule qui l'aurait captÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©.
- **Models** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `SourceVeille` (catalogue des sources EN BASE, jamais une URL en dur dans le code, `actif` = interrupteur), `AvisMarche` (le SAS oÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¹ atterrissent tous les avis quelle que soit la porte ; dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©doublonnage ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â  deux niveaux : contrainte partielle sur l'identitÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© portail + empreinte SHA-256), `MotCleVeille` (mots-clÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©s + poids = DONNÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â°E ; `scoring.py` ne contient aucun littÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©ral), `RegleExclusion` (Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â« Ignorer Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â» APPREND ; motif FR obligatoire), `ExecutionCollecte` (+ **alarme de collecte silencieuse**), `AcheteurCible` (carnet des acheteurs ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â  dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©marcher ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â la vraie contre-mesure FRDISI, livrÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© VIDE).
- **Services** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `changer_statut_avis` est l'UNIQUE point de mutation du statut (gardÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© par introspection AST) ; collecte orchestrÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©e par un REGISTRE de lecteurs (`lecteurs.py`) pour que le collecteur du portail se branche sans rÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©criture ; Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â« Retenir Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â» ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ `AppelOffre` via `apps.ao.services.creer_appel_offre_depuis_avis` (unique point de contact cross-app) ; attribution du CA par source et par informateur (calculÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©e, jamais stockÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©e).
- Endpoints (`/api/django/veille_ao/`) : `sources/`, `avis/` (+ `retenir`/`ignorer`), `mots-cles/`, `regles-exclusion/`, `acheteurs-cibles/` (+ `relances-dues`), `executions/` (lecture seule), `collecter/` (mÃƒÆ’Ã‚Âªme job que la nuit, via `core.jobs.submit`), `sante/`, `attribution/`. Permissions `veille_ao_voir` / `veille_ao_gerer`.
- **Le collecteur HTTP (VAO15-20) est CODE mais DESARME** (2026-08-26) : paquet `apps/veille_ao/portail/` (client PRADO `client.py`, parseur pur bs4 `parser.py`, enrichissement a la demande `detail.py`, garde-fous `garde_fous.py` : cadence 2 s + quota dur 10/jour + interrupteur `VEILLE_AO_COLLECTE_ACTIVE=0` par defaut, verdicts fail-loud `resultats.py`), fixtures HTML committees (reconstructions declarees, jamais capturees), contrat de purete AST + `GardeReseau` (zero socket en test). **Son enregistrement dans `lecteurs.py` est VOLONTAIREMENT ABSENT** : brancher le lecteur = l'acte d'armement VAO4, gate fondateur (regle #5, approbation du fichier de risque `tos_risk/marchespublics_gov_ma.md` encore vide). Dependance : `beautifulsoup4==4.14.2`. User-Agent HONNETE, arret definitif sur 403/401/406/429, jamais de maquillage.

### btp_chantier ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â vertical BTP/EPC : rÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©serves, RFI, visas, DGD (Groupe PACT Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â§E2)  *( `/api/django/btp-chantier/` )*
Le backend existait depuis longtemps ; PACT62-68 lui a donnÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© son PREMIER ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©cran (jusque-lÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â  7 ressources sans aucune interface).
- ÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â°crans (`frontend/src/features/btp_chantier/`) : rÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©serves posÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©es sur un plan GED cliquable (ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©pingles colorÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©es par gravitÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©), RFI avec badge de retard, visas de documents techniques (soumissionÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢revueÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©cision), journal de chantier (effectifs interne/sous-traitant, visiteurs, export PDF), avenants (chiffrage ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ envoi client ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©cision), DGD (notifier/contester/finaliser + comparatif dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©boursÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© sec vs facturÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©), diffusion de plans (accusÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© de rÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©ception + alerte plan pÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©rimÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©).
- Glyphe d'app `Construction` et accent `success` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â distincts d'`installations` (`HardHat`), contrainte des gardes `appGlyph` et `AppIcon.voisinage`.

### portail (cÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â´tÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© ERP) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â administration du portail client (Groupe PACT Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â§E3)  *( `/api/django/portail/` )*
L'app servait le portail CLIENT depuis longtemps ; PACT96-101 lui a donnÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© sa face ERP (un hub ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â  6 onglets, `features/portail/admin/`) : comptes portail (provisionner un accÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¨s sans jamais afficher le mot de passe), preuves d'acceptation de devis (consultation seule ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â ces lignes ne naissent que du geste du client), paiements ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â  rapprocher, documents dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©posÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©s (action dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©diÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©e `marquer_traite`, le champ ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©tant `read_only`), jalons de chantier publiÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©s, demandes de ticket (rattachement ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â  un ticket SAV existant ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â l'action serveur ne CRÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬Â°E aucun ticket, contrairement ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â  ce que dit sa docstring).
- Glyphe `DoorOpen`, accent `lune`.

### innovation ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â internal idea box + innovation campaigns + product feedback (NTIDE1, new app)  *( `/api/django/innovation/` )*
Company-scoped suggestion box: propose/vote/triage ideas, admin dashboard, targeted campaigns, in-app feedback CTA. Chatter via generic `records.Activity` (ARC8, no bespoke *Activity), bulk tagging via `records.Tag`/`TaggedItem`.
- **Models** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `Idee` (statut/contexte/votes_count/opaque devis-ticket-chantier link), `VoteIdee` (unique per idee/votant), `InnovationSettings` (singleton, `SettingsAuditLog` on change).
- Endpoints (`/api/django/innovation/`): `idees/` (+ filters, vote/unvote, status transitions, historique, bulk, export_xlsx), `contextes/` (top-5 autocomplete), dashboard selectors.
- `management/seed_innovation_demo` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â idempotent demo-data seeder (sample idÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©es/votes) for a fresh tenant.

### credit ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â client credit management (Groupe NTCRD, new app)  *( `/api/django/credit/` )*
Credit limits, credit-hold (avertissement/blocage), payment-behaviour scoring, dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©rogations, credit-insurance coverage and consolidated exposure. **Additive & non-blocking by default** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â no `LimiteCredit` for a client means unchanged behaviour (no hold); references ventes/crm strictly by string-FK (`crm.Client`, `ventes.Devis`, `ventes.BonCommande`), reads encours via `ventes.selectors` only.
- **Models** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `LimiteCredit` (unique per company+client, `montant_limite` nullable = unlimited), `ReglageCredit` (per-company settings singleton), `ConditionPaiementSegment` + `SegmentClientCredit`, `EncoursCache`, `DerogationCredit` (request/approve/reject lifecycle lifting hold), `PoliceAssuranceCredit`, `EncoursGarantiClient` (per police+client insurer quota).
- Endpoints (`/api/django/credit/`): ViewSets `limites/`, `derogations/`, `conditions-segment/`, `segments-client/`, `polices-assurance/`, `encours-garantis/`; `reglage/`, `clients/<id>/fiche|score|position-pdf|limite-suggeree/`, `import-limites/`, `exposition/`, `rapport-derogations/`, `badges/`. Selectors: `encours_client`, `disponible_credit`, `fiche_credit`, `rapport_exposition`, `quota_assurance_utilise`. Service `verifier_hold_credit`. Beat tasks: `recalculer_encours_quotidien`, `expirer_derogations`, `alerter_exposition_globale`, `alerter_polices_expirantes`. **PACT47** gives `import-limites/` its first screen (`/credit/import-limites`); **PACT48** gives `polices-assurance/` theirs (`/credit/assurance`); **PACT50** gives `segments-client/` theirs (`/credit/segments-clients`).
- **WIR93 ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â coexistence, documented and locked.** Two credit-hold engines exist in the repo: THIS app (`LimiteCredit`/`verifier_hold_credit`, NTCRD6 ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â has **no production caller today**, its NTCRD7/8 hooks are `[BLOCKED]`) and the actually-wired `crm.Client.plafond_credit`/`CompanyProfile.credit_hold_actif` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ `ventes.services.verifier_credit_hold` (FG41/XFAC28, consumed at devis-accept + tranche generation). `services.ecart_encours_moteurs(client)` materializes the ONE authorized gap between them (engine B intentionally excludes `brouillon` factures, via the new `ventes.selectors.reste_du_factures_brouillon`) and is locked by `test_wir93_encours_non_divergence.py` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â any future drift (new statut, one-sided assiette change) turns it red. No behaviour change; a documentation + regression-test decision, switching to a single source stays open to the founder.

### fpa ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â enterprise FP&A / budgeting (Groupe NTFPA, new app)  *( `/api/django/fpa/` )*
Financial Planning & Analysis: departmental budget cycles, submission/validation workflow, rolling forecasts, what-if scenarios + sensitivity, variance analysis (budget vs actual vs forecast). DISTINCT from per-chantier micro-budget (`gestion_projet.BudgetProjet`). Reads compta/ventes/crm/paie only via their `selectors.py`; emits `budget_cycle_clos` domain event on `core/events.py`.
- **Models** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `Departement` (hierarchy), `CycleBudgetaire` (brouillon/ouvert_saisie/en_validation/clos state machine, write-locked once clos), `LigneBudgetDepartement` (monthÃƒÆ’Ã¢â‚¬â€category), `SoumissionBudgetDepartement`, `MappingCategorieCompte` (FP&A category ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬Â CGNC prefix), `PrevisionGlissante` + `LignePrevisionGlissante`, `HypotheseRecrutement`, `ScenarioBudgetaire` + `LigneScenario` (deltas, one base per cycle), `CommentaireVariance`.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/fpa/`): ViewSets `departements/`, `cycles-budgetaires/` (+ `ouvrir-saisie`/`dupliquer`/`export`), `lignes-budget/`, `soumissions-budget/` (+ `historique`/`noter` chatter actions on `SoumissionBudgetDepartement`), `previsions/`, `scenarios/`, `variance/`, `commentaires-variance/`, `mappings-categorie/`, `drivers/` (revenu-pipeline/engage, masse-salariale), `consolidation/`. **PACT53** fixes `historique`/`noter` to serialize through `ChatterActivitySerializer` (was `ActivitySerializer`, which exposes neither `body` nor the author ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â notes rendered empty/anonymous) and gives the submission-validation workflow its first screen, `/fpa/soumissions`. Frontend routes `/fpa/*` (saisie, dashboard, previsions, scenarios, variance, soumissions) gated Directeur/FP&A.

### assurances ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â enterprise insurance & claims registry (Groupe NTASS, new app)  *( `/api/django/assurances/` )*
Company insurance policies (RC pro, dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©cennale, multirisque, cyber, homme-clÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©) with guarantees, premium schedules, covered assets, claims (sinistres) + indemnities and attestations. DISTINCT from vehicle policies (flotte) and market bank-guarantees (compta). Reads flotte/rh via selectors + string-FK; proposes compta ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©critures (brouillon) via `compta.services`.
- **Models** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `Assureur`, `Courtier`, `PoliceAssurance` (+ `PoliceActivity` chatter, `cyber_clauses`), `GarantiePolice`, `EcheancePrime`, `ActifCouvert`, `DeclarationSinistre` (+ `SinistreActivity` chatter), `IndemnisationSinistre`, `AttestationAssurance`, `ExigenceAssuranceMarche`.
- Endpoints (`/api/django/assurances/`): ViewSets `assureurs/`, `courtiers/`, `polices/` (+ nested garanties/ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©chÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©ancier, renouveler, xlsx export), plus sinistres/indemnisations/attestations/exigences; function views `couverture-actif/`, `tableau-bord/`. Alerts via `notify()` (renouvellement J-60/30/7, warranty_expiring). RBAC keys `assurances_voir/gerer/proposer_ecriture`.

### einvoice ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â Moroccan DGI e-invoicing (Groupe NTMAR, new app)  *( `/api/django/einvoice/` )*
Flag-gated (`EINVOICE_ENABLED`, default OFF ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ app entirely inert) generator of DGI-schema electronic invoices. RE-USES the existing `apps.ventes.dgi` UBL export + art.145 validator via the module API + `ventes.selectors.get_facture_scoped` (never a ventes model import); no new render engine.
- **Models** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `FactureElectronique` (facture-id string-ref + dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©normalized ref, format UBL/CII, mode dry_run/rÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©el, statut brouillonÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢genereÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢signeÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢transmis/rejete, immutable `version`, `xml_key` MinIO, `hash_contenu`, signature scaffold fields), `TransmissionDGI` (inert Simpl queue, unique per e-facture).
- **Services** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `generer` (dry-run XML ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ MinIO, new version), `regenerer` (NTMAR9 immutable re-gen), `preparer_signature` (NTMAR6 empreinte only, never signs ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `EINVOICE_SIGNATURE_PROVIDER=noop`), `transmettre` (NTMAR7 no-op without `DGI_TRANSMISSION_ENABLED`+URL), `validators.controler_avant_transmission` (NTMAR8, reuses art.145 validator).
- Endpoints (`/api/django/einvoice/`): ViewSets `factures-electroniques/` (actions `generer`/`regenerer`/`telecharger`/`preparer-signature`/`transmettre`/`controler`), `transmissions/` (read-only). Live DGI transmission + certified signature stay `[BLOCKED-ESCALATE]` (gate G14, no DGI API/credential). **PACT54** gives `transmissions/` its first screen ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `/fiscal/transmissions-dgi` (grouped under the `fiscal` frontend module for UX, still reads `einvoiceApi`).

### fiscal ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â Moroccan fiscal compliance calendar & attestations (Groupe NTMAR, new app)  *( `/api/django/fiscal/` )*
Full Moroccan tax calendar per obligation + reminders + compliance dashboard, tenant attestations with expiry, UBO registry, actionable regulatory watch. Distinct from `compta.ObligationFiscale` (XACC9 one-off calendar view) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â here the recurring RULE + materialized dated deadlines, covering obligations compta doesn't (CNSS/taxe pro/enregistrement).
- **Models** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `ObligationFiscale` (recurring rule: type TVA/IS/IR/acompte_IS/timbre/RAS/CNSS/taxe_pro/enregistrement, pÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©riodicitÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©, rÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¨gle d'ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©chÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©ance), `EcheanceFiscale` (dated deadline, `rappel_envoye_le` idempotence, declaration string-ref), `AttestationTenant` (expiry), `BeneficiaireEffectif` (UBO), `VeilleReglementaire` (per-company or global, `parametre_cible` actionable link).
- **Services/selectors** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `seed_obligations_standard`, `calendrier(company, annee)` (idempotent), `envoyer_rappels_fiscaux` (+ `manage.py rappels_fiscaux`), `tableau_conformite` (feu tricolore), `attestations_expirantes`, `pieces_reutilisables_attestations` (NTMAR29 source for ao dossiers), `registre_ubo` (+ completeness alert), `export_declaration_ubo` (OMPIC-shaped).
- Endpoints (`/api/django/fiscal/`): ViewSets `obligations/` (`seed-standard`/`calendrier`/`tableau-conformite`/`rappels`), `echeances/`, `attestations-tenant/` (`expirantes`/`pieces-reutilisables`), `beneficiaires-effectifs/` (`registre`/`export-declaration`), `veille/` (`marquer-impact-traite`); function view `tableau-conformite/`. **PACT52** gives `beneficiaires-effectifs/` (UBO registry) its first screen, `/fiscal/beneficiaires-effectifs` (completeness alert + OMPIC export).

### education ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©cole/ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©tablissement scolaire (NTEDU, new app)  *( `/api/django/education/` )*
Company-scoped school ops: annÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©e scolaire / niveau / classe, dossier famille/ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©lÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¨ve, inscriptions (rÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©inscription masse + liste d'attente FIFO), scolaritÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© (grille tarifaire, remises fratrie/bourse en `brouillon` jusqu'ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â  approbation, ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©chÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©ancier auto), prÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©sences (bulk-saisie + notif absence famille), matiÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¨res/coefficients, ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©valuations/notes, emploi du temps, cantine, discipline, and a parent self-service account ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a wider surface than previously written up here.
- **Models** (all `TenantModel` unless noted) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `AnneeScolaire`, `Niveau`, `Classe`, `Famille` (**WIR91** adds an optional `client` FKÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢`crm.Client`, string-referenced/never a `crm.models` import, resolved via `services.resoudre_client_pour_famille` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â same pattern as `sante.Patient.client`/`crm.services.resolve_client_for_lead`: a family is never re-entered as a duplicate CRM client), `Eleve`, `Inscription`, `GrilleTarifaire`, `Remise`, `EcheancierScolarite`/`LigneEcheance`, `Seance`/`Presence`, `Matiere`/`MatiereClasse`, `Evaluation`/`Note`, `CertificatScolarite`, `CreneauEmploiDuTemps`, `MenuCantine`/`InscriptionCantine`, `IncidentDiscipline`, `CompteParent` (parent portal account), `ParametresEducation` (plain `models.Model` settings singleton, not tenant-FK'd the same way).
- Endpoints (`/api/django/education/`): `annees-scolaires/`, `niveaux/`, `classes/`, `familles/`, `eleves/`, `inscriptions/`, `grilles-tarifaires/`, `remises/`, `echeanciers/`, `seances/`, `presences/`, `matieres/`, `matieres-classe/`, `evaluations/`, `notes/`, `parametres/`, `emploi-du-temps/`, `menus-cantine/`, `inscriptions-cantine/`, `incidents/`, `transport/circuits`, `transport/arrets`, `transport/affectations`; public parent portal at `api/django/public/education/` (NTEDU31/32/34, no login).
- **CircuitTransport / ArretTransport / AffectationTransport** (NTEDU23, previously undocumented here) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â school bus transport: circuits, stops, per-student assignment; `Eleve.transport_mensuel` / `EcheancierScolarite.transport_montant` (NTEDU24) fold the fee into the billing schedule. **PACT80** gives it its first screen (`/education/transport`) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â the backend existed complete, the education client had no entry point for it.

### uxviews ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â vues sauvegardÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©es + primitives DataTable (NTUX, new app)  *( `/api/django/uxviews/` )*
Cross-cutting UX layer: `SavedView` (vues sauvegardÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©es par ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©cran, portÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©e owner/ÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©quipe, dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©faut-par-rÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â´le) via `CompanyScopedModelViewSet`; frontend `ui/datatable/*` gains FilterBuilder (AND/OR groups, relative dates), BulkEditDialog + undo, spreadsheet cell nav, quick-create event bus, RecentEntitiesWidget.
- **NTUX1/9/12/27 (30/08-12/09).** `migrateLocalSavedViews.js` (NTUX1, best-effort, jamais bloquant) migre les vues `localStorage` historiques (FG11) vers `SavedView` au premier écran adoptant `useServerSavedViews`. `contextActions.js` (NTUX9) étend la palette de commandes avec des actions CONTEXTUELLES résolues par route, mêmes endpoints que le reste de l'app (aucune logique métier dupliquée). `favoris/` (+ `reordonner/`, NTUX12) expose `FavoriUtilisateur` en widget épinglable (`FavoriButton`/`FavorisWidget`). NTUX27 ajoute l'écran `/parametres/ux` (`UxParametresPage.jsx`) pour `UxParametres`.

### entites ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â hiÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©rarchie d'entitÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©s intra-tenant (Groupe NTADM, new app)  *( `/api/django/entites/` )*
Intra-tenant org structure (holdingÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢filialeÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢agence): `Entite` (TenantModel, `code` unique par company, `parent` self-FK SET_NULL, `actif`), anti-cycle guard (a parent can never be its own descendant, validated client + server 400). Emits `entite_created`/`entite_deactivated` on `core/events.py` (NTADM40). Chatter via `records.Activity` (NTADM47, `historique`/`noter`). NTADM2 shipped (2026-08-05): nullable `entite` FK (SET_NULL) on `crm.Lead`/`ventes.Devis`/`facturation.Facture`/`stock.Produit` + optional `?entite=` filter via `core/entite_scoping.py::EntiteScopeMixin`; NTADM3: `Role.entites_visibles` M2M opt-in data perimeter (NULL rows stay visible, out-of-scope rows 404 on detail, 403 on write); writes on `Entite` gated by fine permission `adminops_entites_gerer` (NTADM39, `noter` exempt).
- **Models** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `Entite` (TenantModel).
- Endpoints (`/api/django/entites/`): ViewSet `entites/` (Administrateur-only, CRUD + `?tree=1` nested tree + `desactiver`/`historique`/`noter` actions + `groupe/` NTADM25 read-only per-entity KPI columns via ventes/crm/stock selectors + `mes-entites/` NTADM26 any-role list feeding the header `EntiteSwitcher`). DELETE = soft-deactivate (never hard-delete).

### adminops ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â administration enterprise (Groupe NTADM, new app)  *( `/api/django/adminops/` )*
SaaS-ops surface: `calculer_health_score(company)` (0-100, 3 sub-scores config-completeness/usage/data-quality + 3 recommandations, strictly company-scoped); sandbox self-service (`SandboxEnvironment` clone the tenant Company+CompanyProfile with a `-sandbox` slug via a Celery task; metier-data anonymized clone is DEFERRED ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â cross-app surface); exportable config packages (`ConfigPackage` snapshot of custom Role/CustomFieldDef/MessageTemplate ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â never metier/client data ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â with dry-run diff `previsualiser` + idempotent `appliquer` logged in `ConfigPackageApplication`); privacy-safe adoption analytics (`EvenementUsage`, no free payload, 180j purge); per-tenant `AdminOpsSettings` (sandbox duration/grace, seats-alert %, usage retention, `sandbox_autorise`); support/diagnostic (non-sensitive tenant snapshot + support-bundle .zip, `is_taqinor_support` or tenant Administrateur). KPI provider `kpi_adminops` on the federated `reporting` endpoint.
- **Models** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `HealthScoreSnapshot`, `SandboxEnvironment`, `ConfigPackage`, `ConfigPackageApplication`, `EvenementUsage`, `AdminOpsSettings` (all TenantModel); `PlanLicence` (NTADM7, GLOBAL editor catalog starter/pro/enterprise, no company FK, seeded); `SessionImpersonation` (NTADM22, consent-gated support session, bounded access token, audit rows marked `[IMPERSONATION ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚Â¦]` pre_save); `AnnonceProduit`/`LectureAnnonce` (NTADM18, founder-published release notes via notifications type `PRODUCT_ANNOUNCEMENT`); `FactureLicence` (N100e, founder licence-billing ledger, never client-facing); `DemandeInscription` (N101b, design-partner signup requests, parked behind `TENANT_SIGNUP_ENABLED`).
- Endpoints (`/api/django/adminops/`): `health-score/`, `adoption/`, `tracker-usage/`, `settings/` (GET/PATCH), `diagnostic/` (+ `support-bundle/` zip), `rapports/journal-admin/` (WeasyPrint interne via `core.pdf`), `licences/` + `licences/pdf/` (NTADM9/29), `impersonation/ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚Â¦` (demande/cibles/en-attente/session-active/consentir/refuser/demarrer/terminer), `annonces/` (+ `marquer-lu`), `facturation-licences/` (+ `export-csv`/`marquer-payee`), `demandes-inscription/` (+ `approuver`/`refuser`), ViewSets `sandbox/` (+ `creer`/`prolonger`), `config-packages/` (+ `exporter`/`previsualiser`/`appliquer`). Fine permissions NTADM39 (`adminops_*`, explicit in-body checks). Celery beat: `purger_sandbox_expires`, `rappeler_sandbox_a_expirer`, `recalculer_health_score_tenants`, `purger_config_packages_anciens`, `purger_evenements_usage`, `perimer_demandes_impersonation` (NTADM37). All @actions carry explicit `permission_classes`.

### juridique — affaires juridiques / contentieux d'entreprise (Groupe NTJUR, new app)  *( `/api/django/juridique/` )*
Dossiers de contentieux/précontentieux d'entreprise, distincts de `litiges` (réclamation client opérationnelle, reliée ici par simple référence d'id) et de `contrats` (cycle de vie contractuel) ; multi-société via `TenantModel`.
- **Models** — `DossierJuridique` (machine à états gouvernée : `en_cours` -> `clos_gagne`/`clos_perdu`/`clos_transaction`/`clos_abandon`, flag `confidentiel` exclu des agrégats pour un rôle non autorisé), `CabinetAvocat`/`MandatAvocat` (conseil externe, activation), `Audience` (report), `DelaiPrescription` (échéances, `expirants/`), `NoteHonoraires` (validation/paiement, échéancier NTJUR4/5/11), `RegleApprobationJuridique`/`EtapeApprobationJuridique` (chaîne d'approbation d'un mandat, budget alloué NTJUR12, approbateur désigné NTJUR40) ; provisions comptables PROPOSÉES (NTJUR14/15, jamais une écriture directe).
- Endpoints : `dossiers/` (+ `budget`, `tableau-bord`, `export`, `timeline`, `statuts-suivants`, `changer-statut`, `clore`, `proposer-provision`/`reprendre-provision`, `lancer-approbation-mandat`, `approuver-etape`/`rejeter-etape`, `etapes-approbation`), `cabinets-avocats/`, `mandats/` (+ `activer`), `audiences/` (+ `reporter`), `delais-prescription/` (+ `expirants`), `notes-honoraires/` (+ `valider`, `marquer-payee`), `regles-approbation/`. Scope publicapi `juridique:read`.
- Signal `dossier_juridique_clos` (NTJUR26) propose la reprise de provision à `apps.compta` sans jamais poster d'écriture ; 4 KPI juridiques dans `reporting` (NTJUR48, voir `reporting`). Écrans `/juridique` (registre + cockpit tableau-bord + détail dossier + wizard de clôture, une seule route à état interne : `JuridiquePage`/`DossierDetailPage`/`WizardClotureDossier`/`ProvisionPanel`/`RisquesJuridiquesCard`).

### semantic — couche métriques nommées (Groupe NTDATA, new app)  *(pas d'endpoint propre — consommé via `core.data_explorer`)*
`MetricDefinition` (`TenantModel`, formule/agrégat/dataset + filtres, NTDATA11) nomme une métrique UNE fois et la rend résolvable partout : `resolve_metric`/`resolve_metric_valeur` (`services.py`) l'exécutent soit via le dataset générique `core.data_explorer` (`ca_ht`/`ca_ttc`/`panier_moyen`/`taux_conversion`/`mrr`), soit via un **adaptateur** enregistré (`adapters.py::register_adapter`, jamais un import croisé) pour les 3 métriques au calcul métier propre : `marge_brute`/`dso` (`pilotage_financier`), `valeur_pipeline_ponderee` (`reporting.pipeline` + scorers `crm`). `manage.py seed_metriques` sème les 8 métriques ci-dessus (idempotent). Consommé par `reporting.KpiAlerte.source=METRIQUE` (NTDATA13, voir `reporting`).

### dataquality — qualité de données (Groupe NTDATA, new app)  *( `/api/django/dataquality/` )*
`RegleQualite` (`TenantModel`, format/référence/règle `core.rules`) + `ResultatQualite` (résultat d'évaluation, historisé) ; évaluation par le beat quotidien 05:45 (`evaluer_regles`) ou à la demande.
- Endpoints : `regles/` (CRUD), `rapport/` (NTDATA15, taux de conformité par règle, `?entite=`/`?evaluer=1`), `completude/` (NTDATA16, champs critiques par entité), `doublons/<entite>/` (NTDATA17/19, `clients`/`fournisseurs`/`produits` — GET liste les groupes candidats LECTURE SEULE, POST `{survivant, doublons}` déclenche la fusion supervisée NTDATA18).
- La fusion (`services.fusionner_clients/fournisseurs/produits`) NEUTRALISE les doublons (jamais un DELETE) et repointe toutes les FK via `core.merge.repointer_relations`/`completer_champs_vides` ; la réponse NOMME ce qui n'a pas pu être repointé (contrainte d'unicité déjà occupée) plutôt que de le taire.

### grc — gouvernance, risques & conformité (Groupe NTGRC, new app)  *( `/api/django/grc/` )*
Porte réelle du registre ERM, des contrôles internes SOX-lite, des violations de données/CNDP et du legal hold transverse, distincte de `qhse` (SST/qualité opérationnelle) qu'elle relie par CAPA (`DeficienceControle`).
- **Models** — `RisqueEntreprise` (registre ERM, matrice 5x5, NTGRC13), `PlanTraitementRisque` (NTGRC14, retards), `RevueRisque` (NTGRC15, cadence périodique), `ControleInterne`/`TestControle`/`DeficienceControle` (NTGRC16/17/18, déficience -> CAPA QHSE), `PolitiqueInterne`/`PolitiqueVersion` (NTGRC19, versionnées), `ViolationDonnees` (NTGRC6, délai légal 72 h, `generer-dossier-notification`/`notifier-cndp`), `LegalHold` (NTGRC8, séquestre transverse au-delà de la GED, `objets-sous-hold/`), `PolitiqueRetentionObjet` (NTGRC4, pilote les balayages `core.retention` de `crm`/`ventes`/`sav`/`audit`), `JournalDestruction` (NTGRC5, APPEND-ONLY).
- Endpoints : `risques-entreprise/`, `plans-traitement-risque/` (+ `en-retard`), `revues-risque/` (+ `risques-a-revoir`), `controles-internes/`, `tests-controle/` (+ `controles-a-tester`), `deficiences-controle/`, `politiques-internes/`, `violations-donnees/` (+ `echeance-depassee`, `changer-statut`, `generer-dossier-notification`, `notifier-cndp`), `legal-holds/` (+ `objets-sous-hold`), `politiques-retention-objet/`, `journal-destruction/`.
- **Portail DSR public tokenisé (NTGRC1/2/3, loi 09-08)** — `public/demande-droit/` (`deposer_demande_droit`, AllowAny + throttle) + `public/demande-droit/<token>/` (`suivre_demande_droit`) sur `core.DataSubjectRequest` (`core/dsr.py`) ; `seed_controles_base` amorce une bibliothèque de contrôles. Abonné à `lead_created` (alerte DPO sur retrait de consentement) sans jamais importer `apps.crm`.

### core ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â foundation layer (events bus, signing, AI scorers, BPM engine, platform kernel)  *(`backend/django_core/core`, NOT under apps/)*
- **NTDATA tête de lane (31/08)** : `dashboard_data.py` — `GET core/dashboards/<id>/donnees/?filtre=` (filtres globaux période/égalités réinjectés par widget, jamais persistés ; un widget en erreur remonte SON erreur) + cache Redis opt-in par widget (`cache`/`cache_ttl`, clé par utilisateur sauf `cache_partage=True`) ; `drill_api.py` — `core/data-explorer/drill/` + registre `register_drill(dataset → id_field, route)` (liste d'IDs + liens profonds, liste blanche inchangée) ; exports : destination `minio` par DÉFAUT (dépôt `warehouse/<company>/<dataset>/`), `SnowflakeDestination` DÉSARMÉE sans les 5 `SNOWFLAKE_*` (connecteur absent des requirements), mode incrémental high-watermark (`mode`/`champ_curseur`/`dernier_curseur` — le curseur n'avance jamais sur erreur) + `manifest.json` par export (migrations core/0045-0047).
The base layer everything depends on and that imports no domain app (import-linter `core-foundation-is-a-base-layer`). Holds `events.py` (the Django-signal domain-event bus, M6), `signing`, and PURE stateless scorers fed data as input ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `forecast.py` (FG361), `win_probability.py` (FG362), `churn_risk.py` (FG363), `stock_reorder.py` (FG364), `payment_delay.py` (FG365), `anomaly.py` (FG360 `AnomalyFlag`). **FG366** adds a generic **BPM/workflow engine**: `WorkflowDefinition` + `WorkflowStepDefinition` (templates), `WorkflowInstance` (runs on ANY model via a `contenttypes` GenericForeignKey ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â no domain import) + `WorkflowStepInstance` (per-step statut, `sla_echeance` = start + `sla_heures`, assignee). `core/workflow.py` services `demarrer_workflow`/`avancer`/`approuver_etape`/`rejeter_etape`/`escalader_etape` + selector `etapes_sla_depassees(company, now)` (now injected) + a `escalate_workflow_sla` management command. All `company`-scoped. Migration core/0002. **ARC10** makes this the SAME engine any app attaches an approval chain to, not a bespoke one per app ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â pilot: `qhse` NCR closure routes through `core.workflow` rather than a local approval flag. **FG367** adds `core/rules.py` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a generic multi-criteria rule engine (no model): `evaluate_condition_group(group, context)` (nested AND/OR/NOT tree; 11 leaf operators eq/ne/gt/gte/lt/lte/in/not_in/contains/startswith/exists; short-circuit; missing-field tolerant; never raises), `validate_condition_group` (structural errors), `sequential_actions` (ordered stop-on-error helper). Reusable by `apps/automation`'s rules (wiring deferred); **ARC34**'s `RECORD_STATE_CHANGE` trigger evaluates its conditions through it (never a second evaluator). **FG368** adds `core/jobs.py` + **ScheduledJobViewSet** (`/api/django/core/jobs/`) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â introspects the Celery `current_app.conf.beat_schedule` (+ optional django-celery-beat) into a normalized job list, with an admin-only `jobs/run/` manual trigger (`send_task`, broker-down ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ 503). Jobs are global infra (no company scoping), `IsAdminRole`-gated, no new dependency. core's first URLConf (`core/urls.py`, wired into the root). **FG369** adds `core/workflow_templates.py` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a pure-data catalogue of pre-built workflow templates (relance devis, onboarding chantier, rappel garantie) + idempotent `installer_modele_workflow(company, code)` materializing the FG366 `WorkflowDefinition`/`WorkflowStepDefinition` per company, exposed via `WorkflowTemplateViewSet` (list any-auth / `installer` admin-responsable) on `core/urls.py`. No new dependency; core stays foundation. `core/saved-queries/` (`datasets/` catalogue + `run/` execution, previously undocumented here ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â no `features/core/` frontend existed to call it) is a saved-query data explorer over registry-declared datasets. **PACT122** gives it its first screen, `/donnees/explorateur` (responsable/admin, new `features/core/` module).
- **Multi-tenant + viewset socle.** **ARC1** adds `core/models.py` `TenantModel` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â an abstract `company` FK (ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ `authentication.Company`, `related_name='%(app_label)s_%(class)s_set'`) + `TimestampedModel` (`created_at`/`updated_at`) in one mixin; the PLAYBOOK rule is every NEW multi-tenant model inherits it instead of hand-rolling the pair, and a model CONVERTED from a hand-written FK redeclares `company` in its own body to keep its historical `related_name` (never a renamed accessor). **ARC15** records the adoption census for `core.SoftDeleteModel` (FG388, `is_deleted`/`deleted_at`/`deleted_by` + `SoftDeleteManager` hiding deleted rows + `DeletionRecord` trash journal): zero business models inherit it yet ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â it is the acted-upon socle that the future YDATA17 soft-delete wave builds pilots on (never a second mixin). **ARC2/ARC55** add `core/viewsets.py` `CompanyScopedModelViewSet` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `TenantMixin` + `ModelViewSet` with `ScopedPermission` as the unified DEFAULT `permission_classes` (no `read_permission`/`write_permission`/`get_permissions` of its own ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â¡Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ authenticated-only, strictly equivalent to the prior project default); the single new base a domain viewset inherits from, with named-but-unimplemented extension points reserved for pagination (YAPIC1) and filter backends (YAPIC2).
- **Numbering, PDF, audit funnel.** **ARC6** relocates the race-safe reference factory (highest-used+1 per company+period, savepoint+retry, never `count()+1`) to `core/numbering.py`; `apps/ventes/utils/references.py` becomes a byte-identical re-export shim so existing importers are untouched. **ARC11** adds `core/pdf.py` `render_pdf(html=None, *, template=None, context=None, company=None, header=False, footer=False, upload_to=None, upload_bucket=None)` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â the shared WeasyPrint plumbing (lazy import, optional branded header/footer from `parametres.CompanyProfile` resolved via `apps.get_model`, optional MinIO upload) that ~45 internal PDF call sites (rh/compta/qhse/pos/paie/reportingÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚Â¦) converge on; rule #4 EXCLUDES `apps/ventes/quote_engine/**` and the legacy Facture PDF permanently (never migrated here). **ARC16** adds `apps/audit/recorder.py` `record_field_change` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â the single funnel that writes both the `AuditLog` entry and (opt-in) the `records.Activity` chatter line for one field change in one call, replacing two independently-drifting call sites.
- **ODY25 ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â journal d'installation des apps.** Chaque bascule de `ModuleToggle` emet `module_toggled` sur le bus de signaux `core/events.py` (aux seuls VRAIS franchissements, une entree par module reellement bascule, cascade comprise) ; l'historique reutilise le chatter generique `records.Activity` cible sur le `ModuleToggle` lui-meme ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â le cloisonnement multi-societes est donc STRUCTUREL, pas un filtre a ne pas oublier. Lu par `GET /core/modules/journal/` et affiche dans la boutique Applications. Le receveur vit dans `apps/records/receivers.py` et NON dans `core/` : `records.services` importe des selectors crm/ventes/stock, donc le brancher depuis `core` creerait la chaine `core -> apps.crm` interdite par le contrat import-linter.
- **Platform registry (the "declare once, appear everywhere" kernel).** **ARC28** adds `core/platform.py` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â each app declares a `PLATFORM` dict in its own `apps/<x>/platform.py` across 7 surfaces (`searchable_models`, `record_targets`, `customfield_models`, `import_specs`, `agent_actions_module`, `automation_state_fields`, `kpi_providers`); `core` collects them generically via `get_app_configs()` + `importlib` at runtime (never a static `import apps.x` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â import-linter stays green), gated per company by `ModuleToggle` (extends ODX23: an OFF module vanishes from every surface at once). 15 apps now carry a `platform.py` (ao/compta/contrats/crm/flotte/ged/gestion_projet/installations/kb/outillage/qhse/rh/sav/stock/ventes). Surfaces switched to read the registry in follow-on tasks: **ARC29** `reporting/search.py` global search iterates `searchable_models` (gated); **ARC30** `records.ALLOWED_TARGETS` becomes a lazy union of `record_targets` (19 historical targets migrated in, drop-in set-identical); **ARC31** `customfields` registry loads `customfield_models` centrally in `CustomfieldsConfig.ready()`; **ARC32** `dataimport`'s `TARGETS` becomes `FIELD_MAPS ÃƒÂ¢Ã‹â€ Ã‚Âª import_specs`; **ARC33** `apps/agent/registry.py` auto-discovers `agent_actions_module` in `AgentConfig.ready()` (convention `register_actions()`, idempotent, gated by `ModuleToggle` in `for_user`); **ARC40** adds a federated KPI endpoint `GET reporting/kpi-federes/` resolving `kpi_providers` dotted callables into normalized `{id,label,valeur,unite?}` tiles (3 pilot providers: rh/compta/gestion_projet). **ARC41** adds `core/platform_coverage.py` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a drift matrix cross-referencing `record_targets` vs `searchable_models` (a chatter-ised-but-unsearchable model, and the inverse), baseline-frozen with a "red only on regression" policy, extending `event_coverage.py` (YEVNT7) to the declarative surfaces.
- **Document kit, request cache, misc foundation.** **SCA30-33** add `core/documents.py` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â the `DocumentMetier` kit composing the ARC1/2/6/11 bricks for any NEW "header + statut + lignes + totaux + reference + PDF + chatter" document type: `DocumentMetierMeta` (a metaclass, not `__init_subclass__`, so each subclass gets its OWN `statut` field/choices instead of mutating the shared abstract field), `LigneDocumentMetier` + `TotauxDocumentMixin` (line/totals factoring), `document_viewset()` (scoping+numbering+chatter in one declaration), `render_document_pdf()` (the ARC11 PDF hook). Rule #4 EXCLUSION is permanent and named: Devis/Facture/BonCommande/Avoir never retrofit onto this kit. Pilots: `installations.OrdreSousTraitance` (SCA34), `installations.DemandeAchat` (SCA36), `contrats.Contrat` (SCA35, adopts the kit's read contract without forced renumbering). **SCA43** adds `core/request_cache.py` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a per-request `contextvar` memo for config accessors (`CompanyProfile`, tax/payment-terms referentials) that de-duplicates the ~6 identical config queries `quote_engine.build_quote_data` re-issued per Devis in a list (N+1 fix, pilot NTPLT16/QPERF1); outside a request scope (Celery, shell, tests) it degrades to calling the producer every time ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â byte-identical rendering. **SCA48** adds `core/benchmarking.py` `BENCHMARK_MIN_COMPANIES = 5` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â the legal k-anonymity floor any cross-tenant aggregate (NTDATA46/47) must respect before publishing a stratum. **SCA20/28** add `core/signup_hooks.py` (`register_signup_hook`/`run_signup_hooks`, the same producer/subscriber pattern as the M6 event bus, best-effort per-hook isolation) + `core/services.py` `seed_tenant_branding` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â seeds a neutral `TenantTheme` + default `BrandedTemplate` rows (e.g. the email signature) idempotently at company signup, alongside the pre-existing roles/catalogue seeds. **WIR138** adds `core/esign.py` (FG372) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a recorded DECISION naming `core.esign` the ONE canonical socle for e-signature REQUESTS sent to an external provider (Yousign/DocuSignÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚Â¦: `EsignProvider`/`GenericEsignProvider` interface, `creer_demande`/`envoyer`/`rafraichir_statut` against `EsignRequest`, contenttypes-targeted so `core` imports no domain app) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â deliberately PARKED/dormant with **no endpoint exposed** until the founder provisions a real provider account (locked by `core/tests/test_wir138_esign_socle.py`). Explicitly NOT a merge target for `ventes.DevisSignature` (loi 53-05 acceptance proof ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a different object) or `ged.DemandeSignatureDocument` (internal GED signature circuit ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â the one path that will one day delegate its external send to this socle). See `docs/esign-socle.md`. **NTEXT17** adds `VuePersonnalisee.est_defaut` + `role_tier` (a default view can also target a role tier, not just the owner) and `core/vues.py::resoudre_vue_defaut` + `GET core/vues/?cible=<x>&defaut=1` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â resolves the one default list view for a caller. **NTEXT37** adds `core/formule_api.py` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `POST core/formule/tester/` evaluates a `core.formula` expression against a sample context (dry-run test bench for admin-authored formulas) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â no persistence.
- **Semantic search index (NTAI24).** `core/ai/search.py` + `core/models.py` **SearchChunk** (`TenantModel`, pgvector, migration core/0044) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â a single cross-module index of fiches (lead/client/devis/installation/ticket/contrat/kb-article) for `ai_governance`'s `recherche-globale/` (NTAI25) to answer "sur les documents" WITH CITATIONS, where the existing NLÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢SQL agent answers on aggregates. Indexable models are declared by STRING in `SPECS_PAR_DEFAUT` (`core` imports no business app); an app can extend via `register_indexable` in its own `apps.py` `ready()`. Key-gated OFF by default: `settings.AI_SEMANTIC_INDEX_ENABLED` (writes byte-identical when off) and a pluggable embedding provider (`embedding` stays NULL, search degrades to full-text keyword matching, without one). `indexer`/`desindexer` are BEST-EFFORT (never raise) and `rechercher(company, question)` is always company-scoped.
- **Matrice d'approbation + moteur BPM enrichi (NTWFL1-14).** `MatriceApprobation` (objet x montant x département -> chaîne de paliers, `matrices-approbation/`) démarre une `WorkflowInstance` via `demarrer_depuis_matrice`/`selectors.resoudre_matrice` ; `core/workflow.py` gagne un SLA en jours ouvrés (`calendrier_ouvre`, `register_business_day_advance`), une garde de transition (`condition_transition` évaluée par `core/rules.py`, jamais un second évaluateur), une étape alternative sur échec, des groupes PARALLÈLES (`groupe_parallele`), un rappel à mi-SLA (`dernier_rappel_le`) et la délégation (`register_delegation_resolver`, branchée par `automation`) ; `FormulaireDefinition`/`FormulaireChampReutilisable` (`formulaires/`, `formulaire-champs/`) attachent un formulaire dynamique à une étape (`donnees_formulaire`). Nouveau signal `workflow_etape_activee` (NTWFL5, consommé par `notifications`).
- **DSR/rétention désormais branchés par les apps (NTGRC1/4).** `core/dsr.py` (export/effacement loi 09-08 via `register_dsr_provider`/`verifier_gardes_effacement`, `DataSubjectRequest` gagne `token_suivi`/`date_echeance`/`preuve` pour le portail public de `grc`) et `core/merge.py` (`repointer_relations`/`completer_champs_vides`, primitive de fusion réutilisée par `dataquality`) sont désormais CONSOMMÉS : `crm`/`ventes`/`stock` enregistrent chacun leur fournisseur DSR (`dsr_provider.py`) et leur politique de rétention (`retention.py`, pilotée par `grc.PolitiqueRetentionObjet`, dry-run par défaut). Nouveau signal `lead_created` (alerte DPO de `grc` sur retrait de consentement, sans import croisé).
- **Explorateur de données + socle IA (NTDATA5/6, NTAI1-4).** `data-explorer/datasets/` (+ détail) enrichit le catalogue BI (label FR + type par champ) et `data-explorer/run/` exécute une requête ad-hoc en liste blanche (plafond 5000 lignes, jamais de SQL brut) — consommés par `semantic.resolve_metric`. `core/ai/usage.py` (registre `record_usage`/budget provider, `ai_governance` s'y branche sans que `core` l'importe), `core/ai/redaction.py` (`redact_pii`/`unredact`, caviardage PII avant tout appel fournisseur) et `core/ai/services.guard_output` (garde anti-invention : un texte généré ne peut pas citer un chiffre absent du contexte fourni) complètent le socle IA existant (`SearchChunk`).

### FastAPI AI service (`backend/fastapi_ia`, root_path `/api/fastapi`)
JWT-protected, key-gated. `GET /health`; `/ocr/*` (Zhipu bill/invoice OCR ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢
structured data, `ZHIPU_API_KEY`); `/sql-agent/*` (LangChain natural-languageÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢SQL,
SELECT-only, tenant-filtered, pgvector table routing, Redis history; `GROQ_API_KEY`
or OpenAI/Anthropic via `SQL_AGENT_PROVIDER`). Group R/S additions: `/sql-agent/confirm` (run a stashed proposeÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢confirm action by signed token), registry-driven agent tools built from the Django `/api/django/agent/actions/` catalogue with proposals surfaced on `/query`, `/sql-agent/transcribe` (Groq `whisper-large-v3` assistant voice, reuses `GROQ_API_KEY`), and `/chat/transcribe` (self-hosted `faster-whisper` for chat voice memos, behind `CHAT_TRANSCRIPTION_ENABLED`, lazy model load). **WIR60** adds `/kb/*` (`POST /kb/redaction`, actions gÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©nÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©rer/reformuler/corriger/traduire/rÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©sumer) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â the KB editor's `AiWritingToolbar.jsx` was already calling it, 404ing every time (no `/kb` router existed); reuses the SAME key-gated LLM as the SQL agent (`SQLAgentService._build_llm`, no new dependency/paid service), degrading to a 503 the frontend already recognizes (`isKeyMissing`) when no provider key is configured. **WIR153** retires the 3 hardcoded agent-action tools (ticket SAV / BC brouillon / visite planifiÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©e) in favour of the registry-driven catalogue above ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â they had no caller left once AG1/AG2 landed.

## 5. Frontend, feature by feature

SPA built with React 19 + Redux Toolkit + react-router 7 + Tailwind 4. `features/`
holds Redux slices and domain logic; `pages/` holds screens; `api/` holds one axios
module per backend area. The **design system** (refonte UI) lives in `design/`
(tokens + theme), `lib/` (cn + format utils), and `ui/` (primitives) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â see below.

### Coquille par app ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â le paradigme ODY (fondateur 2026-08-01)

L'ERP s'ouvre sur SES apps, on entre DANS une app, on en sort pour en choisir une autre.
Tout passe par `frontend/src/lib/apps/` ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â **jamais un second registre d'apps** :

| Module | Role |
|---|---|
| `useInstalledApps.js` | **LA source unique** de la liste d'apps : registre `moduleConfigs` ÃƒÂ¢Ã‹â€ Ã‚Â© modules actifs de la societe (ODX6) ÃƒÂ¢Ã‹â€ Ã‚Â© role/permission (ARC47). Consommee par le Menu d'accueil, le lanceur VX9, les epingles VX10, l'ecran Applications et les surfaces transverses. |
| `ActiveAppContext.jsx` | Derive l'app ACTIVE de la route (fonction pure du `pathname`, donc deep-link/F5/retour rendent la meme coquille). Expose `useActiveApp`, `useAppVisibility`, `crossAppNavigate`. |
| `appPrefs.js` / `appSearch.js` / `appIcon.js` / `appTransition.js` | Favoris + ordre + reprise par app (ODY13/29), recherche type-ahead, resolution d'icone, transition d'entree/sortie (ODY11). |
| `useAppBadges.js` | Badges vivants de la grille ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â UN appel agrege sur l'endpoint federe EXISTANT `GET /reporting/reports/kpi-federes/` (ARC40), jamais une re-agregation locale. |

En immersion, `Sidebar`/`Header`/`BottomTabBar` ne rendent QUE l'app active (identite + ses
ecrans + la sortie Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â« ÃƒÂ¢Ã…Â Ã…Â¾ Toutes les apps Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â»). Le flag de build `VITE_APPS_SHELL` (ODY30, defaut ON,
triplet `.env.example`/`docker-compose.yml`/`Dockerfile.prod`) est un kill-switch d'urgence ;
son retrait est la tache ODY33, **gatee sur validation fondateur en production**.

### API modules (`frontend/src/api`)
`ventesApi`, `crmApi`, `stockApi`, `installationsApi`, `savApi`, `reportingApi`,
`iaApi` (ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ FastAPI), `parametresApi`, `rolesApi`, `customFieldsApi`,
`documentsApi`, `recordsApi`, `messagesApi` (ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ `/api/django/chat/`) ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â one per backend area listed in Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â§4.

### Design system ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â refonte UI (`frontend/src/design`, `lib`, `ui`)
"Prettier-than-Odoo" overhaul (PLAN2 groups F+G). **Additive ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â existing screens
unchanged** until migrated screen-by-screen (groups J/P); custom token names, no
Tailwind default or global body font overridden, no `dark:` used elsewhere.
- **`design/`** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `tokens.css` (Tailwind 4 `@theme`: brand brass/nuit/azur/lune ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢
  semantic light+dark tokens + density), brand fonts (Archivo/Hanken via
  `public/fonts/brand.css`), `theme.js` + `ThemeProvider`/`ThemeToggle`
  (clair/sombre/systÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¨me, dÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©faut systÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â¨me).
- **`lib/`** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `cn.js` (clsx+tailwind-merge), `format.js` (MAD / fr-FR / dates /
  tÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©l. MA ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â one source of truth), **`offlineOutbox.js`/`offlineStore.js`** (NTMOB1 ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â
  the single cross-module offline outbox engine, POSTs the queued batch to
  `offlinesync/operations/batch/`; `features/installations/offline/outbox.js` is now
  only a re-export so the existing field-capture imports keep working, VX105 one-outbox
  decision) surfaced by `components/offline/OfflinePendingBadge.jsx` +
  `useOfflinePending` hook.
- **`ui/`** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â shadcn/Radix primitives: Button/IconButton/Spinner, Input/Textarea/
  Label/NumberÃƒâ€šÃ‚Â·CurrencyÃƒâ€šÃ‚Â·PercentÃƒâ€šÃ‚Â·Phone, Checkbox/Radio/Switch/Segmented/Slider,
  Select/Combobox/MultiSelect, DatePicker/DateRangePicker/TimePicker (calcul de
  dates maison, sans librairie), FileUpload/dropzone, Form system (Form/FormSection/
  FormField/FormActions + useDirtyGuard),
  Dialog/Sheet/AlertDialog/Popover/Tooltip/DropdownMenu/HoverCard/ContextMenu,
  Toaster(sonner)/Badge/StatusPill/Tag/Avatar/Card/Stat/Tabs/Accordion/Progress/
  Separator/DefinitionList/KeyValueTable (VX152 ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â table partagÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â©e Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â« libellÃƒÆ’Ã‚Â© ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ valeur Ãƒâ€šÃ‚Â»),
  Skeleton/EmptyState/ErrorBoundary/NotFound/Offline.
  **`ui/datatable/`** ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â reusable `<DataTable>` engine (TanStack Table): sort/filter/
  column-management/pagination/inline-edit/bulk-bar/saved-views/URL-persistence/
  virtualization/CSV+XLSX-export/mobile-cards. Demoed at `/ui`
  (`pages/ui/UIShowcase.jsx`, `pages/ui/DataTableDemo.jsx`). Deps (all already
  present): @radix-ui/*, @tanstack/react-table, lucide-react, sonner,
  cva/clsx/tailwind-merge. **ARC49/53** wire the FIRST two money-path list screens
  onto it ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â‚¬Â `renderRow`/`renderHeaderRow`/`tableClassName`/`tableRole`/
  `hideToolbar`/`hideMobileCards`/`hidePagination`/`expandedPanels` are ADDITIVE
  opt-in escapes (undocumented/unused ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â¡Ã¢â‚¬â„¢ byte-identical rendering for the ~79 other
  consumer screens) added specifically so a custom `<tr>` (loading-state buttons,
  AlertDialog, independently-toggled expandable panels) can sit inside the engine's
  chrome (pagination/sort/column-mgmt/saved-views) without a rewrite: **DevisList**
  (`DevisRow`/`DevisPdfDialog` split out, parity proven in
